	game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
		Title = "Lunacy Hub",
		Text = "Script already loaded!",
		Duration = 3
	})
	return
end

do
    -- Add usernames as keys, just like the local whitelist.
    local blacklist = {
        -- ["Username"] = true,
    }

    local bootstrapPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local normalizedUsername = string.lower(bootstrapPlayer.Name)
    local blacklisted = false

    for blockedUsername, blocked in pairs(blacklist) do
        if blocked
        and string.lower(tostring(blockedUsername)) == normalizedUsername then
            blacklisted = true
            break
        end
    end

    if blacklisted then
    local blacklistGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    blacklistGui.Name = "LunacyHub"
    blacklistGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    blacklistGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    blacklistGui.Parent = bootstrapPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local initScreen = Instance.new("Frame")
    initScreen.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    initScreen.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    initScreen.BorderSizePixel = 0
    initScreen.Parent = blacklistGui

    local initContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
    initContainer.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 200)
    initContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -100)
    initContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
    initContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
    initContainer.Parent = initScreen
    Instance.new("UICorner", initContainer).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local initTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    initTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60)
    initTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    initTitle.Text = "lunacy hub"
    initTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    initTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    initTitle.TextSize = 28
    initTitle.Parent = initContainer

    local initStatus = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    initStatus.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 30)
    initStatus.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 60)
    initStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    initStatus.Text = "Uh oh! You've been blacklisted!"
    initStatus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 70, 70)
    initStatus.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    initStatus.TextSize = 14
    initStatus.Parent = initContainer

    local loadingBarBG = Instance.new("Frame")
    loadingBarBG.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 6)
    loadingBarBG.Position = UDim2.new(0.1, 0, 0, 110)
    loadingBarBG.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
    loadingBarBG.BorderSizePixel = 0
    loadingBarBG.Parent = initContainer
    Instance.new("UICorner", loadingBarBG).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local loadingBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    loadingBar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    loadingBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    loadingBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    loadingBar.Parent = loadingBarBG
    Instance.new("UICorner", loadingBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local initPercent = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    initPercent.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20)
    initPercent.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 130)
    initPercent.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    initPercent.Text = "0%"
    initPercent.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
    initPercent.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    initPercent.TextSize = 12
    initPercent.Parent = initContainer

        return
    end
end

local VoidProtection = {

    OriginalDestroyHeight =
        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight,

    AntiBangOverride = false,

}

local function updateFallenPartsDestroyHeight()

    if settings.AntiVoid
    or VoidProtection.AntiBangOverride then

        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight =
            -math.huge

    else

        workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight =
            VoidProtection.OriginalDestroyHeight

    end

end

local character
local root
local humanoid

local rebuildAntiFlingConstraints

local autoEat
local connectAutoEat
local autoEatConnection

local function bindCharacter(char)

    character = char
    root = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

    connectAutoEat()

    if rebuildAntiFlingConstraints then

        task.defer(function()

            rebuildAntiFlingConstraints()

        end)

    end

end

local function getEquippedTool()

	for _,tool in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do

		if tool:IsA("Tool") then
			return tool
		end

	end

	return nil

end

-- VISUALS FOLDER

local radiusFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
radiusFolder.Name = "LunacyRadiusVisuals"
radiusFolder.Parent = workspace

local function createRadiusPart(name, color)

    local part = Instance.new("Part")

    part.Name = name
    part.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.CastShadow = false

    part.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
    part.Transparency = 1

    part.Color = color

    part.Parent = radiusFolder

    return part

end

local radiusVisuals = {

    Avalanche = {
        Part = createRadiusPart(
            "AvalancheRadius",
            Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        ),
        Toggle = "ShowAvalancheRadius",
        Radius = "AvalancheRadius",
    },

    Tsunami = {
        Part = createRadiusPart(
            "TsunamiRadius",
            Color3.fromRGB(0,170,255)
        ),
        Toggle = "ShowTsunamiRadius",
        Radius = "TsunamiRadius",
    },

    Volcano = {
        Part = createRadiusPart(
            "VolcanoRadius",
            Color3.fromRGB(255,60,60)
        ),
        Toggle = "ShowVolcanoRadius",
        Radius = "VolcanoRadius",
    },

    Meteor = {
        Part = createRadiusPart(
            "MeteorRadius",
            Color3.fromRGB(120,80,45)
        ),
        Toggle = "ShowMeteorRadius",
        Radius = "MeteorRadius",
    },

    FlingAura = {
        Part = createRadiusPart(
            "FlingAuraRadius",
            Color3.fromRGB(190, 70, 255)
        ),
        Toggle = "ShowFlingAuraRadius",
        Radius = "FlingAuraRadius",
    },

}

local function updateRadiusVisuals()

    if not root then
        return
    end

    for _, data in pairs(radiusVisuals) do

        local part = data.Part

        if settings[data.Toggle] then

            local radius = settings[data.Radius]

            part.Transparency = 0.35  -- transparency of parts (lower = more solid)

            local diameter = radius * 2

            part.Size = Vector3.new(
                diameter,
                diameter,
                diameter
            )

            part.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position)

        else

            part.Transparency = 1

        end

    end

end
--------------------------------------------------
-- Anti Fling
--------------------------------------------------
local AntiFling = {

    Constraints = {},

    PlayerConnections = {},

    CharacterConnections = {},

    ForeignCollisionStates = {},
    LastSweep = 0,
    LastConstraintRepair = 0,
    LastSafeCFrame = nil,

}

local function setAntiFlingConstraintsEnabled(enabled)

    for _, lookup in pairs(
        AntiFling.Constraints
    ) do

        for _, constraint in pairs(
            lookup
        ) do

            if constraint
            and constraint.Parent then

                constraint.Enabled =
                    enabled

            end

        end

    end

end

local function destroyAntiFlingConstraints()

    for _, lookup in pairs(
        AntiFling.Constraints
    ) do

        for _, constraint in pairs(
            lookup
        ) do

            if constraint then
                constraint:Destroy()
            end

        end

    end

    table.clear(
        AntiFling.Constraints
    )

end

local function createAntiFlingConstraint(
    ownPart,
    otherPart
)

    if not ownPart
    or not ownPart.Parent
    or not ownPart:IsA("BasePart") then

        return

    end

    if not otherPart
    or not otherPart.Parent
    or not otherPart:IsA("BasePart") then

        return

    end

    AntiFling.Constraints[ownPart] =
        AntiFling.Constraints[ownPart] or {}

    if AntiFling.Constraints[ownPart][otherPart] then
        return
    end

    local constraint =
        Instance.new("NoCollisionConstraint")

    constraint.Part0 = ownPart
    constraint.Part1 = otherPart

    constraint.Enabled =
        settings.AntiFling

    constraint.Parent =
        ownPart

    AntiFling.Constraints[ownPart][otherPart] =
        constraint

end

local function applyAntiFlingToCharacter(
    otherCharacter
)

    if not settings.AntiFling then
        return
    end

    if not character
    or not character.Parent then

        return

    end

    if not otherCharacter
    or not otherCharacter.Parent then

        return

    end

    AntiFling.SuppressCharacterCollisions(otherCharacter)

    for _, ownPart in ipairs(
        character:GetDescendants()
    ) do

        if ownPart:IsA("BasePart")
        and ownPart.CanCollide then

            for _, otherPart in ipairs(
                otherCharacter:GetDescendants()
            ) do

                if otherPart:IsA("BasePart")
                and (
                    otherPart.CanCollide
                    or otherPart.Name == "HumanoidRootPart"
                    or otherPart.Name == "Head"
                    or string.find(otherPart.Name, "Torso", 1, true)
                ) then

                    createAntiFlingConstraint(
                        ownPart,
                        otherPart
                    )

                end

            end

        end

    end

end

function AntiFling.UpdateProtection()
    if not settings.AntiFling then return end

    local now = time()
    if now - AntiFling.LastSweep >= 0.1 then
        AntiFling.LastSweep = now
        for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if otherPlayer ~= player and otherPlayer.Character then
                AntiFling.SuppressCharacterCollisions(otherPlayer.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    if now - AntiFling.LastConstraintRepair >= 0.75 then
        AntiFling.LastConstraintRepair = now
        for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if otherPlayer ~= player and otherPlayer.Character then
                applyAntiFlingToCharacter(otherPlayer.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    if not root or not root.Parent then return end
    local ownSpeed = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
    if ownSpeed < 55 then AntiFling.LastSafeCFrame = root.CFrame end
    if ownSpeed < 120 and root.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude < 45 then return end

    for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local otherCharacter = otherPlayer ~= player and otherPlayer.Character
        local otherRoot = otherCharacter and otherCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if otherRoot
        and (otherRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude <= 14
        and (otherRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude >= 90
            or otherRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity.Magnitude >= 35) then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if AntiFling.LastSafeCFrame then
                root.CFrame = AntiFling.LastSafeCFrame
            end
            break
        end
    end
end

rebuildAntiFlingConstraints = function()

    destroyAntiFlingConstraints()

    if not character
    or not character.Parent then
        return
    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(
        Services.Players:GetPlayers()
    ) do

        if plr ~= player
        and plr.Character then

            applyAntiFlingToCharacter(
                plr.Character
            )

        end

    end

end

local function hookAntiFlingPlayer(plr)

    if plr == player then
        return
    end

    if AntiFling.PlayerConnections[plr] then
        return
    end

    AntiFling.PlayerConnections[plr] =
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(
            function(otherCharacter)

                task.defer(function()

                    if settings.AntiFling
                    and otherCharacter
                    and otherCharacter.Parent then

                        applyAntiFlingToCharacter(
                            otherCharacter
                        )

                    end

                end)

            end
        )

    if settings.AntiFling
    and plr.Character then

        applyAntiFlingToCharacter(
            plr.Character
        )

    end

end

for _, plr in ipairs(
    Services.Players:GetPlayers()
) do

    hookAntiFlingPlayer(plr)

end

Services.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(
    hookAntiFlingPlayer
)

Services.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(
    function(plr)

        local connection =
            AntiFling.PlayerConnections[plr]

        if connection then

            connection:Disconnect()

            AntiFling.PlayerConnections[plr] =
                nil

        end

    end
)
--------------------------------------------------
-- Anti Fling 2
--------------------------------------------------
local allowedCollisionParts = {}

local modifiedCollisionParts = {}

local function RegisterAllowedCollision(instance)

    if not instance then
        return
    end

    if instance:IsA("BasePart") then

        allowedCollisionParts[instance] = true
        return

    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then

            allowedCollisionParts[obj] = true

        end

    end

end

local function UnregisterAllowedCollision(instance)

    if not instance then
        return
    end

    if instance:IsA("BasePart") then

        allowedCollisionParts[instance] = nil
        return

    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then

            allowedCollisionParts[obj] = nil

        end

    end

end

RegisterAllowedCollision(workspace:FindFirstChild("Island"))
RegisterAllowedCollision(workspace:FindFirstChild("Tower"))

local function ProcessCollisionPart(part)

    if not settings.AntiFling2 then
        return
    end

    local parent = part.Parent

    if parent and parent.Name == "LunacyEarthquakePlatform" then
        return
    end

	-- Volcano
	if part.Name == "Lava"
	and parent
	and parent.Name == "Structure" then
		allowedCollisionParts[part] = true
		return
	end

    if allowedCollisionParts[part] then
        return
    end

    if modifiedCollisionParts[part] == nil then
        modifiedCollisionParts[part] = part.CanCollide
    end

    part.CanCollide = false

end

workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)

    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then

        -- Avalanche
        if obj.Name == "AvalanchePart" or obj.Name == "MtnPart" then
            allowedCollisionParts[obj] = true
            return
        end

        -- Tsunami
        local tsunami = obj:FindFirstAncestor("TsunamiWave")
        if tsunami and (
            obj.Name == "Center"
            or obj.Name == "Part"
            or obj.Name == "Wedge"
        ) then
            allowedCollisionParts[obj] = true
            return
        end

        ProcessCollisionPart(obj)

    end

end)

local function RefreshCollisionParts()

    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then

            ProcessCollisionPart(obj)

        end

    end

end

local function RestoreCollisionParts()

    for part,original in pairs(modifiedCollisionParts) do

        if part and part.Parent then

            part.CanCollide = original

        end

    end

    table.clear(modifiedCollisionParts)

end

--------------------------------------------------
-- Water Walker
--------------------------------------------------
local WATER_PLATFORM_OFFSET = -0.85
local WATER_COLLISION_DISABLE_BELOW = 2.5
local WATER_COLLISION_ENABLE_ABOVE = 1

local waterCollisionState = {
    Normal = false,
    Flood = false,
}

local waterPlatform = Instance.new("Part")

waterPlatform.Name = "LunacyWaterWalker"

waterPlatform.Anchored = true
waterPlatform.CanCollide = false
waterPlatform.CanTouch = false
waterPlatform.CanQuery = false

waterPlatform.Transparency = 1

waterPlatform.Size = Vector3.new(
    6000,
    2,
    6000
)

waterPlatform.Parent = workspace

local floodPlatform = Instance.new("Part")

floodPlatform.Name = "LunacyFloodWalker"

floodPlatform.Anchored = true
floodPlatform.CanCollide = false
floodPlatform.CanTouch = false
floodPlatform.CanQuery = false

floodPlatform.Transparency = 1

floodPlatform.Size = Vector3.new(
    6000,
    2,
    6000
)

floodPlatform.Parent = workspace

local function updateOneWayWaterCollision(
    platform,
    stateKey,
    available
)

    if not platform then
        return
    end

    if not settings.WaterWalker
    or not available
    or not root
    or not root.Parent then

        platform.CanCollide = false
        waterCollisionState[stateKey] = false

        return
    end

    local relativeY =
        root.Position.Y - platform.Position.Y

    local currentlyCollidable =
        waterCollisionState[stateKey]

    ----------------------------------
    -- If currently solid, only turn
    -- it off once we're clearly below.
    ----------------------------------

    if currentlyCollidable then

        if relativeY <
            -WATER_COLLISION_DISABLE_BELOW then

            currentlyCollidable = false

        end

    ----------------------------------
    -- If currently intangible, only
    -- turn it back on once we're
    -- clearly above the platform.
    ----------------------------------

    else

        if relativeY >
            WATER_COLLISION_ENABLE_ABOVE then

            currentlyCollidable = true

        end

    end

    waterCollisionState[stateKey] =
        currentlyCollidable

    platform.CanCollide =
        currentlyCollidable

end

--------------------------------------------------
-- Anti Earthquake
--------------------------------------------------
local Earthquake = {
    Parts = {},
    Folder = Instance.new("Folder")
}

Earthquake.Folder.Name = "LunacyEarthquakePlatform"
Earthquake.Folder.Parent = workspace

function Earthquake:Build()

    local island = workspace:FindFirstChild("Island")

    if not island then
        return
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(island:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then

            local clone = obj:Clone()

            clone.Anchored = true
            clone.Transparency = 1

            clone.CanCollide = false
            clone.CanTouch = false
            clone.CanQuery = false

            clone.CastShadow = false
            clone.Massless = true

            clone.Parent = self.Folder

            table.insert(self.Parts, {
                Real = obj,
                Clone = clone
            })

        end

    end

end

function Earthquake:SetEnabled(enabled)

    for _, pair in ipairs(self.Parts) do

        pair.Real.CanCollide = not enabled
        pair.Clone.CanCollide = enabled

    end

end

Earthquake:Build()
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- Anti Bang
----------------------------------------------------------------
local AntiBang = {
    Connections = {},
    CharacterConnection = nil,
    Busy = false,
    LastPosition = nil,
    TouchData = {},
    TouchWindow = 1,
    RequiredTouches = 5
}

local BangAnimations = {
    ["148840371"] = true, -- R6
    ["5918726674"] = true -- R15
}

local function disconnectAntiBangTouches()

    for _, connection in ipairs(AntiBang.Connections) do
        connection:Disconnect()
    end

    table.clear(AntiBang.Connections)

end

local function disconnectAntiBang()

    disconnectAntiBangTouches()

    if AntiBang.CharacterConnection then
        AntiBang.CharacterConnection:Disconnect()
        AntiBang.CharacterConnection = nil
    end

    table.clear(AntiBang.TouchData)

    AntiBang.Busy = false

end

local function performAntiBang()

    if AntiBang.Busy then
        return
    end

    AntiBang.Busy = true

    local character = player.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not root then
        AntiBang.Busy = false
        return
    end

    local originalCFrame = root.CFrame

    VoidProtection.AntiBangOverride = true
    updateFallenPartsDestroyHeight()

    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, -55000, 0)

    local ping = game:GetService("Stats")
        .Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()

    task.wait(math.clamp(ping / 1000, 0.05, 0.5))

    root.CFrame = originalCFrame

    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

    VoidProtection.AntiBangOverride = false
    updateFallenPartsDestroyHeight()

    AntiBang.Busy = false

end

local function onAntiBangTouched(
    hit,
    contactZone
)

    if not settings.AntiBang or AntiBang.Busy then
        return
    end

    if not hit then
        return
    end

    local character = player.Character
    if not character then
        return
    end

    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        return
    end

    local otherCharacter = hit:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not otherCharacter then
        return
    end

    local otherPlayer = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(otherCharacter)

    if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == player then
        return
    end

    local otherRoot = otherCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not otherRoot then
        return
    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Animation detection (highest priority)
    --------------------------------------------------

    local humanoid = otherCharacter:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

    if humanoid then

        for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do

            local animation = track.Animation

            if animation then

                local id = animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")

                if id and BangAnimations[id] then

                    performAntiBang()
                    return

                end

            end

        end

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Behavioral detection
    --------------------------------------------------

    local now = tick()

    local touchData = AntiBang.TouchData[otherPlayer.UserId]

    if not touchData then
        touchData = {
            Count = 0,
            LastTouch = now
        }
        AntiBang.TouchData[otherPlayer.UserId] = touchData
    end

    local offset = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(otherRoot.CFrame)

    local isBehind = offset.Position.Z > 0

    local facingSameDirection =
        root.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(otherRoot.CFrame.LookVector) > 0.8

    local facingTowardEachOther =
        root.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(otherRoot.CFrame.LookVector) < -0.8

    local distance =
        (root.Position - otherRoot.Position).Magnitude

    local closeEnough = distance <= 2

    local faceContact =
        contactZone == "Head"
        and offset.Position.Z < 0
        and facingTowardEachOther
        and distance <= 2.5

    if (isBehind and facingSameDirection and closeEnough)
    or faceContact then

        if now - touchData.LastTouch > AntiBang.TouchWindow then
            touchData.Count = 0
        end

        touchData.Count += 1
        touchData.LastTouch = now

    else

        touchData.Count = 0
        touchData.LastTouch = now

    end

    if touchData.Count >= AntiBang.RequiredTouches then

        performAntiBang()

        touchData.Count = 0

        return

    end

end

local function onAntiBangAnimationTouched(hit)

    if not settings.AntiBangAnimations or AntiBang.Busy then
        return
    end

    if not hit then
        return
    end

    local character = player.Character
    if not character then
        return
    end

    local otherCharacter = hit:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if not otherCharacter then
        return
    end

    local otherPlayer = Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(otherCharacter)

    if not otherPlayer or otherPlayer == player then
        return
    end

    local humanoid = otherCharacter:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")

    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do

        local animation = track.Animation

        if animation then

            local id = animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")

            if id and BangAnimations[id] then

                performAntiBang()
                return

            end

        end

    end

end

local function connectAntiBang(character)

    disconnectAntiBangTouches()

    local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local torso = character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
        or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")

    if head then

        local connection = head.Touched:Connect(function(hit)

            if settings.AntiBang then
                onAntiBangTouched(
                    hit,
                    "Head"
                )
            end

            if settings.AntiBangAnimations then
                onAntiBangAnimationTouched(hit)
            end

        end)

        table.insert(AntiBang.Connections, connection)

    end

    if torso then

        local connection = torso.Touched:Connect(function(hit)

            if settings.AntiBang then
                onAntiBangTouched(
                    hit,
                    "Torso"
                )
            end

            if settings.AntiBangAnimations then
                onAntiBangAnimationTouched(hit)
            end

        end)

        table.insert(AntiBang.Connections, connection)

    end

end

-- FLY

local flyObjects = {
    Velocity = nil,
    Gyro = nil,
    Attachment = nil,
    LinearVelocity = nil,
    AlignOrientation = nil,
}

local function SetFly(enabled)

    settings.Fly = enabled

    if not character or not root then
        return
    end

    if enabled then

        -- Clean up any existing fly objects first.
        if flyObjects.Velocity then
            flyObjects.Velocity:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Velocity = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.Gyro then
            flyObjects.Gyro:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Gyro = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.LinearVelocity then
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity:Destroy()
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.AlignOrientation then
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation:Destroy()
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.Attachment then
            flyObjects.Attachment:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Attachment = nil
        end

        if settings.FlyMethod == "CFrame" then

            flyObjects.Velocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            flyObjects.Velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            flyObjects.Velocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            flyObjects.Velocity.Parent = root

            flyObjects.Gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            flyObjects.Gyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            flyObjects.Gyro.P = 10000
            flyObjects.Gyro.CFrame = workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame
            flyObjects.Gyro.Parent = root

        elseif settings.FlyMethod == "Velocity" then

            flyObjects.Velocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            flyObjects.Velocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
            flyObjects.Velocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            flyObjects.Velocity.Parent = root

            -- No gyro.

        elseif settings.FlyMethod == "LinearVelocity" then

            flyObjects.Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
            flyObjects.Attachment.Parent = root

            flyObjects.LinearVelocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity.Attachment0 = flyObjects.Attachment
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity.MaxForce = math.huge
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity.Parent = root

            flyObjects.AlignOrientation = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation.Attachment0 = flyObjects.Attachment
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation.Parent = root

        end

    else

        if flyObjects.Velocity then
            flyObjects.Velocity:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Velocity = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.Gyro then
            flyObjects.Gyro:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Gyro = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.LinearVelocity then
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity:Destroy()
            flyObjects.LinearVelocity = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.AlignOrientation then
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation:Destroy()
            flyObjects.AlignOrientation = nil
        end

        if flyObjects.Attachment then
            flyObjects.Attachment:Destroy()
            flyObjects.Attachment = nil
        end

    end

end

    and obj.Parent
    and obj.Parent.Name == "Structure" then

        if not table.find(disasterState.VolcanoParts, obj) then

            table.insert(
                disasterState.VolcanoParts,
                obj
            )

        end

    end

end)

    if obj.Name ~= "Lava" then
        return
    end

    for i = #disasterState.VolcanoParts, 1, -1 do

        if disasterState.VolcanoParts[i] == obj then
            table.remove(disasterState.VolcanoParts, i)
            break
        end

    end

end)

local function bypassAntiTeleport(fn)
	antiTeleport.Bypass = true
	local ok, result = pcall(fn)
	if root and root.Parent then
		antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position
		antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame
	end
	task.wait()
	antiTeleport.Bypass = false
	if not ok then
		warn("Lunacy teleport failed: " .. tostring(result))
	end
	return ok, result
end

local function ApplyNoclip()

    if not character then
        return
    end

    for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then

            if noclipCollisionStates[part] == nil then
                noclipCollisionStates[part] = part.CanCollide
            end

            if settings.Noclip then
                part.CanCollide = false
            else
                part.CanCollide = noclipCollisionStates[part]
            end

        end
    end

end

local function removeBlizzardGui()

    local playerGui = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then
        return
    end

    local gui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("BlizzardGui")

    if gui then
        gui:Destroy()
    end

end

    local playerGui = player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if not playerGui then
        return
    end

    local gui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("SandStormGui")

    if gui then
        gui:Destroy()
    end

end

end

local function ClickTeleport(position)

    if not root then
        return
    end

    bypassAntiTeleport(function()

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
        )

    end)

end

autoEat = function()

    if humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end

	if not settings.AutoEat then
		return
	end

	if appleState.Eating then
		return
	end

	if appleState.Cooldown then
		return
	end

	if humanoid.Health >= HEALTH_THRESHOLD then
		return
	end

	local apple =
		character:FindFirstChild(APPLE_NAME)
		or player.Backpack:FindFirstChild(APPLE_NAME)

	if not apple then
		return
	end

	appleState.Eating = true

	local previousTool = getEquippedTool()

	if previousTool == apple then
		previousTool = nil
	end

	if apple.Parent ~= character then
		humanoid:EquipTool(apple)
		task.wait(0.2)
	end

	local previousHealth = humanoid.Health

	apple:Activate()

	local start = tick()

    while humanoid.Parent
    	and humanoid.Health <= previousHealth
    	and tick() - start < 5 do

    	task.wait()

    end

	humanoid:UnequipTools()

	task.wait(0.1)

	if previousTool
		and previousTool.Parent == player.Backpack then

		humanoid:EquipTool(previousTool)

	end

	appleState.Eating = false
    appleState.Cooldown = true

    task.spawn(function()

	    task.wait(APPLE_COOLDOWN)

	    while settings.AutoEat
	    	and humanoid.Health < HEALTH_THRESHOLD do

	    	appleState.Cooldown = false

	    	local oldHealth = humanoid.Health

	    	autoEat()

	    	if humanoid.Health > oldHealth then
	    		return
	    	end

	    	appleState.Cooldown = true
	    	task.wait(COOLDOWN_STEP)

	    end

	    appleState.Cooldown = false

    end)

end

local function startAutoEat()

    if settings.AutoEat
        and humanoid
        and humanoid.Health > 0
        and humanoid.Health < HEALTH_THRESHOLD then

        task.spawn(autoEat)

    end

end

connectAutoEat = function()

    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    if autoEatConnection then
        autoEatConnection:Disconnect()
        autoEatConnection = nil
    end

    autoEatConnection = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)

        if health < HEALTH_THRESHOLD then
            task.spawn(autoEat)
        end

    end)

end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Particle spawner
    ----------------------------------

    local function spawnOwnerParticle()

        if not billboard
        or not billboard.Parent then
            return
        end

        local particle =
            Instance.new("Frame")

        local size =
            math.random(
                2,
                5
            )

        particle.Size =
            UDim2.fromOffset(
                size,
                size
            )

        particle.AnchorPoint =
            Vector2.new(
                0.5,
                0.5
            )

        particle.Position =
            UDim2.new(
                math.random(15, 85) / 100,
                0,

                math.random(30, 80) / 100,
                0
            )

        particle.BackgroundColor3 =
            Color3.fromHSV(
                currentOwnerHue,
                1,
                1
            )

        particle.BackgroundTransparency =
            0.12

        particle.BorderSizePixel =
            0

        particle.ZIndex =
            0

        particle.Parent =
            billboard

        local corner =
            Instance.new("UICorner")

        corner.CornerRadius =
            UDim.new(
                1,
                0
            )

        corner.Parent =
            particle

        local destination =
            UDim2.new(
                particle.Position.X.Scale
                + math.random(-10, 10) / 100,
                0,

                particle.Position.Y.Scale
                - math.random(20, 45) / 100,
                0
            )

        Services.TweenService:Create(
            particle,

            TweenInfo.new(
                math.random(7, 14) / 10,
                Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
                Enum.EasingDirection.Out
            ),

            {
                Position =
                    destination,

                BackgroundTransparency =
                    1,

                Rotation =
                    math.random(
                        -90,
                        90
                    )
            }

        ):Play()

        task.spawn(function()

            while particle
            and particle.Parent
            and billboard
            and billboard.Parent do

                particle.BackgroundColor3 =
                    Color3.fromHSV(
                        currentOwnerHue,
                        1,
                        1
                    )

                task.wait(0.03)

            end

        end)

        task.delay(
            1.5,
            function()

                if particle then
                    particle:Destroy()
                end

            end
        )

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Animation ID
    ----------------------------------

    ownerTagAnimationId += 1

    local animationId =
        ownerTagAnimationId

    ----------------------------------
    -- Animation
    ----------------------------------

    task.spawn(function()

        local timeValue = 0
        local particleTimer = 0

        while animationId
            == ownerTagAnimationId
        and billboard
        and billboard.Parent
        and plr.Parent
            == Services.Players do

            timeValue += 0.03
            particleTimer += 0.03

            ----------------------------------
            -- Animation values
            ----------------------------------

            local float =
                math.sin(
                    timeValue * 1.8
                )

            local wobble =
                math.sin(
                    timeValue * 2.4
                )

            local breathe =
                math.sin(
                    timeValue * 1.5
                )

            local hue =
                (timeValue * 0.45) % 1

            ----------------------------------
            -- Continuous Rainbow Wave
            ----------------------------------

            local rainbowSpeed = 0.6

            local hue =
                (timeValue * rainbowSpeed) % 1

            currentOwnerHue =
                hue

            local gradientColor =
                ColorSequence.new({

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            hue,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0.16,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            (hue + 0.16) % 1,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0.33,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            (hue + 0.33) % 1,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0.50,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            (hue + 0.50) % 1,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0.66,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            (hue + 0.66) % 1,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        0.83,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            (hue + 0.83) % 1,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(
                        1,
                        Color3.fromHSV(
                            hue,
                            1,
                            1
                        )
                    ),

                })

            gradient.Color =
                gradientColor

            glowGradient.Color =
                gradientColor

            ----------------------------------
            -- Keep the spatial gradient fixed.
            -- The colors themselves flow.
            ----------------------------------

            gradient.Offset =
                Vector2.zero

            glowGradient.Offset =
                Vector2.zero

            ----------------------------------
            -- Flicker / flash
            ----------------------------------

            local flicker =
                math.sin(
                    timeValue * 18
                )
                * math.sin(
                    timeValue * 7.3
                )

            local flash =
                math.max(
                    0,
                    flicker
                )

            label.TextTransparency =
                0.02
                + flash * 0.18

            glow.TextTransparency =
                math.clamp(
                    0.62
                    - flash * 0.30,
                    0.20,
                    0.70
                )

            ----------------------------------
            -- Occasional hard flash
            ----------------------------------

            local hardFlash =
                math.sin(
                    timeValue * 31
                )
                * math.sin(
                    timeValue * 13
                )

            if hardFlash > 0.92 then

                label.TextTransparency =
                    0

                glow.TextTransparency =
                    0.15

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Keep outline off
            ----------------------------------

            label.TextStrokeTransparency =
                1

            glow.TextStrokeTransparency =
                1

            sweep.TextStrokeTransparency =
                1

            ----------------------------------
            -- Vertical float
            ----------------------------------

            billboard.StudsOffset =
                Vector3.new(
                    0,

                    3.15
                    + float * 0.30,

                    0
                )

            ----------------------------------
            -- Breathing
            ----------------------------------

            local scale =
                1
                + breathe * 0.06

            label.Size =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    scale,
                    scale
                )

            label.Position =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    (1 - scale) * 0.5,
                    (1 - scale) * 0.5
                )

            ----------------------------------
            -- Glow follows breathing
            ----------------------------------

            local glowScale =
                scale * 1.08

            glow.Size =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    glowScale,
                    glowScale
                )

            glow.Position =
                UDim2.fromScale(
                    (1 - glowScale) * 0.5,
                    (1 - glowScale) * 0.5
                )

            ----------------------------------
            -- Strong wobble
            ----------------------------------

            local rotation =
                wobble * 10

            label.Rotation =
                rotation

            glow.Rotation =
                rotation

            ----------------------------------
            -- Sweep follows main text
            ----------------------------------

            sweep.Size =
                label.Size

            sweep.Position =
                label.Position

            sweep.Rotation =
                label.Rotation

            ----------------------------------
            -- Periodic light sweep
            ----------------------------------

            local sweepCycle =
                timeValue % 4

            if sweepCycle < 4 then

                local progress =
                    sweepCycle / 4

                sweepGradient.Offset =
                    Vector2.new(
                        -1.25
                        + progress * 2.5,
                        0
                    )

            else

                sweepGradient.Offset =
                    Vector2.new(
                        -1.5,
                        0
                    )

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Particles
            ----------------------------------

            if particleTimer >= 0.14 then

                particleTimer = 0

                spawnOwnerParticle()

            end

            task.wait(0.03)

        end

    end)

end

local function applyNametag(plr)

    local emoji =
        nametagList[plr.Name]

    if not emoji then
        return
    end

    local char =
        plr.Character

    if not char then
        return
    end

    local humanoid =
        char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)

    if not humanoid then
        return
    end

    local desiredName =
        plr.DisplayName
        .. " ["
        .. emoji
        .. "]"

    ----------------------------------
    -- Roblox default nametag fallback
    ----------------------------------

    humanoid.DisplayName =
        desiredName

    ----------------------------------
    -- Try cached custom nametag first
    ----------------------------------

    local customLabel =
        nametagLabels[plr]

    if customLabel
    and not customLabel.Parent then

        nametagLabels[plr] = nil
        customLabel = nil

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Search only if cache is missing
    ----------------------------------

    if not customLabel then

        for _, obj in ipairs(
            char:GetDescendants()
        ) do

            if obj:IsA("TextLabel") then

                local text =
                    obj.Text

                if text == plr.DisplayName
                or text == plr.Name
                or text:find(
                    plr.DisplayName,
                    1,
                    true
                ) then

                    customLabel = obj

                    nametagLabels[plr] =
                        obj

                    break

                end

            end

        end

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Apply custom game nametag
    ----------------------------------

    if customLabel then

        customLabel.Text =
            desiredName

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Replace old DisplayName watcher
    ----------------------------------

    local oldConnection =
        nametagConnections[plr]

    if oldConnection then

        oldConnection:Disconnect()

        nametagConnections[plr] =
            nil

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Keep default DisplayName tagged
    ----------------------------------

    nametagConnections[plr] =
        humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal(
            "DisplayName"
        ):Connect(function()

            if humanoid.DisplayName
            ~= desiredName then

                humanoid.DisplayName =
                    desiredName

            end

        end)

end

--================================================
-- ADMIN SYSTEM
--================================================
local rankPower = {
	["Owner"]     = 100,
	["Co-Owner"]  = 90,
	["Premium"]     = 50,
	["User"]      = 0,
}

local whitelist = {
    ["rainingallaround"] = "Owner",
	["Comaro"]           = "Premium",
	["unlikelyconvict"]  = "Premium",
	["onsznn"]           = "Premium",
    ["Fendi_PradaXx"]    = "Co-Owner",
}

local function getPlayerRole(plr)
	return whitelist[plr.Name] or "User"
end

local function hasPremium(plr)
	local role = getPlayerRole(plr)

	return rankPower[role] >= rankPower["Premium"]
end

local function getRank(name)
	return whitelist[name] or "User"
end

local function getPower(name)
	return rankPower[getRank(name)] or 0
end

local function canTarget(senderName, targetName)
	return getPower(senderName) > getPower(targetName)
end

local function findPlayer(partialName)
	partialName = partialName:lower()
	for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if plr.Name:lower():find(partialName, 1, true) then
			return plr
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local currentCommandInfo

local function findTargets(senderName, targetName)
	local targets = {}

    local function canSelectTarget(targetName)
        if currentCommandInfo and currentCommandInfo.IgnoreTargetRank then
            return getPower(senderName) > 0
        end

        return canTarget(senderName, targetName)
    end

	if targetName == "." then
		for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if canSelectTarget(plr.Name) then
				table.insert(targets, plr)
			end
		end
	else
		local plr = findPlayer(targetName)
		if plr and canSelectTarget(plr.Name) then
			table.insert(targets, plr)
		end
	end

	return targets
end

--================================================
-- COMMAND HANDLER
--================================================
local PREFIX = "L!"
local commands = {}

local BotMode = {
    Enabled = false,
    Prefix = ">",
    Whitelist = {},

    OwnerCommands = {},
    Commands = {},

    ChatChannel = nil,

    Movement = nil,
    Target = nil,

    MovementData = {},

    MovementConnection = nil,

    LastMoveTo = Vector3.zero,
}

local commandInfo = {
    stand1 = {
        IgnoreTargetRank = true,
    },

    stand2 = {
        IgnoreTargetRank = true,
    },

    stand3 = {
        IgnoreTargetRank = true,
    },

    unstand = {
        IgnoreTargetRank = true,
    },
}

currentCommandInfo = nil

local function stopStandMovement()
	settings.Stand1 = false
	settings.Stand2 = false
	settings.Stand3 = false

	if commands._StandConnection then
		commands._StandConnection:Disconnect()
		commands._StandConnection = nil
	end
end

local function exitStandMode()
	stopStandMovement()

	if settings.StandIdleTrack then
		settings.StandIdleTrack:Stop()
		settings.StandIdleTrack:Destroy()
		settings.StandIdleTrack = nil
	end

	local animate = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	if animate then
		animate.Disabled = false
	end
end

local function playStandIdle()
	local character = player.Character
	if not character or not humanoid then
		return
	end

	local animate = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	if not animate then
		return
	end

	-- Already playing? Don't recreate it.
	if settings.StandIdleTrack and settings.StandIdleTrack.IsPlaying then
		animate.Disabled = true
		return
	end

	local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then
		return
	end

	local idleFolder = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")
	if not idleFolder then
		return
	end

	local animation =
		idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation1")
		or idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation2")
		or idleFolder:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Animation")

	if not animation then
		return
	end

	animate.Disabled = true

	local track = animator:LoadAnimation(animation)
	track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
	track.Looped = true
	track:Play()

	settings.StandIdleTrack = track
end

local function stopMovement()

    local previousMovement = BotMode.Movement

    if previousMovement == "carpet"
    and root
    and root.Parent then

        local cleanupTargetRoot =
            BotMode.Target
            and BotMode.Target.Character
            and BotMode.Target.Character:FindFirstChild(
                "HumanoidRootPart"
            )

        local cleanupLook = cleanupTargetRoot
            and cleanupTargetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
            or root.CFrame.LookVector

        local flatLook = Vector3.new(
            cleanupLook.X,
            0,
            cleanupLook.Z
        )

        if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.001 then
            flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        else
            flatLook = flatLook.Unit
        end

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
                root.Position,
                root.Position + flatLook
            )

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        end)

    end

    BotMode.Movement = nil
    BotMode.Target = nil
    BotMode.MovementData = {}

    if humanoid then
        humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)

        if previousMovement == "carpet" then
            humanoid.Sit = false
        end
    end

end

local function findBotCommandPlayer(query)

    if typeof(query) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Trim + lowercase
    -------------------------------------------------

    query =
        string.lower(
            string.gsub(
                query,
                "^%s*(.-)%s*$",
                "%1"
            )
        )

    if query == "" then
        return nil
    end


    local players =
        Services.Players:GetPlayers()


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 1. EXACT USERNAME
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        if target ~= player
        and string.lower(target.Name) == query then

            return target

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 2. EXACT DISPLAY NAME
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        if target ~= player
        and string.lower(target.DisplayName) == query then

            return target

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 3. USERNAME STARTS WITH QUERY
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        local username =
            string.lower(
                target.Name
            )

        if target ~= player
        and string.sub(
            username,
            1,
            #query
        ) == query then

            return target

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 4. DISPLAY NAME STARTS WITH QUERY
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        local displayName =
            string.lower(
                target.DisplayName
            )

        if target ~= player
        and string.sub(
            displayName,
            1,
            #query
        ) == query then

            return target

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 5. PARTIAL USERNAME ANYWHERE
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        if target ~= player
        and string.find(
            string.lower(target.Name),
            query,
            1,
            true
        ) then

            return target

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- 6. PARTIAL DISPLAY NAME ANYWHERE
    -------------------------------------------------

    for _, target in ipairs(players) do

        if target ~= player
        and string.find(
            string.lower(target.DisplayName),
            query,
            1,
            true
        ) then

            return target

        end

    end


    return nil

end

function BotMode.Say(message)
    if not BotMode.ChatChannel then
        local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
        local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
        BotMode.ChatChannel = channels and channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
    end

    if BotMode.ChatChannel then
        pcall(function()
            BotMode.ChatChannel:SendAsync("[Lunacy Bot] " .. message)
        end)
    end
end

BotMode.OwnerCommands["whitelist"] = function(sender, args)
    local targetName = args[1]
    if not targetName then
        return
    end

    local target = findPlayer(targetName)
    if not target then
        BotMode.Say("Player not found.")
        return
    end

    if target == player then
        return
    end

    if BotMode.Whitelist[target] then
        BotMode.Say(target.Name .. " is already whitelisted.")
        return
    end

    BotMode.Whitelist[target] = true
    BotMode.Say(target.Name .. " has been whitelisted. Type >cmds for a list of commands.")
end

BotMode.OwnerCommands["unwhitelist"] = function(sender, args)
    local targetName = args[1]
    if not targetName then
        return
    end

    local target = findPlayer(targetName)
    if not target then
        BotMode.Say("Player not found.")
        return
    end

    if not BotMode.Whitelist[target] then
        BotMode.Say(target.Name .. " is not whitelisted.")
        return
    end

    BotMode.Whitelist[target] = nil
    BotMode.Say(target.Name .. " has been removed from the whitelist.")
end

BotMode.OwnerCommands["list"] = function(sender, args)
    local names = {}

    for plr in pairs(BotMode.Whitelist) do
        if plr.Parent == Services.Players then
            table.insert(names, plr.Name)
        end
    end

    if #names == 0 then
        BotMode.Say("Whitelist is empty.")
        return
    end

    table.sort(names)

    BotMode.Say("Whitelisted: " .. table.concat(names, ", "))
end

BotMode.OwnerCommands["clear"] = function(sender, args)
    table.clear(BotMode.Whitelist)
    BotMode.Say("Whitelist cleared.")
end

BotMode.Commands["cmds"] = function(sender, args)
    local names = {}

    for name in pairs(BotMode.Commands) do
        if name ~= "cmds" then
            table.insert(names, name)
        end
    end

    table.sort(names)

    BotMode.Say("Commands: " .. table.concat(names, ", "))
end

BotMode.Commands["rejoin"] = function()
    local teleportService = Services.TeleportService
        or game:GetService("TeleportService")

    local ok = pcall(function()
        teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
            game.PlaceId,
            game.JobId,
            player
        )
    end)

    if not ok then
        pcall(function()
            teleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, player)
        end)
    end
end

BotMode.Commands["bring"] = function(sender)

    stopMovement()

    local senderRoot = sender.Character and sender.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if senderRoot and root then
        bypassAntiTeleport(function()
            root.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame + senderRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 3
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame + senderRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 3
        end)
    end
end

BotMode.Commands["follow"] = function(sender)

    BotMode.Movement = "follow"
    BotMode.Target = sender
    BotMode.LastMoveTo = Vector3.zero

end

function BotMode.CaptureStandForFlingSpin()

    local movement = BotMode.Movement

    if movement == "stand1"
    or movement == "stand2"
    or movement == "stand3" then

        return {
            Movement = movement,
            Target = BotMode.Target,
        }

    end

    return nil

end

function BotMode.ResumeStandAfterFlingSpin()

    local resume = BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin
    BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin = nil

    if not resume
    or not resume.Target
    or resume.Target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
        return
    end

    stopMovement()
    stopStandMovement()

    settings.Stand1 = resume.Movement == "stand1"
    settings.Stand2 = resume.Movement == "stand2"
    settings.Stand3 = resume.Movement == "stand3"

    playStandIdle()

    BotMode.Movement = resume.Movement
    BotMode.Target = resume.Target
    BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand = false

end

function BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits()

    local resume = BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin

    if BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand
    or not resume
    or not resume.Target
    or resume.Target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
        return
    end

    stopMovement()
    stopStandMovement()

    settings.Stand1 = resume.Movement == "stand1"
    settings.Stand2 = resume.Movement == "stand2"
    settings.Stand3 = resume.Movement == "stand3"

    playStandIdle()

    BotMode.Movement = resume.Movement
    BotMode.Target = resume.Target
    BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand = true

end

function BotMode.SuspendTemporaryStandForFlingSpin()

    if not BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand then
        return
    end

    stopMovement()
    exitStandMode()
    BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand = false

end

BotMode.Commands["fling"] = function(sender, args)

    if not args
    or #args == 0 then
        return
    end


    local query =
        table.concat(
            args,
            " "
        )


    local target =
        findBotCommandPlayer(
            query
        )

    if not target then
        return
    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- STOP NORMAL BOT MOVEMENT
    -------------------------------------------------

    local standToResume =
        BotMode.CaptureStandForFlingSpin()

    stopMovement()
    exitStandMode()


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- FINISH ANY PREVIOUS FLING SPIN SESSION
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.FlingSpinTarget
    and BotMode.StopFlingSpin then

        BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin = nil

        BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
            true
        )

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- NORMAL FLING SPIN
    --
    -- false = do NOT survive target respawn.
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.StartFlingSpin then

        BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin =
            standToResume

        BotMode.StartFlingSpin(
            target,
            false
        )

    end

end

BotMode.Commands["loopfling"] = function(sender, args)

    if not args
    or #args == 0 then
        return
    end


    local query =
        table.concat(
            args,
            " "
        )


    local target =
        findBotCommandPlayer(
            query
        )

    if not target then
        return
    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- STOP NORMAL BOT MOVEMENT
    -------------------------------------------------

    local standToResume =
        BotMode.CaptureStandForFlingSpin()

    stopMovement()
    exitStandMode()


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- FINISH ANY PREVIOUS FLING SPIN SESSION
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.FlingSpinTarget
    and BotMode.StopFlingSpin then

        BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin = nil

        BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
            true
        )

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- LOOP FLING SPIN
    --
    -- true = keep same Player target through
    -- death / respawn.
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.StartFlingSpin then

        BotMode.ResumeAfterFlingSpin =
            standToResume

        BotMode.StartFlingSpin(
            target,
            true
        )

    end

end

BotMode.Commands["stand1"] = function(sender)

    stopMovement()
    stopStandMovement()

    settings.Stand1 = true
    playStandIdle()

    BotMode.Movement = "stand1"
    BotMode.Target = sender

end

    BotMode.Movement = "stand2"
    BotMode.Target = sender

end

BotMode.Commands["stand3"] = function(sender)

    settings.Stand3 = true
    playStandIdle()

    BotMode.Movement = "stand3"
    BotMode.Target = sender

end

BotMode.Commands["elevator"] = function(sender)

BotMode.Commands["pickup"] = function(sender)

        Phase = 1,
        Height = 0,
        Rotation = 90
    }
