BotMode.Commands["stop"] = function()

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- NORMAL BOT MOVEMENT
    -------------------------------------------------

    stopMovement()

    exitStandMode()


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- SPIN / LOOP FLING SPIN
    --
    -- true means return to the location saved
    -- before the Fling Spin session began.
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.StopFlingSpin then

        BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
            true
        )

    end

end

BotMode.MovementConnection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- BOT MODE DISABLED
    -------------------------------------------------

    if not BotMode.Enabled then
        return
    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- COMMAND FLING SPIN
    --
    -- This runs independently from the normal
    -- BotMode.Movement system.
    -------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.FlingSpinTarget then

        if BotMode.UpdateFlingSpin then
            BotMode.UpdateFlingSpin()
        end

        if not BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand then
            return
        end
    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- NORMAL BOT MOVEMENT
    -------------------------------------------------

    if not BotMode.Movement then

        BotMode.LastMoveTo =
            Vector3.zero

        return

    end


    local target =
        BotMode.Target

    if not target then
        BotMode.LastMoveTo = Vector3.zero
        return
    end

    if target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
        stopMovement()
        exitStandMode()
        return
    end

    local targetCharacter = target.Character
    local targetHumanoid = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
        stopMovement()
        exitStandMode()
        return
    end

    local targetRoot = targetCharacter and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not targetRoot or not humanoid or not root then
        return
    end

    ------------------------------------------------
    -- FOLLOW
    ------------------------------------------------

    if BotMode.Movement == "follow" then

        if (root.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude <= 4 then
            return
        end

        local destination = Vector3.new(
            targetRoot.Position.X - targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector.X * 3,
            root.Position.Y,
            targetRoot.Position.Z - targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector.Z * 3
        )

        if (destination - BotMode.LastMoveTo).Magnitude > 2 then
            BotMode.LastMoveTo = destination
            humanoid:MoveTo(destination)
        end

    ------------------------------------------------
    -- STAND 1
    ------------------------------------------------

    elseif BotMode.Movement == "stand1" then

        local t = tick()

        local offset = CFrame.new(
            3 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
            2.7 + math.sin(t * 4) * 0.4,
            0
        )

        local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            position,
            position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        )

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

    ------------------------------------------------
    -- STAND 2
    ------------------------------------------------

    elseif BotMode.Movement == "stand2" then

        local t = tick()

        local offset = CFrame.new(
            1.2 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
            3.2 + math.sin(t * 3) * 0.3,
            2.8 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.2
        )

        local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            position,
            position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        )

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

    ------------------------------------------------
    -- STAND 3
    ------------------------------------------------

    elseif BotMode.Movement == "stand3" then

        local t = tick()

        local offset = CFrame.new(
            -3 - math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
            2.7 + math.sin(t * 4) * 0.4,
            0
        )

        local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            position,
            position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        )

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- ELEVATOR
    -------------------------------------------------

    elseif BotMode.Movement == "elevator" then

        local data = BotMode.MovementData

        data.Height = data.Height or 0
        data.MaxHeight = data.MaxHeight or 6

        data.Height += dt * 2

        if data.Height >= data.MaxHeight then
            stopMovement()
            return
        end

        local goal =
            targetRoot.CFrame
            * CFrame.new(0, -3 + data.Height, 0)
            * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)

        local alpha = math.clamp(dt * 12, 0, 1)

        -- Smooth ONLY the position
        local newPosition = root.Position:Lerp(goal.Position, alpha)

        -- Keep the ORIGINAL rotation exactly
        local rotation = goal - goal.Position

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(newPosition) * rotation

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        
        end)

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- CARPET
    -------------------------------------------------

    elseif BotMode.Movement == "carpet" then

        local velocity = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local horizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(
            velocity.X,
            0,
            velocity.Z
        )

        local speed = horizontalVelocity.Magnitude

        -- Scale prediction based on movement speed
        local predictionScale = math.clamp(speed / 12, 0, 1) * 0.58
        local prediction = horizontalVelocity * predictionScale

        -- Original goal (keeps the bot perfectly flat)
        local goal =
            targetRoot.CFrame
            * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
            * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)

        local goalPosition = Vector3.new(
            targetRoot.Position.X + prediction.X,
            targetRoot.Position.Y - 3,
            targetRoot.Position.Z + prediction.Z
        )

        -- Faster catch-up when the player stops
        local alpha

        if speed > 2 then
            alpha = math.clamp(dt * 18, 0, 1)
        else
            alpha = math.clamp(dt * 35, 0, 1)
        end

        local smoothedPosition =
            root.Position:Lerp(
                goalPosition,
                alpha
            )

        -- Smooth only X/Z. Keeping Y exact prevents
        -- gravity and character collision resolution
        -- from progressively dragging the carpet down.
        local newPosition = Vector3.new(
            smoothedPosition.X,
            goalPosition.Y,
            smoothedPosition.Z
        )

        local rotation = goal - goal.Position

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(newPosition) * rotation

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

        end)

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- PICKUP
    -------------------------------------------------
    elseif BotMode.Movement == "pickup" then

        local data = BotMode.MovementData

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- PHASE 1
        -------------------------------------------------

        if data.Phase == 1 then

            local goal =
                targetRoot.CFrame
                * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)

            local alpha = math.clamp(dt * 18, 0, 1)

            local newPosition = root.Position:Lerp(goal.Position, alpha)

            local rotation = goal - goal.Position

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(newPosition) * rotation

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

            end)

            if (root.Position - goal.Position).Magnitude < 0.5 then
                data.Phase = 2
            end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- PHASE 2
        -------------------------------------------------

        elseif data.Phase == 2 then

            data.Height += dt * 4

            local goal =
                targetRoot.CFrame
                * CFrame.new(0, -3 + data.Height, 0)
                * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)

            local alpha = math.clamp(dt * 20, 0, 1)

            local newPosition = root.Position:Lerp(goal.Position, alpha)

            local rotation = goal - goal.Position

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(newPosition) * rotation

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

            end)

            if data.Height >= 5 then
                data.Phase = 3
            end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- PHASE 3
        -------------------------------------------------

        elseif data.Phase == 3 then

            local position = targetRoot.Position + Vector3.new(0, -3.3, 0)

            local goal = CFrame.new(
                position,
                position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
            )

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame = goal

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
                humanoid.AutoRotate = true

            end)

            stopMovement()

        end

    end

end)

commands["kill"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			if humanoid then
				humanoid.Health = 0
			end
		end
	end
end

commands["bring"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			local senderRoot = sender.Character and sender.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if senderRoot and root then
				bypassAntiTeleport(function()
					root.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame + senderRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 3
					root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					root.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame + senderRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 3
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

commands["benx"] = function(sender, args)
    local targetName = args[1]
    if not targetName then
        return
    end

    for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
        if target == player then

            local previous = commands._BenxState

            if previous then
                if previous.Connection then
                    previous.Connection:Disconnect()
                end
                commands._BenxState = nil
            end

            settings.Benx = true

            local state = {
                Sender = sender,
                Connection = nil,
                SenderCharacter = nil,
                PoseBaseline = nil
            }

            commands._BenxState = state

            state.Connection = Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()

                if not settings.Benx
                or commands._BenxState ~= state then

                    state.Connection:Disconnect()

                    if commands._BenxState == state then
                        commands._BenxState = nil
                    end

                    if humanoid then
                        humanoid.Sit = false
                    end

                    return
                end

                local senderCharacter = sender.Character
                local targetCharacter = player.Character

                local senderRoot = senderCharacter
                    and senderCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                local animatedTorso = senderCharacter
                    and (
                        senderCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                        or senderCharacter:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        or senderCharacter:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso")
                    )

                local targetRoot = targetCharacter
                    and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                local targetHumanoid = targetCharacter
                    and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if not senderRoot
                or not targetRoot
                or not targetHumanoid
                or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then

                    return

                end

                targetHumanoid.Sit = true

                local s = math.sin(tick() * 30) -- last number is speed of bounce

                local distance = 1.65 + s * 0.85 -- 1st number is how far target is from attached body, 2nd is how far they go out and back
                local bounce = s * 0.08

                local poseCFrame = senderRoot.CFrame

                if animatedTorso then

                    local animationOffset =
                        senderRoot.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(
                            animatedTorso.CFrame
                        )

                    if state.SenderCharacter ~= senderCharacter then

                        state.SenderCharacter = senderCharacter
                        state.PoseBaseline = animationOffset

                    elseif not state.PoseBaseline then

                        state.PoseBaseline = animationOffset

                    end

                    poseCFrame =
                        senderRoot.CFrame
                        * animationOffset
                        * state.PoseBaseline:Inverse()

                end

                local goal =
                    poseCFrame
                    *
                    CFrame.new(
                        0,
                        -0.95 + bounce,
                        -distance
                    )
                    * CFrame.Angles(
                        math.rad(90),
                        math.rad(180),
                        math.rad(180)
                    )

                bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                    targetRoot.CFrame = goal
                    targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    targetRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                end)

            end)

        end
    end
end

commands["unbenx"] = function(sender, args)
    local targetName = args[1]
    if not targetName then
        return
    end

    for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
        if target == player then
            settings.Benx = false

            local state = commands._BenxState

            if state then
                if state.Connection then
                    state.Connection:Disconnect()
                end
                commands._BenxState = nil
            end

            if humanoid then
                humanoid.Sit = false
            end
        end
    end
end

commands["firework"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			if root then
				local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
				bodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 300, 0)
				bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
				bodyVelocity.Parent = root

				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(bodyVelocity, 0.2)
			end
		end
	end
end

local commandOrbit = {
	Active = false,
	Generation = 0,
	OriginalMovement = nil,
}

function commandOrbit.RestoreMovement()
	local saved = commandOrbit.OriginalMovement

	if saved and saved.Humanoid and saved.Humanoid.Parent then
		saved.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = saved.WalkSpeed
		saved.Humanoid.JumpPower = saved.JumpPower
		saved.Humanoid.JumpHeight = saved.JumpHeight
	end

	commandOrbit.OriginalMovement = nil
end

function AntiFling.RestorePlayerCollisions()
    for part, original in pairs(AntiFling.ForeignCollisionStates) do
        if part and part.Parent then
            part.CanCollide = original
        end
    end
    table.clear(AntiFling.ForeignCollisionStates)
end

function AntiFling.SuppressCharacterCollisions(otherCharacter)
    if not settings.AntiFling
    or not otherCharacter then
        return
    end

    for _, otherPart in ipairs(otherCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
        if otherPart:IsA("BasePart") then
            if AntiFling.ForeignCollisionStates[otherPart] == nil then
                AntiFling.ForeignCollisionStates[otherPart] = otherPart.CanCollide
            end
            otherPart.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
end

commands["orbit"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			if root and humanoid then
				if not commandOrbit.Active then
					commandOrbit.OriginalMovement = {
						Humanoid = humanoid,
						WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed,
						JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
						JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight,
					}
				end

				commandOrbit.Generation += 1
				local generation = commandOrbit.Generation
				commandOrbit.Active = true
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
				humanoid.JumpPower = 0

				local horizontalAngle = 0
				local verticalAngle = 0
				local orbitRadius = 10
				local orbitSpeed = 2

				local connection
				connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
					if not sender.Character or not sender.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						connection:Disconnect()
						commandOrbit.Active = false
						commandOrbit.RestoreMovement()
						return
					end

					if not commandOrbit.Active
					or generation ~= commandOrbit.Generation then
						connection:Disconnect()
						commandOrbit.RestoreMovement()
						return
					end

					local senderRoot = sender.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					horizontalAngle = horizontalAngle + orbitSpeed * dt
					verticalAngle = verticalAngle + (orbitSpeed * 0.7) * dt

					local x = senderRoot.Position.X + orbitRadius * math.cos(verticalAngle) * math.cos(horizontalAngle)
					local y = senderRoot.Position.Y + orbitRadius * math.sin(verticalAngle)
					local z = senderRoot.Position.Z + orbitRadius * math.cos(verticalAngle) * math.sin(horizontalAngle)

					antiTeleport.Bypass = true
					root.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, y, z)
					root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position
					antiTeleport.Bypass = false

					if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
						humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
					end
				end)

			end
		end
	end
end

commands["unorbit"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			commandOrbit.Active = false
			commandOrbit.Generation += 1
			commandOrbit.RestoreMovement()
		end
	end
end

commands["spin"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			settings.Spinning = true
			if commands._SpinConnection then
				commands._SpinConnection:Disconnect()
			end
			local angle = 0
			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				if not settings.Spinning then
					connection:Disconnect()
					if commands._SpinConnection == connection then
						commands._SpinConnection = nil
					end
					return
				end
				angle = angle + 20 * dt
				if root then
					bypassAntiTeleport(function()
						root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(angle * 10), 0)
						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end)
				end
			end)
			commands._SpinConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["unspin"] = function(sender, args)

			end
		end
	end
end

	if not targetName then return end

			settings.Bouncing = true

				if not settings.Bouncing then
					connection:Disconnect()

						commands._BounceConnection = nil
					end
					return
				end
				if root then
					local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
					if vel.Y <= 0 then
						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, 80, vel.Z)
					end
				end
			end)
			commands._BounceConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["freeze"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			if not root or not humanoid then
				return
			end

			if commands._FreezeConnection then
				commands._FreezeConnection:Disconnect()
			end

			local frozenPos = root.CFrame
			if not settings.Frozen then
				commands._FreezeMovement = {
					Humanoid = humanoid,
					WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed,
					JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
					JumpHeight = humanoid.JumpHeight,
				}
			end
			settings.Frozen = true
			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not settings.Frozen then
					connection:Disconnect()
					if commands._FreezeConnection == connection then
						commands._FreezeConnection = nil
					end
					return
				end
				if root then
					bypassAntiTeleport(function()
						root.CFrame = frozenPos
						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end)
				end
				if humanoid then
					humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
					humanoid.JumpPower = 0
				end
			end)
			commands._FreezeConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["unfreeze"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			settings.Frozen = false
			if commands._FreezeConnection then
				commands._FreezeConnection:Disconnect()
				commands._FreezeConnection = nil
			end
			local saved = commands._FreezeMovement
			if saved and saved.Humanoid and saved.Humanoid.Parent then
				saved.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = saved.WalkSpeed
				saved.Humanoid.JumpPower = saved.JumpPower
				saved.Humanoid.JumpHeight = saved.JumpHeight
			end
			commands._FreezeMovement = nil
		end
	end
end

	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end
	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			settings.ForceFollowing = true
			if commands._FollowConnection then commands._FollowConnection:Disconnect() end
			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not settings.ForceFollowing then
					connection:Disconnect()
					if commands._FollowConnection == connection then commands._FollowConnection = nil end
					return
				end
				local senderChar = sender.Character
				if senderChar then
					local senderRoot = senderChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if senderRoot and root then
						bypassAntiTeleport(function()
							root.CFrame = senderRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 4)
							root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
							root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
			commands._FollowConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["checkuser"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then
		return
	end

	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if target == player then
			local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
			local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
			local general = channels and channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")

			if general then
				pcall(function()
					general:SendAsync(("@%s [Lunacy Hub] I'm using Lunacy Hub."):format(sender.Name))
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end

commands["stand1"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end

	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if sender == player then
            stopStandMovement()
			settings.Stand1 = true
            playStandIdle()

			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not settings.Stand1 then
					connection:Disconnect()
					return
				end

				local targetChar = target.Character
				if targetChar then
					local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if targetRoot and root then
						local t = tick()

						local offset = CFrame.new(
							3 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
							2.7 + math.sin(t * 4) * 0.4,
							0
						)

						local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

						bypassAntiTeleport(function()
						root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
							position,
							position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
						)

						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
			commands._StandConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["stand2"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end

	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if sender == player then
            stopStandMovement()
			settings.Stand2 = true
            playStandIdle()

			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not settings.Stand2 then
					connection:Disconnect()
					return
				end

				local targetChar = target.Character
				if targetChar then
					local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if targetRoot and root then
						local t = tick()

						local offset = CFrame.new(
							1.2 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
							3.2 + math.sin(t * 3) * 0.3,
							2.8 + math.cos(t * 2) * 0.2
						)

						local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

						bypassAntiTeleport(function()
						root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
							position,
							position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
						)

						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
			commands._StandConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["stand3"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then return end

	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if sender == player then
            stopStandMovement()
			settings.Stand3 = true
            playStandIdle()

			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not settings.Stand3 then
					connection:Disconnect()
					return
				end

				local targetChar = target.Character
				if targetChar then
					local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

					if targetRoot and root then
						local t = tick()

						local offset = CFrame.new(
							-3 - math.cos(t * 2) * 0.15,
							2.7 + math.sin(t * 4) * 0.4,
							0
						)

						local position = (targetRoot.CFrame * offset).Position

						bypassAntiTeleport(function()
						root.CFrame = CFrame.new(
							position,
							position + targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector
						)

						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						end)
					end
				end
			end)
			commands._StandConnection = connection
		end
	end
end

commands["unstand"] = function(sender, args)
	local targetName = args[1]
	if not targetName then
		return
	end

	for _, target in ipairs(findTargets(sender.Name, targetName)) do
		if sender == player then
			exitStandMode()
		end
	end
end

--================================================
-- CHAT LISTENER
--================================================
local function onChat(message, senderPlayer)

    message = message
        :gsub("&gt;", ">")
        :gsub("&lt;", "<")
        :gsub("&amp;", "&")

    --================================================
    -- BOT MODE
    --================================================

    if message:sub(1, #BotMode.Prefix) == BotMode.Prefix then

        if not BotMode.Enabled then
            return
        end

        local body = message:sub(#BotMode.Prefix + 1)

        local parts = {}
        for word in body:gmatch("%S+") do
            table.insert(parts, word)
        end

        local cmdName = (parts[1] or ""):lower()
        table.remove(parts, 1)

        --========================================
        -- BOT OWNER COMMANDS
        -- Only the bot itself may execute these.
        --========================================

        if senderPlayer == player then
            local cmd = BotMode.OwnerCommands[cmdName]

            if cmd then
                local success, err = pcall(cmd, senderPlayer, parts)

                if not success then
                    warn(("Bot owner command '%s' failed: %s"):format(cmdName, err))
                end

                return
            end
        end

        --========================================
        -- CONTROLLER COMMANDS
        --========================================

        local isBotOwner =
            senderPlayer == player

        local isWhitelisted =
            BotMode.Whitelist[senderPlayer] == true

        ----------------------------------------
        -- >stop may be used by BOTH
        -- the bot owner and whitelisted users.
        ----------------------------------------

        if cmdName == "stop" then

            if not isBotOwner
            and not isWhitelisted then
                return
            end

        else

            ------------------------------------
            -- Everything else keeps the old
            -- behavior: whitelist only.
            ------------------------------------

            if not isWhitelisted then
                return
            end

        end

        local cmd =
            BotMode.Commands[cmdName]

        if cmd then

            local success, err =
                pcall(
                    cmd,
                    senderPlayer,
                    parts
                )

            if not success then

                warn(
                    ("Bot command '%s' failed: %s")
                        :format(
                            cmdName,
                            err
                        )
                )

            end

        end

        return
    end

    --================================================
    -- PREMIUM COMMANDS
    --================================================

    if message:sub(1, #PREFIX) ~= PREFIX then
        return
    end

    if whitelist[senderPlayer.Name] == nil then
        return
    end

    local body = message:sub(#PREFIX + 1)

    local parts = {}
    for word in body:gmatch("%S+") do
        table.insert(parts, word)
    end

    local cmdName = (parts[1] or ""):lower()
    table.remove(parts, 1)

    local cmd = commands[cmdName]

    if cmd then
        currentCommandInfo = commandInfo[cmdName]

        local success, err = pcall(cmd, senderPlayer, parts)

        currentCommandInfo = nil

        if not success then
            warn(("Command '%s' failed: %s"):format(cmdName, err))
        end
    end
end

local function hookPlayerChat(plr)
	plr.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
		onChat(msg, plr)
	end)
end

local function hookLocalCommands()

    player.Chatted:Connect(function(message)

        message = message:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")

        if message == "!rj" then

            Services.TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, player)

        end

    end)

end

local function hookPlayer(plr)

    hookPlayerChat(plr)

    ----------------------------------
    -- Current character
    ----------------------------------

    if plr.Character then

        task.spawn(function()

            task.wait(1)

            applyNametag(plr)
            applyOwnerTag(plr)

        end)

    end

    ----------------------------------
    -- Future respawns
    ----------------------------------

    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()

        task.wait(1)

        applyNametag(plr)
        applyOwnerTag(plr)

    end)

end

for _, plr in ipairs(
    Services.Players:GetPlayers()
) do

    hookPlayer(plr)

end

hookLocalCommands()

Services.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(
    hookPlayer
)

Services.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)

    BotMode.Whitelist[plr] = nil

    local connection =
        nametagConnections[plr]

    if connection then

        connection:Disconnect()

        nametagConnections[plr] =
            nil

    end

    nametagLabels[plr] = nil

end)

currentSpeed = 16

Services.UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if settings.InfiniteJump and humanoid then
        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    end
end)
--================================================
-- ANIMATION UTILITIES
--================================================
local function createButtonAnimation(button, hoverColor, normalColor, pressColor)

	local isPressed = false
	local active = false

	button:SetAttribute("Active", false)

	local function updateColor()
		local targetColor

		if isPressed then
			targetColor = pressColor
		elseif active then
			targetColor = Color3.fromRGB(180, 30, 30)
		else
			targetColor = normalColor
		end

		Services.TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
			BackgroundColor3 = targetColor
		}):Play()
	end

	button:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Active"):Connect(function()
		active = button:GetAttribute("Active")
		updateColor()
	end)

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			isPressed = true
			updateColor()
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			isPressed = false
			updateColor()
		end
	end)

	button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not isPressed and not active then
			Services.TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = hoverColor
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		updateColor()
	end)

end

--================================================
-- LEFT ALT TOGGLE WITH ANIMATION
--================================================
local uiVisible = true
local originalTransparencies = {}

-- Store original transparency values
local function storeOriginalTransparencies(target)
	if target:IsA("GuiObject") then
		if target:IsA("Frame") or target:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			originalTransparencies[target] = {
				bg = target.BackgroundTransparency
			}
		end
		if target:IsA("TextLabel") or target:IsA("TextButton") then
			originalTransparencies[target] = {
				bg = target.BackgroundTransparency,
				text = target.TextTransparency
			}
		end
		if target:IsA("ImageLabel") or target:IsA("ImageButton") then
			originalTransparencies[target] = {
				bg = target.BackgroundTransparency,
				img = target.ImageTransparency
			}
		end
	end
	
	for _, child in ipairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
			if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
				originalTransparencies[child] = {
					bg = child.BackgroundTransparency
				}
			end
			if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
				originalTransparencies[child] = {
					bg = child.BackgroundTransparency,
					text = child.TextTransparency
				}
			end
			if child:IsA("ImageLabel") or child:IsA("ImageButton") then
				originalTransparencies[child] = {
					bg = child.BackgroundTransparency,
					img = child.ImageTransparency
				}
			end
		end
        if child:IsA("UIStroke") then
            originalTransparencies[child] = {
                stroke = child.Transparency
            }
        end
	end
end

local function fadeElement(element, fadeOut, tweenInfo)

    local original = originalTransparencies[element]
    if not original then
        return
    end

    local props = {}

    if original.bg ~= nil then
        props.BackgroundTransparency = fadeOut and 1 or original.bg
    end

    if original.text ~= nil then
        props.TextTransparency = fadeOut and 1 or original.text
    end

    if original.img ~= nil then
        props.ImageTransparency = fadeOut and 1 or original.img
    end

    if original.stroke ~= nil then
        props.Transparency = fadeOut and 1 or original.stroke
    end

    if next(props) then
        Services.TweenService:Create(element, tweenInfo, props):Play()
    end

end

local function fadeUIElements(target, fadeOut)

    local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(
        0.3,
        Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
        Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
    )

    -- Fade the target itself
    fadeElement(target, fadeOut, tweenInfo)

    -- Fade every descendant
    for _, element in ipairs(target:GetDescendants()) do
        fadeElement(element, fadeOut, tweenInfo)
    end

end

-- Store original values AFTER all UI is fully created (at the very end of script)

Services.UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
	if gameProcessed then return end

    -- Waiting for Respawn key selection
if keybindState.Mode == "Movement" and keybindState.Waiting then

    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then

        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace then
            keybinds[keybindState.Waiting].Key = nil

        elseif input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
            keybinds[keybindState.Waiting].Key = input.KeyCode
        end

        if keybindState.Waiting == "Respawn" then

            keybindButton.Text =
                keybinds.Respawn.Key and keybinds.Respawn.Key.Name or "None"

        elseif keybindState.Waiting == "Fly" then

            flyButton.Text =
                keybinds.Fly.Key and keybinds.Fly.Key.Name or "None"

        elseif keybindState.Waiting == "ClickTP" then

            clickTPButton.Text =
                keybinds.ClickTP.Key and keybinds.ClickTP.Key.Name or "None"

        end

    end

    keybindState.Waiting = nil
    keybindState.Mode = nil
    inputState.UICapturingInput = false
    Controls:Enable()

    return
end

    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        if input.KeyCode == keybinds.ClickTP.Key then
            inputState.ClickTPHeld = true
            end
    end

    for _, bind in pairs(keybinds) do
        if bind.Key
        and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard
        and input.KeyCode == bind.Key
        and bind.Callback then

            bind.Callback()
            break -- stop checking after the first match

        end
    end

    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt then
        uiVisible = not uiVisible

        if uiVisible then
            uiRoot.Visible = true
            fadeUIElements(uiRoot, false)
        else
            fadeUIElements(uiRoot, true)

            task.delay(0.3, function()
                if not uiVisible then
                    uiRoot.Visible = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

-- DRAG SYSTEM
--================================================
local dragging = false
local dragStart
local startPos

topbar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = true
		dragStart = input.Position
		startPos = main.Position
	end
end)

topbar.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
		dragging = false
	end
end)

Services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		local delta = input.Position - dragStart
		main.Position = UDim2.new(
			startPos.X.Scale,
			startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
			startPos.Y.Scale,
			startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
		)
	end
end)

--================================================
-- TAB SWITCH
--================================================
local function switchTab(tab)
	if tab == "Home" then
		task.defer(function()
			if _G.fadeHomeCredits then
				_G.fadeHomeCredits()
			end
		end)
	end
	for name, page in pairs(pages) do
		page.Visible = (name == tab)
	end
	for _, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
	end
	local btn = tabButtons[tab]
	if btn then
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
		Services.TweenService:Create(indicator, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, tabY[tab])
		}):Play()
	end
end

--================================================
-- TAB BUTTONS WITH ANIMATIONS
--================================================
for i, name in ipairs(tabs) do
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 32)
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 10 + (i - 1) * 40)
	btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
	btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	btn.Text = name
	btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	btn.TextSize = 12
	btn.Parent = sidebar

	Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	tabButtons[name] = btn
	tabY[name] = btn.Position.Y.Offset

	-- Add button animations
	createButtonAnimation(
		btn,
		Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75),  -- hover
		Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50),  -- normal
		Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40)   -- press
	)

	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		-- Add click animation with scale
		local originalSize = btn.Size
		Services.TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = UDim2.new(originalSize.X.Scale, originalSize.X.Offset * 0.95, originalSize.Y.Scale, originalSize.Y.Offset)
		}):Play()
		
		task.wait(0.05)
		
		Services.TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Size = originalSize
		}):Play()
		
		switchTab(name)
	end)
end

--================================================
-- TOGGLES
--================================================
local function hasRequiredRole(requiredRole)

    if not requiredRole then
        return true
    end

    local playerRole = getPlayerRole(player)

    return rankPower[playerRole] >= rankPower[requiredRole]

end

local function createToggle(parent, text, key, callback, requiredRole)

	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parent

	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Size = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 1, 0)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Text = text
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
	label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	label.TextSize = 13
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = row

	local track = Instance.new("Frame")
	track.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 22)
	track.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -11)
	track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
	track.BorderSizePixel = 0
	track.Parent = row

	Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
	knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
	knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
	knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
	knob.Parent = track

	Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local function set(on)
		Services.TweenService:Create(knob, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
			Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, -9) or UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -9)
		}):Play()
		Services.TweenService:Create(track, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
			BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(60,170,90) or Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
		}):Play()
	end

    set(settings[key])

	row.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            return
        end

        if not hasRequiredRole(requiredRole) then
            return
        end

		settings[key] = not settings[key]

        if callback then
            callback(settings[key])
        end

        if key == "AutoEat" and settings[key] then
            task.spawn(autoEat)
        end

        if settings[key] then

            if key == "RemoveBlizzardUI" then
                removeBlizzardGui()

            elseif key == "RemoveSandstormUI" then
                removeSandstormGui()

            elseif key == "RemoveMugshotUI" then
                removeMugshotGui()

            end

        end

        set(settings[key])
    end)
end

local function createButton(parent, text, callback, requiredRole)

    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parent

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0.7, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    label.TextSize = 13
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.Parent = row

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 24)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -76, 0.5, -12)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55,55,70)
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.Text = "Run"
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.TextSize = 12
    button.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)

    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasRequiredRole(requiredRole) then
            return
        end

        Services.TweenService:Create(button,TweenInfo.new(.1),{

            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70,170,90)

        }):Play()

        task.delay(.15,function()

            Services.TweenService:Create(button,TweenInfo.new(.15),{

                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(55,55,70)

            }):Play()

        end)

        if callback then
            callback()
        end

    end)

end

local function createActionButton(parent, text, callback)

    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parent

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
    button.BorderSizePixel = 0
    button.Text = text
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    button.TextSize = 12
    button.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)

    button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()

        Services.TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50,50,60)
        }):Play()

    end)

    button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()

        Services.TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30,30,38)
        }):Play()

    end)

    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(callback)

    return button

end

local function createButtonPair(parent, leftText, rightText, requiredRole)

    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,36)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parent

    local left = Instance.new("TextButton")
    left.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-5,1,0)
    left.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
    left.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
    left.BorderSizePixel = 0
    left.Text = leftText
    left.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230,230,230)
    left.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    left.TextSize = 12
    left.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", left).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)

    local right = Instance.new("TextButton")
    right.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-5,1,0)
    right.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,5,0,0)
    right.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
    right.BorderSizePixel = 0
    right.Text = rightText
    right.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230,230,230)
    right.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    right.TextSize = 12
    right.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", right).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)

    createButtonAnimation(
        left,
        Color3.fromRGB(70,70,80),
        Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50),
        Color3.fromRGB(25,25,35)
    )

    createButtonAnimation(
        right,
        Color3.fromRGB(70,70,80),
        Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50),
        Color3.fromRGB(25,25,35)
    )

    return left, right
end

local function createSlider(parent, text, settingKey, min, max, defaultValue, requiredRole)

    if settings[settingKey] == nil then
        settings[settingKey] = defaultValue
    end

    local currentValue = settings[settingKey]

    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,36)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.Parent = parent

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text .. ": " .. currentValue
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160,160,180)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.Parent = row

    local track = Instance.new("Frame")
    track.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
    track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22)
    track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
    track.BorderSizePixel = 0
    track.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)

    local percent = (currentValue - min) / (max - min)

    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(percent,0,1,0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220,50,50)
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = track

    Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)

    local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
    knob.Size = UDim2.new(0,14,0,14)
    knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
    knob.Position = UDim2.new(percent,0,0.5,0)
    knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    knob.Parent = track

    Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)

    local dragging = false

    knob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)

        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            return
        end

        if not hasRequiredRole(requiredRole) then
            return
        end

        dragging = true

    end)

    Services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging = false
        end
    end)

    Services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)

        if not dragging then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            return
        end

        local relX = math.clamp(
            (input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / track.AbsoluteSize.X,
            0,
            1
        )

        local value = math.floor(min + relX * (max - min))

        settings[settingKey] = value

        label.Text = text .. ": " .. value

        fill.Size = UDim2.new(relX,0,1,0)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(relX,0,0.5,0)

    end)

    return row, label

end

local function showDropdown(button, options, callback)

    dropdownPopup:ClearAllChildren()

    Instance.new("UICorner", dropdownPopup).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)

    dropdownPopup.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,#options * 24)

    local absPos = button.AbsolutePosition

    dropdownPopup.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
        absPos.X,
        absPos.Y + button.AbsoluteSize.Y + 2
    )

    dropdownPopup.Visible = true

    for i, optionText in ipairs(options) do

        local option = Instance.new("TextButton")
        option.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,24)
        option.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(i-1)*24)
        option.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        option.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        option.TextSize = 10
        option.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
        option.Text = optionText
        option.ZIndex = 101
        option.Parent = dropdownPopup

        option.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

            dropdownPopup.Visible = false

            callback(optionText)

        end)

    end

end

local function createScrollingPage(page)

    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20)
    scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
    scroll.Active = true
    scroll.TopImage = ""
    scroll.BottomImage = ""
    scroll.MidImage = ""
    scroll.Parent = page

    task.defer(function()
        scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,scroll.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y)
    end)

    Instance.new("UICorner", scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,8)

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0,6)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = scroll

    layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
            0,
            0,
            0,
            layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10
        )
    end)

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0,8)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,8)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0,8)
    padding.Parent = scroll

    return scroll

end

local function createSection(parent, height)
    local section = Instance.new("Frame")
    section.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
    section.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    section.Parent = parent

    return section
end

local function createDropdown(
    parent,
    text,
    settingKey,
    options,
    requiredRole,
    callback
)

    settings[settingKey] =
        settings[settingKey]
        or options[1]

    local row =
        Instance.new("Frame")

    row.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            0,
            0,
            40
        )

    row.Parent =
        parent

    row.BackgroundTransparency =
        1


    local label =
        Instance.new("TextLabel")

    label.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            0.5,
            0,
            1,
            0
        )

    label.BackgroundTransparency =
        1

    label.Text =
        text

    label.TextColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            240,
            240,
            240
        )

    label.Font =
        Enum.Font.Gotham

    label.TextSize =
        13

    label.TextXAlignment =
        Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

    label.Parent =
        row


    local button =
        Instance.new("TextButton")

    button.Size =
        UDim2.new(
            0,
            120,
            0,
            24
        )

    button.Position =
        UDim2.new(
            1,
            -120,
            0.5,
            -12
        )

    button.BackgroundColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            40,
            40,
            50
        )

    button.TextColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            240,
            240,
            240
        )

    button.Font =
        Enum.Font.Gotham

    button.TextSize =
        10

    button.Text =
        "▾ "
        .. settings[settingKey]

    button.Parent =
        row


    Instance.new(
        "UICorner",
        button
    ).CornerRadius =
        UDim.new(
            0,
            4
        )


    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasRequiredRole(
            requiredRole
        ) then
            return
        end

        showDropdown(
            button,
            options,
            function(selected)

                settings[settingKey] =
                    selected

                button.Text =
                    "▾ "
                    .. selected

                if callback then

                    callback(
                        selected
                    )

                end

            end
        )

    end)

end

local function createKeybind(parent, text, settingKey)

    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40)
    row.Parent = parent
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = text
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    label.TextSize = 13
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.Parent = row

    local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
    button.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,24)
    button.Position = UDim2.new(1,-120,0.5,-12)
    button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
    button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    button.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    button.TextSize = 10
    button.Text = "None"
    button.Parent = row

    Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,4)

    button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasRequiredRole(requiredRole) then
            return
        end

        keybindState.Waiting = true
        keybindState.Mode = "Combat"
        keybindState.Setting = settingKey
        keybindState.Button = button

        button.Text = "Press any key..."

    end)

end

Services.UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)

    if gameProcessed then
        return
    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Normal Aim Assist activation
    -------------------------------------------------

    if keybindState.Mode ~= "Combat" then

        if settings.AimKey then

            if input.KeyCode == settings.AimKey
            or input.UserInputType == settings.AimKey then

                aimState.KeyHeld = true

            end

        end

        return

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Keybind assignment
    -------------------------------------------------

    keybindState.Waiting = false

    local value

    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then

        value = input.KeyCode

    elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
        or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then

        value = input.UserInputType

    end

    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape
        or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace then

        value = nil

    end

    settings[keybindState.Setting] = value

    if value then

        keybindState.Button.Text = tostring(value)
            :gsub("Enum%.UserInputType%.","")
            :gsub("Enum%.KeyCode%.","")

    else

        keybindState.Button.Text = "None"

    end

    keybindState.Mode = nil
    keybindState.Setting = nil
    keybindState.Button = nil

end)

Services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)

    if not settings.AimKey then
        return
    end

    if input.KeyCode == settings.AimKey
    or input.UserInputType == settings.AimKey then

        aimState.KeyHeld = false
        aimState.CurrentTarget = nil

    end

end)

-- HOME TAB

local function createHomeTab()
	local page = pages["Home"]

	local container = Instance.new("Frame")
	container.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20)
	container.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
	container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	container.Parent = page

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = "Welcome to Lunacy Hub"
	title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	title.TextSize = 18
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.Parent = container

	local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	subtitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
	subtitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 40)
	subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	subtitle.Text = "Use the tabs on the left to navigate features."
	subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
	subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	subtitle.TextSize = 13
	subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	subtitle.Parent = container

	local info = Instance.new("Frame")
	info.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 180)
	info.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 90)
	info.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
	info.BorderSizePixel = 0
	info.Parent = container

	Instance.new("UICorner", info).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

	local function makeLine(text, y)
		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 20)
		lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.Text = text
		lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
		lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		lbl.TextSize = 12
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		lbl.TextTransparency = 1
		lbl.Parent = info
		return lbl
	end

	local creditLabels = {}
	creditLabels[1] = makeLine("Creator: lunacy/xavicys", 10)
	creditLabels[2] = makeLine("Roblox: @rainingallaround", 30)
	creditLabels[3] = makeLine("Discord: xavicys", 50)
	creditLabels[4] = makeLine("Version: 1.0", 70)

	for _, lbl in ipairs(creditLabels) do
		originalTransparencies[lbl] = {
			bg = lbl.BackgroundTransparency,
			text = 0
		}
	end

	local creatorId = 4122
	local creatorThumb = Services.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(creatorId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)

	local creatorPfpFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	creatorPfpFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 75, 0, 75)
	creatorPfpFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 90)
	creatorPfpFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	creatorPfpFrame.Parent = info
	Instance.new("UICorner", creatorPfpFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local creatorPfp = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	creatorPfp.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	creatorPfp.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	creatorPfp.Image = creatorThumb
	creatorPfp.Parent = creatorPfpFrame
	Instance.new("UICorner", creatorPfp).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local function fadeInCredits()
		for _, lbl in ipairs(creditLabels) do
			Services.TweenService:Create(lbl, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {
				TextTransparency = 0
			}):Play()
			originalTransparencies[lbl] = {
				bg = lbl.BackgroundTransparency,
				text = 0
			}
		end
	end

	_G.fadeHomeCredits = fadeInCredits
end

createHomeTab()

local function createCombatTab()

    local page = pages["Combat"]
    local scroll = createScrollingPage(page)

    createKeybind(scroll, "Aim Assist Keybind", "AimKey")

    createDropdown(
        scroll,
        "Aim Method",
        "AimMethod",
        {"Camera", "Cursor"}
    )

    createToggle(scroll, "Target Indicator", "TargetIndicator")

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Switch Margin",
        "TargetSwitchMargin",
        0,
        50,
        10
    )

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Strength",
        "AimStrength",
        1,
        100,
        15
    )

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "FOV",
        "AimFOV",
        25,
        500,
        150
    )

    createToggle(scroll, "Draw FOV", "DrawFOV")
    createToggle(scroll, "Sticky Aim", "StickyAim")
    createToggle(scroll, "Team Check", "TeamCheck")
    createToggle(scroll, "Visible Check", "VisibleCheck")
    createToggle(scroll, "Prediction", "Prediction")

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Prediction X",
        "PredictionX",
        -100,
        100,
        0
    )

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Prediction Y",
        "PredictionY",
        -100,
        100,
        0
    )

    createToggle(scroll, "Dynamic Prediction", "DynamicPrediction")

    createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Dynamic Scale",
        "DynamicPredictionScale",
        0,
        300,
        100
    )

    createDropdown(
        scroll,
        "Target Part",
        "TargetPart",
        {
            "Head",
            "HumanoidRootPart",
            "UpperTorso"
        }
    )

end

createCombatTab()
--================================================

local visuals = {

    ItemESP = {
        Enabled = false,
        Highlights = {},
    },

    Fullbright = {
        Enabled = false,
    },

    PlayerESP = {
        Enabled = false,
    },

    SkeletonESP = {
        Enabled = false,
        Objects = {},
    },

    BoxESP = {
        Enabled = false,
        Objects = {},
    },

    NearbyHighlight = {
        Enabled = false,
    },

    Nametags = {
        Enabled = false,
    },

    Tracers = {
        Enabled = false,
        Objects = {},
    },

    XRay = {
        Enabled = false,
        Parts = {},
    }

}

    local function enableXray()

        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

        if obj:IsA("BasePart")
        and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj.Parent)
        and not obj:IsDescendantOf(player.Character)
        and obj.Transparency < 1
        and obj.Name ~= "Water" then

                visuals.XRay.Parts[obj] = obj.Transparency

                obj.Transparency = math.max(
                    obj.Transparency,
                    0.75
                )
            end
        end
    end

    local function disableXray()

        for part, transparency in pairs(visuals.XRay.Parts) do

            if part and part.Parent then
                part.Transparency = transparency
            end
        end

        table.clear(visuals.XRay.Parts)
    end

    local function enableItemESP()
        
        for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do

            if obj:IsA("Tool") then

                local handle =
                    obj:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                
                if handle and not visuals.ItemESP.Highlights[obj] then

                    local highlight =
                        Instance.new("Highlight")

                    highlight.FillColor =
                        Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)

                    highlight.OutlineColor =
                        Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

                    highlight.DepthMode =
                        Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop

                    highlight.Parent = obj

                    visuals.ItemESP.Highlights[obj] =
                        highlight
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function disableItemESP()

        for _, highlight in pairs(visuals.ItemESP.Highlights) do

            if highlight then
                highlight:Destroy()
            end
        end

        table.clear(visuals.ItemESP.Highlights)
    end

local lightingState = {

    Service = game:GetService("Lighting"),

    Brightness = nil,
    ClockTime = nil,
    FogEnd = nil,
    GlobalShadows = nil,
    OutdoorAmbient = nil,
    Ambient = nil,
    ExposureCompensation = nil,

}

lightingState.Brightness = lightingState.Service.Brightness
lightingState.ClockTime = lightingState.Service.ClockTime
lightingState.FogEnd = lightingState.Service.FogEnd
lightingState.GlobalShadows = lightingState.Service.GlobalShadows
lightingState.OutdoorAmbient = lightingState.Service.OutdoorAmbient
lightingState.Ambient = lightingState.Service.Ambient
lightingState.ExposureCompensation = lightingState.Service.ExposureCompensation

    local scroll = createScrollingPage(page)

    createToggle(scroll, "Player ESP", "PlayerESP", function(enabled)

        visuals.PlayerESP.Enabled = enabled

    end)

        visuals.Nametags.Enabled = enabled

    end)

        visuals.Tracers.Enabled = enabled

        if not enabled then

            for _, tracer in pairs(visuals.Tracers.Objects) do

                if tracer then
                    tracer:Destroy()
                end

            end

            table.clear(visuals.Tracers.Objects)

        end

        visuals.NearbyHighlight.Enabled = enabled

            lightingState.Service.Brightness = 3
            lightingState.Service.ClockTime = 14
            lightingState.Service.FogEnd = 100000
            lightingState.Service.GlobalShadows = false
            lightingState.Service.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            lightingState.Service.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            lightingState.Service.ExposureCompensation = 0.5

        else

            lightingState.Service.Brightness = lightingState.Brightness
            lightingState.Service.ClockTime = lightingState.ClockTime
            lightingState.Service.FogEnd = lightingState.FogEnd
            lightingState.Service.GlobalShadows = lightingState.GlobalShadows
            lightingState.Service.OutdoorAmbient = lightingState.OutdoorAmbient
            lightingState.Service.Ambient = lightingState.Ambient
            lightingState.Service.ExposureCompensation = lightingState.Exposure

        end

    end)

            disableXray()

    local predictedDisaster
    local roundEnded = false

    local disasterNames = {
        "Acid Rain",
        "Avalanche",
        "Blizzard",
        "Deadly Virus",
        "Earthquake",

        "Flash Flood",
        "Meteor Shower",
        "Sandstorm",
        "Thunder Storm",

        "Tsunami",
        "Volcanic Eruption",
    }

    local function findDisasterName(value, depth)

        depth = depth or 0

        if depth > 2 then
            return nil
        end

        if typeof(value) == "string" then

            local lowered = string.lower(value)

            for _, disasterName in ipairs(disasterNames) do

                if string.find(
                    lowered,
                    string.lower(disasterName),
                    1,
                    true
                ) then

                    return disasterName

                end

            end

        elseif typeof(value) == "table" then

            for key, nestedValue in pairs(value) do

                local found =
                    findDisasterName(key, depth + 1)
                    or findDisasterName(
                        nestedValue,
                        depth + 1
                    )

                if found then
                    return found
                end

            end

        end

        return nil

    end

    local roundRemote =
        game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            :FindFirstChild("Remotes")

    roundRemote =
        roundRemote
        and roundRemote:FindFirstChild("Round")

    if roundRemote
    and roundRemote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then

        roundRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(action, ...)

            if action == "Display Survivors" then

                roundEnded = true
                predictedDisaster = nil
                disasterLabel.Text = "None"
                return

            end

            local found =
                findDisasterName(action)
                or findDisasterName({...})

            if found then

                roundEnded = false
                predictedDisaster = found
                disasterLabel.Text = found

            end

        end)

    end

    local contentModel =
        workspace:FindFirstChild("ContentModel")

    local lastMapName =
        contentModel
        and contentModel:FindFirstChild("Information")
        and contentModel.Information.Value

    task.spawn(function()

        local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

        while task.wait(1) do

            local currentMapName =
                contentModel
                and contentModel:FindFirstChild("Information")
                and contentModel.Information.Value

            if currentMapName
            and currentMapName ~= lastMapName then

                lastMapName = currentMapName
                predictedDisaster = nil
                roundEnded = true

            end

            local foundDisaster =
                predictedDisaster

            if not foundDisaster then

                if #disasterState.AvalancheParts > 0 then
                    foundDisaster = "Avalanche"

                elseif #disasterState.MeteorParts > 0 then
                    foundDisaster = "Meteor Shower"

                elseif workspace:FindFirstChild(
                    "TsunamiWave",
                    true
                ) then
                    foundDisaster = "Tsunami"

                elseif workspace:FindFirstChild(
                    "Tornado",
                    true
                ) then
                    foundDisaster = "Tornado"

                elseif playerGui:FindFirstChild(
                    "BlizzardGui"
                ) then
                    foundDisaster = "Blizzard"

                elseif playerGui:FindFirstChild(
                    "SandStormGui"
                ) then
                    foundDisaster = "Sandstorm"
                end

                predictedDisaster =
                    foundDisaster

            end

            local uis = playerGui:FindFirstChild("UIs")

            if uis then

                for _, gui in ipairs(uis:GetChildren()) do

                    local warningText =
                        gui:FindFirstChild("warningText")

                    if warningText
                    and warningText:IsA("TextLabel")
                    and warningText.Text ~= "" then

                        local text = warningText.Text

                            foundDisaster =
                                findDisasterName(text)
                                or string.match(text, "^(.-)!")
                                or text

                        predictedDisaster =
                            foundDisaster

                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if roundEnded then

                foundDisaster = nil
                predictedDisaster = nil

            end

            disasterLabel.Text =
                foundDisaster or "None"

        end
    end)

    local FOV = {

        Min = 70,
        Max = 120,

    }

    local fovRow = Instance.new("Frame")
