    fovLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    fovLabel.TextSize = 11
    fovLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    fovLabel.Parent = fovRow

    local fovTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
    fovTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6)
    fovTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 24)
    fovTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
    fovTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fovTrack.Parent = fovRow

    Instance.new("UICorner", fovTrack).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local fovFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fovFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    fovFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    fovFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fovFill.Parent = fovTrack

    Instance.new("UICorner", fovFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local fovKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
    fovKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
    fovKnob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    fovKnob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
    fovKnob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    fovKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fovKnob.Parent = fovTrack

    Instance.new("UICorner", fovKnob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    local draggingFOV = false

    fovKnob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
	    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
	    	draggingFOV = true
    	end
    end)

    Services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
    	if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
    		draggingFOV = false
    	end
    end)

    Services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    	if draggingFOV and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then

    		local trackPos = fovTrack.AbsolutePosition.X
    		local trackWidth = fovTrack.AbsoluteSize.X

    		local relX = math.clamp(
    			(input.Position.X - trackPos) / trackWidth,
    			0,
    			1
    		)

    		local fov = math.floor(
    			FOV.Min +
    			relX * (FOV.Max - FOV.Min)
    		)

    		workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = fov

    		fovLabel.Text = "FOV: " .. fov

    		fovFill.Size = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 1, 0)
    		fovKnob.Position = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 0.5, 0)
    	end
    end)
end

    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)

        if not visuals.XRay.Enabled then
            return
        end

        if obj:IsA("BasePart")
        and not Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj.Parent)

createVisualsTab()

local function updateTracers()

    if not visuals.Tracers.Enabled then
        return
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do

        if plr == player then
            continue
        end

        local character = plr.Character
        local tracer = visuals.Tracers.Objects[plr]

        if not character then

            if tracer then
                tracer:Destroy()
                visuals.Tracers.Objects[plr] = nil
            end

            continue
        end

        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not rootPart then

            if tracer then
                tracer:Destroy()
                visuals.Tracers.Objects[plr] = nil
            end

            continue
        end

        if not tracer then

            tracer = Instance.new("Frame")
            tracer.Name = plr.Name
            tracer.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(.5,.5)
            tracer.BorderSizePixel = 0
            tracer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)

            tracer.Parent = tracerContainer

            visuals.Tracers.Objects[plr] = tracer
        end

        local screenPos,onScreen =
            camera:WorldToViewportPoint(rootPart.Position)

        if not onScreen then
            tracer.Visible = false
            continue
        end

        tracer.Visible = true

        local startPos = Vector2.new(
            viewport.X/2,
            viewport.Y-20
        )

        local endPos = Vector2.new(
            screenPos.X,
            screenPos.Y
        )

        local length =
            (endPos-startPos).Magnitude

        if length < 5 then
            tracer.Visible = false
            continue
        end

        tracer.Size = UDim2.new(
            0,
            length,
            0,
            2
        )

        local midpoint =
            (startPos+endPos)/2

        tracer.Position = UDim2.new(
            0,
            midpoint.X,
            0,
            midpoint.Y
        )

        tracer.Rotation = math.deg(
            math.atan2(
                endPos.Y-startPos.Y,
                endPos.X-startPos.X
            )
        )

    end

end

local function updateNametags()

    if not visuals.Nametags.Enabled then

        for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do

            if plr.Character then

                local head = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

                if head then
                    local tag = head:FindFirstChild("LunacyNametag")

                    if tag then
                        tag:Destroy()
                    end
                end

            end
        end

        return
    end

    if not root then
        return
    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do

        if plr == player then
            continue
        end

        local character = plr.Character

        if not character then
            continue
        end

        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not head or not rootPart then
            continue
        end

        local billboard = head:FindFirstChild("LunacyNametag")

        if not billboard then

            billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "LunacyNametag"
            billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,40)
            billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0,3,0)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Parent = head

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
            label.TextSize = 12
            label.Parent = billboard

        end

        local distance =
            (root.Position-rootPart.Position).Magnitude

        billboard.TextLabel.Text =
            ("%s\n%d studs"):format(
                plr.Name,
                math.floor(distance)
            )

    end

end

local function GetPredictedPosition(part)

    if not settings.Prediction
    and not settings.DynamicPrediction then
        return part.Position
    end

    local velocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity

    local x = settings.PredictionX / 100
    local y = settings.PredictionY / 100

    if settings.DynamicPrediction then
        
        local speed = velocity.Magnitude

        local scale = (settings.DynamicPredictionScale / 100) * (speed / 16)

        x *= scale
        y *= scale
    end

    return part.Position + Vector3.new(

        velocity.X * x,

        velocity.Y * y,

        velocity.Z * x

    )

end

local function IsVisible(targetPart)

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local direction = targetPart.Position - origin

    local params = RaycastParams.new()
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {
        character
    }

    local result = workspace:Raycast(
        origin,
        direction,
        params
    )

    if not result then
        return true
    end

    return result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent)

end

local function GetClosestPlayerInFOV()

    local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

    local closestPlayer = nil
    local closestDistance = math.huge

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do

        if plr ~= player then

            local character = plr.Character

            if not character then
                continue
            end

            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

            if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                continue
            end

            if settings.TeamCheck then

                if plr.Team == player.Team then
                    continue
                end
            
            end

            local targetPart = character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)

            if not targetPart then
                continue
            end

            if settings.VisibleCheck then
                if not IsVisible(targetPart) then
                    continue
                end

            end

            local screenPos, onScreen =
                workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)

            if not onScreen then
                continue
            end

            local distance =
                (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mouse).Magnitude

            if distance > settings.AimFOV then
                continue
            end

            if distance < closestDistance then

                closestDistance = distance
                closestPlayer = plr

            end

        end

    end

    return closestPlayer

end

local function updateCameraAim(targetPart)

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local desired = CFrame.lookAt(
        camera.CFrame.Position,
        GetPredictedPosition(targetPart)
    )

    camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(
        desired,
        settings.AimStrength / 100
    )

end

local function updateCursorAim(targetPart)

    local screenPos, onScreen =
        workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(GetPredictedPosition(targetPart))

    if not onScreen then
        return
    end

    local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

    local difference = Vector2.new(
        screenPos.X - mouse.X,
        screenPos.Y - mouse.Y
    )

    local strength = settings.AimStrength / 100

    mousemoverel(
        difference.X * strength,
        difference.Y * strength
    )

end

local function IsTargetValid(player)

    if not player then
        return false
    end

    if player == Services.Players.LocalPlayer then
        return false
    end

    if not player.Character then
        return false
    end

    local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end

    local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)

    if not targetPart then
        return false
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local screenPos, visible =
        camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)

    if not visible then
        return false
    end

    local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

    local distance = (
        Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
        - mouse
    ).Magnitude

    if distance > settings.AimFOV then
        return false
    end

    if settings.TeamCheck then

        if player.Team == Services.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
            return false
        end

    end

    if settings.VisibleCheck then

        if not IsVisible(targetPart) then
            return false
        end

    end

    return true

end

local function GetBestTarget(currentTarget)

    local bestTarget = GetClosestPlayerInFOV()

    -- No current target
    if not currentTarget then
        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0
        return bestTarget
    end

    -- Current target became invalid
    if not IsTargetValid(currentTarget) then
        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0
        return bestTarget
    end

    -- Nobody else available
    if not bestTarget then
        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0
        return currentTarget
    end

    -- Already the best target
    if bestTarget == currentTarget then
        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0
        return currentTarget
    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Compare screen distance
    --------------------------------------------------

    local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

    local currentPart =
        currentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)

    local bestPart =
        bestTarget.Character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)

    if not currentPart or not bestPart then
        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0
        return currentTarget
    end

    local currentScreen =
        workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(currentPart.Position)

    local bestScreen =
        workspace.CurrentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(bestPart.Position)

    local currentDistance =
        (Vector2.new(currentScreen.X,currentScreen.Y)-mouse).Magnitude

    local bestDistance =
        (Vector2.new(bestScreen.X,bestScreen.Y)-mouse).Magnitude

    -- Not enough improvement
    if bestDistance >
        currentDistance - settings.TargetSwitchMargin then

        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0

        return currentTarget

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Angle Check
    --------------------------------------------------

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local currentDirection =
        (currentPart.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Unit

    local bestDirection =
        (bestPart.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Unit

    local angle =
        math.deg(
            math.acos(
                math.clamp(
                    currentDirection:Dot(bestDirection),
                    -1,
                    1
                )
            )
        )

    if angle > settings.TargetSwitchAngle then

        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0

        return currentTarget

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Timer
    --------------------------------------------------

    if aimState.PendingTarget ~= bestTarget then

        aimState.PendingTarget = bestTarget
        aimState.PendingSince = tick()

        return currentTarget

    end

    if tick() - aimState.PendingSince >= settings.TargetSwitchDelay then

        aimState.PendingTarget = nil
        aimState.PendingSince = 0

        return bestTarget

    end

    return currentTarget

end

local function IsStickyTargetValid(player)

    if not player then
        return false
    end

    if player == Services.Players.LocalPlayer then
        return false
    end

    if not player.Character then
        return false
    end

    local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

    if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end

    local targetPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)

    if not targetPart then
        return false
    end

    if settings.TeamCheck then

        if player.Team == Services.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
            return false
        end

    end

    if settings.VisibleCheck then

        if not IsVisible(targetPart) then
            return false
        end

    end

    return true

end

local function updateAimAssist()

    if not aimState.KeyHeld then
        return
    end

    local target

    if settings.StickyAim then

        if not aimState.CurrentTarget
        or not IsStickyTargetValid(aimState.CurrentTarget) then

            aimState.CurrentTarget = GetClosestPlayerInFOV()

        end

        target = aimState.CurrentTarget

    else

        aimState.CurrentTarget = GetBestTarget(aimState.CurrentTarget)
        target = aimState.CurrentTarget

    end

    if not target then
        return
    end

    local character = target.Character
    if not character then
        return
    end

    local targetPart = character:FindFirstChild(settings.TargetPart)
    if not targetPart then
        return
    end

    if settings.AimMethod == "Camera" then

        updateCameraAim(targetPart)

    elseif settings.AimMethod == "Cursor" then

        updateCursorAim(targetPart)

    end

end

local function DrawCorners(left, right, top, bottom)

    local length =
        math.min(right - left, bottom - top) * 0.22

    local corners = {

        {
            Vector2.new(left, top),
            Vector2.new(left + length, top),

            Vector2.new(left, top),
            Vector2.new(left, top + length)
        },

        {
            Vector2.new(right - length, top),
            Vector2.new(right, top),

            Vector2.new(right, top),
            Vector2.new(right, top + length)
        },

        {
            Vector2.new(right, bottom - length),
            Vector2.new(right, bottom),

            Vector2.new(right - length, bottom),
            Vector2.new(right, bottom)
        },

        {
            Vector2.new(left, bottom - length),
            Vector2.new(left, bottom),

            Vector2.new(left, bottom),
            Vector2.new(left + length, bottom)
        }

    }

    local index = 1

    for _, corner in ipairs(corners) do

        targetCorners[index].From = corner[1]
        targetCorners[index].To = corner[2]

        targetCorners[index + 1].From = corner[3]
        targetCorners[index + 1].To = corner[4]

        targetCorners[index].Visible = true
        targetCorners[index + 1].Visible = true

        index += 2

    end

end

local function GetTargetBox(character)

    local cf, size = character:GetBoundingBox()

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    local points = {}

    for x = -1, 1, 2 do
        for y = -1, 1, 2 do
            for z = -1, 1, 2 do

                local worldPoint =
                    cf.Position +
                    Vector3.new(
                        size.X * x / 2,
                        size.Y * y / 2,
                        size.Z * z / 2
                    )

                local screenPoint, visible =
                    camera:WorldToViewportPoint(worldPoint)

                if visible then

                    table.insert(
                        points,
                        Vector2.new(
                            screenPoint.X,
                            screenPoint.Y
                        )
                    )

                end

            end
        end
    end

    if #points == 0 then
        return nil
    end

    local left = math.huge
    local right = -math.huge

    local top = math.huge
    local bottom = -math.huge

    for _, p in ipairs(points) do

        left = math.min(left, p.X)
        right = math.max(right, p.X)

        top = math.min(top, p.Y)
        bottom = math.max(bottom, p.Y)

    end

    return left, right, top, bottom

end

local function updateTargetIndicator()

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Disabled
    --------------------------------------------------

    if not settings.TargetIndicator then

        for _, line in ipairs(targetCorners) do
            line.Visible = false
        end

        return

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- No Target
    --------------------------------------------------

    if not aimState.CurrentTarget then

        for _, line in ipairs(targetCorners) do
            line.Visible = false
        end

        return

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Character Check
    --------------------------------------------------

    local character = aimState.CurrentTarget.Character

    if not character then

        for _, line in ipairs(targetCorners) do
            line.Visible = false
        end

        return

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Bounding Box
    --------------------------------------------------

    local left, right, top, bottom =
        GetTargetBox(character)

    if not left then

        for _, line in ipairs(targetCorners) do
            line.Visible = false
        end

        return

    end

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Draw
    --------------------------------------------------

    DrawCorners(left, right, top, bottom)

end

local function updateAimCircle()

    local mouse = Services.UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()

    aimCircle.Position = Vector2.new(mouse.X, mouse.Y)
    aimCircle.Radius = settings.AimFOV

    aimCircle.Visible = settings.DrawFOV

end

function visuals.UpdateSkeletonESP()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    if not camera then
        return
    end

    local pairsR15 = {
        {"Head", "UpperTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
        {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"}, {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
        {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"}, {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
        {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"}, {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
        {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"}, {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
        {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"}, {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
        {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"}, {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
    }
    local pairsR6 = {
        {"Head", "Torso"}, {"Torso", "Left Arm"},
        {"Torso", "Right Arm"}, {"Torso", "Left Leg"},
        {"Torso", "Right Leg"},
    }

    for trackedPlayer, bundle in pairs(
        visuals.SkeletonESP.Objects
    ) do

        if trackedPlayer.Parent ~= Services.Players then

            for _, line in ipairs(bundle) do
                line:Remove()
            end

            visuals.SkeletonESP.Objects[trackedPlayer] = nil

        end

    end

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == player then continue end
        local character = plr.Character
        local bundle = visuals.SkeletonESP.Objects[plr]

        local humanoid = character
            and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if not settings.SkeletonESP
        or not character
        or not humanoid
        or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
            if bundle then
                for _, line in ipairs(bundle) do line.Visible = false end
            end
            continue
        end

        local bonePairs = humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15
            and pairsR15
            or pairsR6

        if not bundle then
            bundle = {}
            for index = 1, #pairsR15 do
                local line = Drawing.new("Line")
                line.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                line.Thickness = 1.5
                line.Transparency = 1
                line.Visible = false
                bundle[index] = line
            end
            visuals.SkeletonESP.Objects[plr] = bundle
        end

        for _, line in ipairs(bundle) do line.Visible = false end
        for index, names in ipairs(bonePairs) do
            local first = character:FindFirstChild(names[1])
            local second = character:FindFirstChild(names[2])
            if first
            and first:IsA("BasePart")
            and second
            and second:IsA("BasePart") then
                local a, visibleA = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(first.Position)
                local b, visibleB = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(second.Position)
                if visibleA
                and visibleB
                and a.Z > 0
                and b.Z > 0
                and a.X == a.X
                and a.Y == a.Y
                and b.X == b.X
                and b.Y == b.Y then
                    local line = bundle[index]
                    line.From = Vector2.new(a.X, a.Y)
                    line.To = Vector2.new(b.X, b.Y)
                    line.Visible = true
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

function visuals.UpdateBoxESP()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr == player then continue end
        local character = plr.Character
        local box = visuals.BoxESP.Objects[plr]

        if not settings.BoxESP or not character then
            if box then box.Visible = false end
            continue
        end

        if not box then
            box = Drawing.new("Square")
            box.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
            box.Thickness = 1.5
            box.Transparency = 1
            box.Filled = false
            box.Visible = false
            visuals.BoxESP.Objects[plr] = box
        end

        local pivot, size = character:GetBoundingBox()
        local half = size * 0.5
        local minimum = Vector2.new(math.huge, math.huge)
        local maximum = Vector2.new(-math.huge, -math.huge)
        local visible = false

        for x = -1, 1, 2 do
            for y = -1, 1, 2 do
                for z = -1, 1, 2 do
                    local world = pivot:PointToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(
                        half.X * x, half.Y * y, half.Z * z
                    ))
                    local screen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(world)
                    if screen.Z > 0 then
                        visible = true
                        minimum = Vector2.new(
                            math.min(minimum.X, screen.X),
                            math.min(minimum.Y, screen.Y)
                        )
                        maximum = Vector2.new(
                            math.max(maximum.X, screen.X),
                            math.max(maximum.Y, screen.Y)
                        )
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if visible then
            box.Position = minimum
            box.Size = maximum - minimum
            box.Visible = true
        else
            box.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

local function updatePlayerESP()

    for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do

        if plr == player then
            continue
        end

        local character = plr.Character

        if not character then
            continue
        end

        local highlight =
            character:FindFirstChild("LunacyESP")

        if not visuals.PlayerESP.Enabled and not visuals.NearbyHighlight.Enabled then

            if highlight then
                highlight:Destroy()
            end

            continue
        end

        if not highlight then

            highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "LunacyESP"
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1,1,1)
            highlight.Parent = character

        end

        local rootPart =
            character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not root or not rootPart then
            continue
        end

        local distance =
            (root.Position-rootPart.Position).Magnitude

        if visuals.NearbyHighlight.Enabled then

            if distance > 75 then

                highlight:Destroy()

            elseif distance <= 25 then

                highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0,255,0)

            elseif distance <= 50 then

                highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,0)

            else

                highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)

            end

        else

            highlight.FillColor =
                Color3.fromRGB(255,0,0)

        end

    end

end

Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Normal Water
    --------------------------------------------------

    local waterLevel =
        workspace:FindFirstChild("WaterLevel")

    if waterLevel then

        waterPlatform.Position =
            Vector3.new(
                waterLevel.Position.X,
                waterLevel.Position.Y
                    + WATER_PLATFORM_OFFSET,
                waterLevel.Position.Z
            )

    end

    updateOneWayWaterCollision(
        waterPlatform,
        "Normal",
        waterLevel ~= nil
    )

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- Flash Flood
    --------------------------------------------------

    local structure =
        workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")

    local floodLevel

    if structure then

        floodLevel =
            structure:FindFirstChild("FloodLevel")

    end

    if floodLevel then

        floodPlatform.Position =
            Vector3.new(
                floodLevel.Position.X,
                floodLevel.Position.Y
                    + WATER_PLATFORM_OFFSET,
                floodLevel.Position.Z
            )

    end

    updateOneWayWaterCollision(
        floodPlatform,
        "Flood",
        floodLevel ~= nil
    )

    if settings.AntiFling2 ~= lastState.AntiFling2 then

        lastState.AntiFling2 = settings.AntiFling2

        if settings.AntiFling2 then

            RefreshCollisionParts()

        else

            RestoreCollisionParts()

            if settings.AntiEarthquake then
                Earthquake:SetEnabled(true)
            end

        end

    end

    updateAimCircle()

    updateAimAssist()

    updateTargetIndicator()

    updateTracers()

    updateNametags()

    updatePlayerESP()

    visuals.UpdateSkeletonESP()

    visuals.UpdateBoxESP()

    updateRadiusVisuals()

end)

local function createMovementTab()
    local page = pages["Movement"]

    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20)
    scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
    scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
    scroll.Active = true
    scroll.TopImage = ""
    scroll.BottomImage = ""
    scroll.MidImage = ""
    scroll.Parent = page

    Instance.new("UICorner", scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = scroll

    layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
            0,
            0,
            0,
            layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10
        )
    end)

    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    padding.Parent = scroll

	local speedLabel = Instance.new("Frame")
	speedLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 38)
	speedLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	speedLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	speedLabel.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", speedLabel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	local speedTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	speedTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	speedTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
	speedTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	speedTitle.Text = "Walk Speed (default: 16)"
	speedTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
	speedTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

	speedTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	speedTitle.Parent = speedLabel

	local speedValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")

	speedValue.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
	speedValue.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	speedValue.Text = "16"

	speedValue.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	speedValue.TextSize = 11
	speedValue.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	speedValue.Parent = speedLabel

	local speedRow = Instance.new("Frame")

	speedRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	speedRow.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", speedRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	local SPEED_MIN, SPEED_MAX = 8, 100

	local speedTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
	speedTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 6)
	speedTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0.5, -3)
	speedTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
	speedTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
	speedTrack.Parent = speedRow
	Instance.new("UICorner", speedTrack).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local speedFill = Instance.new("Frame")
	speedFill.Size = UDim2.new((currentSpeed - SPEED_MIN) / (SPEED_MAX - SPEED_MIN), 0, 1, 0)
	speedFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
	speedFill.BorderSizePixel = 0

			currentSpeed = math.floor(SPEED_MIN + relX * (SPEED_MAX - SPEED_MIN))

			speedKnob.Position = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 0.5, 0)

			end
		end
	end)

	floatTitle.TextSize = 11
	floatTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	floatTitle.Parent = floatLabel

	local floatValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	floatValue.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 1, 0)
	floatValue.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
	floatValue.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	floatValue.Text = "Off"
	floatValue.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
	floatValue.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	floatValue.TextSize = 11
	floatValue.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	floatValue.Parent = floatLabel

	local floatRow = Instance.new("Frame")
	floatRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 28)
	floatRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	floatRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	floatRow.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", floatRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	local FLOAT_MIN, FLOAT_MAX = 0, 100

	floatKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
	floatKnob.Parent = floatTrack
	Instance.new("UICorner", floatKnob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local draggingFloat = false

	floatKnob.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			draggingFloat = true
		end
	end)

	Services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			draggingFloat = false
		end
	end)

	Services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if draggingFloat and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			local trackPos = floatTrack.AbsolutePosition.X
			local trackWidth = floatTrack.AbsoluteSize.X
			local relX = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackPos) / trackWidth, 0, 1)

			floatHeight = math.floor(FLOAT_MIN + relX * (FLOAT_MAX - FLOAT_MIN))
			floatValue.Text = floatHeight == 0 and "Off" or tostring(floatHeight) .. " studs"

			floatFill.Size = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 1, 0)
			floatKnob.Position = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 0.5, 0)

			if humanoid then
				humanoid.HipHeight = floatHeight
			end
		end
	end)

    local noclipRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    noclipRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 38)
    noclipRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
    noclipRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    noclipRow.Parent = scroll
    Instance.new("UICorner", noclipRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local noclipLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    noclipLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0)
    noclipLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
    noclipLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    noclipLabel.Text = "Noclip"
    noclipLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160,160,180)
    noclipLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    noclipLabel.TextSize = 11
    noclipLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    noclipLabel.Parent = noclipRow

	Services.UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
	    if draggingCFrame and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
	        local trackPos = cframeSpeedTrack.AbsolutePosition.X
	        local trackWidth = cframeSpeedTrack.AbsoluteSize.X
	        local relX = math.clamp((input.Position.X - trackPos) / trackWidth, 0, 1)

	        settings.CFrameSpeedValue = math.floor(CFRAME_MIN + relX * (CFRAME_MAX - CFRAME_MIN))
	        cframeSpeedValue.Text = tostring(settings.CFrameSpeedValue)

	        cframeSpeedFill.Size = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 1, 0)
	        cframeSpeedKnob.Position = UDim2.new(relX, 0, 0.5, 0)
	    end
	end)

    local function doRespawn()

        local character = player.Character
        if not character then return end

        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not humanoid or not root then
            return
        end

        local savedCFrame

        if settings.RememberRespawnPosition then
            savedCFrame = root.CFrame
        end

        humanoid.Health = 0

        if settings.RememberRespawnPosition then

            task.spawn(function()

                local newCharacter = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local newRoot = newCharacter:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                task.wait(0.3)

                for i = 1,5 do
                    newRoot.CFrame = savedCFrame
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

            end)

        end

    end

    respawnBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(doRespawn)
    keybinds.Respawn.Callback = doRespawn

    keybinds.Fly.Callback = function()
        SetFly(not settings.Fly)
    end

        settings.Noclip = not settings.Noclip

        ApplyNoclip()

    end

    createActionButton(scroll, "Launch Rocket", function()

        local structure =
            workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")

        if not structure then
            return
        end

        local launchLand =
            structure:FindFirstChild("Launch Land")

        if not launchLand then

            game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Lunacy Hub - Rocket",
                Text = "Launch Land is not the current map.",
                Duration = 3
            })

            return
        end

        local bridgeDetector =
            launchLand:FindFirstChild("LoadingTower")
            and launchLand.LoadingTower:FindFirstChild("Console")
            and launchLand.LoadingTower.Console:FindFirstChild("ReleaseEntryBridge")
            and launchLand.LoadingTower.Console.ReleaseEntryBridge:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")

        local lowerDetector =
            launchLand:FindFirstChild("RocketStand")
            and launchLand.RocketStand:FindFirstChild("ConsoleLower")
            and launchLand.RocketStand.ConsoleLower:FindFirstChild("ReleaseButtonLower")
            and launchLand.RocketStand.ConsoleLower.ReleaseButtonLower:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")

        local upperDetector =
            launchLand:FindFirstChild("RocketStand")
            and launchLand.RocketStand:FindFirstChild("ConsoleUpper")
            and launchLand.RocketStand.ConsoleUpper.ReleaseButtonUpper:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")

        local ignitionDetector =
            launchLand:FindFirstChild("SPACESHIP!!")
            and launchLand["SPACESHIP!!"]:FindFirstChild("Shuttle")
            and launchLand["SPACESHIP!!"].Shuttle:FindFirstChild("IgnitionButton")
            and launchLand["SPACESHIP!!"].Shuttle.IgnitionButton:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector")

        for _, detector in ipairs({
            bridgeDetector,
            lowerDetector,
            upperDetector,
            ignitionDetector
        }) do

            if detector then
                task.spawn(function()

                    pcall(function()
                        fireclickdetector(detector)
                    end)

                end)
            end

        end

    end)
    
end



createMovementTab()
--================================================
-- DEFENSE TAB
--================================================
local function createDefenseTab()

    local page = pages["Defense"]
    local scroll = createScrollingPage(page)

    createToggle(
        scroll,
        "Anti Void",
        "AntiVoid",
        function()

            updateFallenPartsDestroyHeight()

        end
    )
    createToggle(
        scroll,
        "Anti Void Auto TP",
        "AntiVoidAutoTP"
    )
    createToggle(
        scroll,
        "Anti Fling (player collisions only)",
        "AntiFling",
        function(enabled)

            if enabled then
                AntiFling.LastSafeCFrame = root and root.CFrame or nil
                rebuildAntiFlingConstraints()
                setAntiFlingConstraintsEnabled(true)
            else
                destroyAntiFlingConstraints()
                AntiFling.RestorePlayerCollisions()
                AntiFling.LastSafeCFrame = nil
            end

        end
    )
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Fling (player + map collisions)", "AntiFling2")
    -- Regular mode: known bang animations only.
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Bang", "AntiBangAnimations")
    -- Stricter mode: known animations plus behind/contact behavior.
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Bang (stricter)", "AntiBang")
    createToggle(scroll, "Velocity Guard", "VelocityGuard")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Teleport", "AntiTeleport")
    createToggle(scroll, "Water Walker", "WaterWalker")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Sit", "AntiSit")
    createToggle(scroll, "Auto Eat", "AutoEat")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Avalanche", "AntiAvalanche")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Tsunami", "AntiTsunami")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Earthquake", "AntiEarthquake")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Volcano", "AntiVolcano")
    createToggle(scroll, "Anti Meteor", "AntiMeteor")
    createToggle(scroll, "No Fall Damage", "NoFallDamage")
    createToggle(
        scroll,
        "Max Simulation Radius",
        "MaxSimulationRadius",
        function(enabled)

            if settings._SimulationRadiusHeartbeat then

                settings._SimulationRadiusHeartbeat:Disconnect()
                settings._SimulationRadiusHeartbeat = nil

            end

            if not enabled then
                return
            end

            if sethiddenproperty then

                pcall(sethiddenproperty, player, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
                pcall(sethiddenproperty, player, "MaximumSimulationRadius", math.huge)

            elseif set_simulation_radius then

                pcall(set_simulation_radius, math.huge, math.huge)

            end

            settings._SimulationRadiusHeartbeat =
                Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

                    if sethiddenproperty then

                        pcall(sethiddenproperty, player, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
                        pcall(sethiddenproperty, player, "MaximumSimulationRadius", math.huge)

                    elseif set_simulation_radius then

                        pcall(set_simulation_radius, math.huge, math.huge)

                    end

                end)

        end
    )

    createSlider(scroll, "Avalanche Radius", "AvalancheRadius", 5, 100, 20)
    createSlider(scroll, "Tsunami Radius", "TsunamiRadius", 5, 100, 50)
    createSlider(scroll, "Volcano Radius", "VolcanoRadius", 5, 100, 60)
    createSlider(scroll, "Meteor Radius", "MeteorRadius", 5, 100, 60)

    createToggle(scroll, "Protection Escape", "ProtectionEscape")

    local escapeModeRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    escapeModeRow.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,40)
    escapeModeRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    escapeModeRow.Parent = scroll

    local escapeModeLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    escapeModeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,0,1,0)
    escapeModeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    escapeModeLabel.Text = "Escape Mode"
    escapeModeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    escapeModeLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    escapeModeLabel.TextSize = 13
    escapeModeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    escapeModeLabel.Parent = escapeModeRow

    local escapeModeButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
    escapeModeButton.Size = UDim2.new(0,120,0,24)
    escapeModeButton.Position = UDim2.new(1,-120,0.5,-12)
    escapeModeButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40,40,50)
    escapeModeButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240,240,240)
    escapeModeButton.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    escapeModeButton.TextSize = 10
    escapeModeButton.Text = "▾ " .. settings.ProtectionEscapeMode
    escapeModeButton.Parent = escapeModeRow

    local escapeDelayRow

    Instance.new("UICorner", escapeModeButton).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,4)

    escapeModeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        showDropdown(
            escapeModeButton,
            {
                "Round End",
                "Timer",
            },
            function(selected)

                settings.ProtectionEscapeMode = selected

                escapeModeButton.Text = "▾ " .. selected

                escapeDelayRow.Visible = (selected == "Timer")

            end
        )

    end)

    escapeDelayRow = createSlider(
        scroll,
        "Escape Delay",
        "ProtectionEscapeDelay",
        5,
        60,
        10
    )

    escapeDelayRow.Visible =
        settings.ProtectionEscapeMode == "Timer"

end

createDefenseTab()
--================================================
-- TARGET TAB
--================================================
local TargetRuntime = {
    AutoTeleportActive = false,
    FlingTouchActive = false,
    FlingTouchBusy = false,
    LastTargetVelocity = nil,
    SmoothedAcceleration = Vector3.zero,
    LastPredictTime = nil,
    LastHorizontalVelocity = nil,
    CircleConfidence = 0,
    TPOnDeathActive = false,
    TPDeathHandled = false,
    FlingSpinActive = false,
    FlingSpinBusy = false,
    SpinDeathPinned = false,
    PredictFlingPinned = false,
    PredictFlingSettledAt = nil,
    TouchFlingGeneration = 0,
    TouchFlingPulseStep = 0,
    FlingAuraActive = false,
}

local function createTargetSelector()
    local page = pages["Target"]
	local scroll = createScrollingPage(page)

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Target State
    -------------------------------------------------

    local spectating = false
    local highlightActive = false

    local followActive = false
    local lookActive = false
    local orbitActive = false

    local targetHighlight = nil
    local highlightCharacterConnection = nil
    local spectateCharacterConnection = nil

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Button References
    -------------------------------------------------

    local spectateBtn
    local flingTouchBtn

    local teleportBtn
    local followBtn

    local lookBtn
    local orbitBtn

    local autoTeleportBtn
    local highlightBtn

    local tpDeathBtn
    local flingSpinBtn

    -------------------------------------------------

    local headerSection = createSection(scroll, 22)

	local selectedLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	selectedLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    selectedLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	selectedLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedLabel.Text = "Selected: None"
	selectedLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
	selectedLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	selectedLabel.TextSize = 12
	selectedLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	selectedLabel.Parent = headerSection

    local function hasTarget()

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        return target
            and target ~= player
            and target.Parent == Services.Players

    end

    local function selectTarget(plr)

        if not plr
        or plr.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        if plr == player then

            game.StarterGui:SetCore(
                "SendNotification",
                {
                    Title = "Lunacy Hub - Target",
                    Text = "You cannot target yourself.",
                    Duration = 3
                }
            )

            return
        end

        settings.TargetPlayer =
            plr

        selectedLabel.Text =
            "Selected: "
            .. plr.DisplayName
            .. " (@"
            .. plr.Name
            .. ")"

    end

    local function stopSpectate()

        spectating = false
        settings.Spectating = false

        if spectateCharacterConnection then

            spectateCharacterConnection:Disconnect()

            spectateCharacterConnection = nil

        end

        if humanoid
        and workspace.CurrentCamera then

            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
                humanoid

        end

        spectateBtn.Text =
            "Spectate"

        spectateBtn:SetAttribute(
            "Active",
            false
        )

    end

    local function startSpectate(target)

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

            return false

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Clear old respawn watcher
        ----------------------------------

        if spectateCharacterConnection then

            spectateCharacterConnection:Disconnect()

            spectateCharacterConnection = nil

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Attach to current character
        ----------------------------------

        local targetCharacter =
            target.Character

        if targetCharacter then

            local targetHumanoid =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                    "Humanoid"
                )

            if targetHumanoid
            and workspace.CurrentCamera then

                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
                    targetHumanoid

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Reattach after every respawn
        ----------------------------------

        spectateCharacterConnection =
            target.CharacterAdded:Connect(
                function(newCharacter)

                    if not spectating then
                        return
                    end

                    if settings.TargetPlayer
                        ~= target then

                        return

                    end

                    local newHumanoid =
                        newCharacter:WaitForChild(
                            "Humanoid",
                            10
                        )

                    if not newHumanoid then
                        return
                    end

                    if not spectating then
                        return
                    end

                    if settings.TargetPlayer
                        ~= target then

                        return

                    end

                    if workspace.CurrentCamera then

                        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
                            newHumanoid

                    end

                end
            )

        return true

    end

    local function clearTargetHighlight()

        if targetHighlight then

            targetHighlight:Destroy()
            targetHighlight = nil

        end

    end

    local function disconnectHighlightCharacterConnection()

        if highlightCharacterConnection then

            highlightCharacterConnection:Disconnect()
            highlightCharacterConnection = nil

        end

    end

    local function applyTargetHighlight()

        clearTargetHighlight()

        if not highlightActive then
            return
        end

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        local targetCharacter =
            target.Character

        if not targetCharacter then
            return
        end

        local highlight =
            Instance.new("Highlight")

        highlight.Name =
            "LunacyTargetHighlight"

        highlight.Adornee =
            targetCharacter

        highlight.FillColor =
            Color3.fromRGB(
                220,
                50,
                50
            )

        highlight.FillTransparency =
            0.65

        highlight.OutlineColor =
            Color3.fromRGB(
                255,
                80,
                80
            )

        highlight.OutlineTransparency =
            0

        highlight.DepthMode =
            Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop

        highlight.Parent =
            targetCharacter

        targetHighlight =
            highlight

    end

    local function bindTargetHighlight()

        disconnectHighlightCharacterConnection()
        clearTargetHighlight()

        if not highlightActive then
            return
        end

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Current character
        ----------------------------------

        applyTargetHighlight()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Future respawns
        ----------------------------------

        highlightCharacterConnection =
            target.CharacterAdded:Connect(
                function(newCharacter)

                    if not highlightActive then
                        return
                    end

                    if settings.TargetPlayer
                        ~= target then
                        return
                    end

                    newCharacter:WaitForChild(
                        "HumanoidRootPart",
                        10
                    )

                    if not highlightActive then
                        return
                    end

                    if settings.TargetPlayer
                        ~= target then
                        return
                    end

                    applyTargetHighlight()

                end
            )

    end

    local infoSection = createSection(scroll, 135)

	-- Target Info Panel
	local infoPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
    infoPanel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    infoPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	infoPanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
	infoPanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	infoPanel.Parent = infoSection
	Instance.new("UICorner", infoPanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	originalTransparencies[infoPanel] = {bg = infoPanel.BackgroundTransparency}

	local function makeInfoLine(labelText, y)
		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 0, 18)
		lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.Text = labelText
		lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 150)
		lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		lbl.TextSize = 11
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		lbl.Parent = infoPanel
		originalTransparencies[lbl] = {bg = 1, text = 0}

		local val = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		val.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -10, 0, 18)
		val.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, y)
		val.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		val.Text = "—"
		val.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
		val.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		val.TextSize = 11
		val.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		val.Parent = infoPanel
		originalTransparencies[val] = {bg = 1, text = 0}

		return val
	end

	local infoName = makeInfoLine("Name", 8)
	local infoHealth = makeInfoLine("Health", 26)
	local infoSpeed = makeInfoLine("Speed", 44)
	local infoDist = makeInfoLine("Distance", 62)
    local infoJump = makeInfoLine("JumpPower", 80)
    local infoState = makeInfoLine("State", 98)
    local infoTool = makeInfoLine("Tool", 116)

    local function clearTargetInfo()

        infoName.Text = "—"
        infoHealth.Text = "—"
        infoSpeed.Text = "—"
        infoDist.Text = "—"
        infoJump.Text = "—"
        infoState.Text = "—"
        infoTool.Text = "—"

    end


	-- Update info panel every heartbeat
    task.spawn(function()

        while task.wait(0.1) do

            local target =
                settings.TargetPlayer

            ----------------------------------
            -- No selected target
            ----------------------------------

            if not target
            or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

                clearTargetInfo()
                continue

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Keep permanent player info
            -- through death / respawn.
            ----------------------------------

            infoName.Text =
                target.Name

            local targetCharacter =
                target.Character

            ----------------------------------
            -- Target is respawning
            ----------------------------------

            if not targetCharacter then

                infoHealth.Text = "—"
                infoSpeed.Text = "—"
                infoDist.Text = "—"
                infoJump.Text = "—"
                infoState.Text = "Respawning"
                infoTool.Text = "—"

                continue

            end

            local targetHum =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                    "Humanoid"
                )

            local targetRoot =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                    "HumanoidRootPart"
                )

            ----------------------------------
            -- Humanoid info
            ----------------------------------

            if targetHum then

                infoHealth.Text =
                    math.floor(targetHum.Health)
                    .. "/"
                    .. math.floor(targetHum.MaxHealth)

                infoSpeed.Text =
                    math.floor(targetHum.WalkSpeed)
                    .. " ws"

                infoJump.Text =
                    math.floor(targetHum.JumpPower)

                local state =
                    targetHum:GetState()

                local velocity =
                    targetRoot
                    and targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    or Vector3.zero

                local stateText

                ----------------------------------
                -- Explicit states first
                ----------------------------------

                if state ==
                    Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming then

                    stateText = "Swimming"

                elseif state ==
                    Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing then

                    stateText = "Climbing"

                elseif targetHum.Health <= 0 then

                    stateText = "Dead"

                elseif velocity.Y > 2 then

                    stateText = "Jumping"

                elseif velocity.Y < -2 then

                    stateText = "Falling"

                elseif velocity.Magnitude < 1 then

                    stateText = "Idle"

                else

                    stateText = "Running"

                end

                infoState.Text =
                    stateText

            else

                infoHealth.Text = "—"
                infoSpeed.Text = "—"
                infoJump.Text = "—"
                infoState.Text = "Respawning"

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Tool
            ----------------------------------

            local equippedTool =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                    "Tool"
                )

            infoTool.Text =
                equippedTool
                and equippedTool.Name
                or "None"

            ----------------------------------
            -- Distance
            ----------------------------------

            if targetRoot
            and root then

                local distance =
                    math.floor(
                        (
                            root.Position
                            - targetRoot.Position
                        ).Magnitude
                    )

                infoDist.Text =
                    distance .. " studs"

            else

                infoDist.Text = "—"

            end

        end

    end)

    spectateBtn, flingTouchBtn =
        createButtonPair(scroll, "Spectate", "Fling (Touch)")

    teleportBtn, followBtn =
        createButtonPair(
            scroll,
            "Teleport To",
            "Toggle Follow"
        )

	spectateBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not spectating then
			Services.TweenService:Create(spectateBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	spectateBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not spectating then
			Services.TweenService:Create(spectateBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

    spectateBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Turn Spectate off
        ----------------------------------

        if spectating then

            stopSpectate()

            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Turn Spectate on
        ----------------------------------

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        spectating = true
        settings.Spectating = true

        spectateBtn.Text =
            "Stop Spectating"

        spectateBtn:SetAttribute(
            "Active",
            true
        )

        startSpectate(
            target
        )

    end)

    flingTouchBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive then
            Services.TweenService:Create(flingTouchBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    flingTouchBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive then
            Services.TweenService:Create(flingTouchBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    flingTouchBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive =
            not TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive

        if TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive then

            followActive = false
            settings.Following = false

            TargetRuntime.FlingSpinActive =
                false

            TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy =
                false

            followBtn:SetAttribute(
                "Active",
                false
            )

            followBtn.Text =
                "Toggle Follow"

            if flingSpinBtn then

                flingSpinBtn:SetAttribute(
                    "Active",
                    false
                )

                flingSpinBtn.Text =
                    "Fling (Spin)"

            end

            if root
            and root.Parent then

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

        end

        flingTouchBtn:SetAttribute(
            "Active",
            TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive
        )

        flingTouchBtn.Text =
            TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive
            and "Flinging"
            or "Fling (Touch)"

    end)

    teleportBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

        local target = settings.TargetPlayer

        if target and target.Character and root then
            local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if targetRoot then
                bypassAntiTeleport(function()
                    root.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    local followActive = false

	followBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not followActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(followBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	followBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not followActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(followBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

    followBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

        followActive =
            not followActive

        if followActive then

            ----------------------------------
            -- Disable Fling Touch
            ----------------------------------

            TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive = false

            flingTouchBtn:SetAttribute(
                "Active",
                false
            )

            flingTouchBtn.Text =
                "Fling (Touch)"

        end

        settings.Following =
            followActive

        followBtn:SetAttribute(
            "Active",
            followActive
        )

        followBtn.Text =
            followActive
            and "Following"
            or "Toggle Follow"

    end)

    -- Look At / Orbit

    lookBtn, orbitBtn =
        createButtonPair(
            scroll,
            "Look At",
            "Orbit"
        )

	lookBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not lookActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(lookBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	lookBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not lookActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(lookBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	lookBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

		lookActive = not lookActive
        lookBtn:SetAttribute("Active", lookActive)
		lookBtn.Text = lookActive and "Looking At" or "Look At"

		if lookActive then
			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if not lookActive then
					connection:Disconnect()
					return
				end
				local target = settings.TargetPlayer
				if target and target.Character and root then
					local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if targetRoot then
						local targetHead = target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
						local lookTarget = targetHead and targetHead.Position or targetRoot.Position
						workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
							workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position,
							lookTarget
						)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

	orbitBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not orbitActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(orbitBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	orbitBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not orbitActive then
			Services.TweenService:Create(orbitBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
				BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
			}):Play()
		end
	end)

	orbitBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if not hasTarget() then
            return
        end

		orbitActive = not orbitActive
		settings.Orbiting = orbitActive
        orbitBtn:SetAttribute("Active", orbitActive)
		orbitBtn.Text = orbitActive and "Orbiting" or "Orbit"

		if orbitActive then
			local horizontalAngle = 0
			local verticalAngle = 0
			local orbitRadius = 10
			local orbitSpeed = 2
			local connection
			connection = Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				if not orbitActive then
					connection:Disconnect()
					return
				end
				local target = settings.TargetPlayer
				if target and target.Character then
					local targetRoot = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if targetRoot and root then
						horizontalAngle = horizontalAngle + (settings.OrbitSpeed or 2) * dt
                        verticalAngle = verticalAngle + ((settings.OrbitSpeed or 2) * 0.7) * dt

						local x = targetRoot.Position.X + orbitRadius * math.cos(verticalAngle) * math.cos(horizontalAngle)
						local y = targetRoot.Position.Y + orbitRadius * math.sin(verticalAngle)
						local z = targetRoot.Position.Z + orbitRadius * math.cos(verticalAngle) * math.sin(horizontalAngle)

						root.CFrame = CFrame.new(x, y, z)
						root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)

    -- Auto TP / highlight
    autoTeleportBtn, highlightBtn =
        createButtonPair(scroll, "Auto Teleport", "Highlight")

        autoTeleportBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
	        if not TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive then
		        Services.TweenService:Create(autoTeleportBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
			        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
		        }):Play()
	        end
        end)

        autoTeleportBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
	        if not TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive then
		        Services.TweenService:Create(autoTeleportBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
			        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
		        }):Play()
	        end
        end)

        autoTeleportBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

            if not hasTarget() then
                return
            end

	        TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive = not TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive

            autoTeleportBtn:SetAttribute("Active", TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive)

		        TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive and "Auto TP ON" or "Auto Teleport"

	        if not highlightActive then

			        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
		        }):Play()
	        end
        end)

            if not hasTarget() then
                return

	        highlightActive = not highlightActive

            highlightBtn:SetAttribute("Active", highlightActive)

	        highlightBtn.Text =
	        	highlightActive and "Highlighted" or "Highlight"

            if highlightActive then

                bindTargetHighlight()

            else

                disconnectHighlightCharacterConnection()
                clearTargetHighlight()

            end
        end)

    local function resetTargetFeatures()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Spectate
        ----------------------------------

        stopSpectate()

        ----------------------------------
        -- State
        ----------------------------------

        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive = false
        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchBusy = false

        followActive = false
        lookActive = false
        orbitActive = false
        TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive = false
        highlightActive = false

        TargetRuntime.TPOnDeathActive = false
        TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled = false

        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinActive = false
        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy = false

        if root
        and root.Parent then

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end

        TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
            false

        TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned =
            false

        TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
            nil

        ----------------------------------
        -- Settings
        ----------------------------------

        settings.Following = false
        settings.Orbiting = false

        ----------------------------------
        -- Camera
        ----------------------------------

        if humanoid
        and workspace.CurrentCamera then

            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject =
                humanoid

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Highlight
        ----------------------------------

        disconnectHighlightCharacterConnection()
        clearTargetHighlight()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Buttons
        ----------------------------------

        spectateBtn.Text =
            "Spectate"

        flingTouchBtn.Text =
            "Fling (Touch)"

        followBtn.Text =
            "Toggle Follow"

        lookBtn.Text =
            "Look At"

        orbitBtn.Text =
            "Orbit"

        autoTeleportBtn.Text =
            "Auto Teleport"

        highlightBtn.Text =
            "Highlight"

        tpDeathBtn.Text =
            "TP on Death"

        flingSpinBtn.Text =
            "Fling (Spin)"

        local buttons = {
            spectateBtn,
            flingTouchBtn,
            followBtn,
            lookBtn,
            orbitBtn,
            autoTeleportBtn,
            highlightBtn,
            tpDeathBtn,
            flingSpinBtn,
        }

        for _, button in ipairs(buttons) do

            button:SetAttribute(
                "Active",
                false
            )

            button.BackgroundColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    40,
                    40,
                    50
                )

        end

    end

	local function refreshPlayers(filter)
		filter = (filter or ""):lower()
		for _, child in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == "PlayerButton" then
				child:Destroy()
			end
		end

		for _, plr in ipairs(Services.Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if plr ~= player then
                local username =
                    plr.Name:lower()

                local displayName =
                    plr.DisplayName:lower()

                if filter == ""
                or username:find(filter, 1, true)
                or displayName:find(filter, 1, true) then
					local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
					btn.Name = "PlayerButton"
					btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
					btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 52)
					btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
					btn.Text =
                        plr.DisplayName
                        .. " (@"
                        .. plr.Name
                        .. ")"
					btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
					btn.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
					btn.TextSize = 12
					btn.Parent = scrollFrame

					Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

					originalTransparencies[btn] = {
						bg = btn.BackgroundTransparency,
						text = btn.TextTransparency
					}

                    if settings.TargetPlayer == plr then
                        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 30, 30)
                        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    end

					btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
						if settings.TargetPlayer ~= plr then
							Services.TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
								BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 80)
							}):Play()
						end
					end)

					btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
						if settings.TargetPlayer ~= plr then
							Services.TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
								BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 52)
							}):Play()
						end
					end)

btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

    if settings.TargetPlayer == plr then

        resetTargetFeatures()

        settings.TargetPlayer =
            nil

        selectedLabel.Text =
            "Selected: None"

        clearTargetInfo()

        btn.BackgroundColor3 =
            Color3.fromRGB(
                40,
                40,
                52
            )

        btn.TextColor3 =
            Color3.fromRGB(
                230,
                230,
                230
            )

        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 52)
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
    else

        for _, b in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if b.Name == "PlayerButton" then
                b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 52)
                b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
            end
        end

        selectTarget(plr)

        Services.TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.05), {
    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 30, 30)
}):Play()
        btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

    end
end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	refreshPlayers()

	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		refreshPlayers(searchBox.Text)
	end)
