    -------------------------------------------------
    -- REFRESH WHITELIST DISPLAY
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function refreshWhitelist()

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Remove old generated rows
        -------------------------------------------------

        for _, child in ipairs(
            whitelistList:GetChildren()
        ) do

            if child:IsA("Frame") then
                child:Destroy()
            end

        end


        local whitelist =
            settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist
            or {}


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Empty state
        -------------------------------------------------

        if #whitelist == 0 then

            local emptyLabel =
                Instance.new("Frame")

            emptyLabel.Size =
                UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)

            emptyLabel.BackgroundTransparency =
                1

            emptyLabel.Parent =
                whitelistList


            local text =
                Instance.new("TextLabel")

            text.Size =
                UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

            text.BackgroundTransparency =
                1

            text.Text =
                "No whitelisted users"

            text.TextColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    140,
                    140,
                    155
                )

            text.Font =
                Enum.Font.Gotham

            text.TextSize =
                11

            text.TextXAlignment =
                Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

            text.Parent =
                emptyLabel

            return

        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- USER ROWS
        -------------------------------------------------

        for _, username in ipairs(
            whitelist
        ) do

            local row =
                Instance.new("Frame")

            row.Size =
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    0,
                    0,
                    30
                )

            row.BackgroundColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    30,
                    30,
                    38
                )

            row.BorderSizePixel =
                0

            row.Parent =
                whitelistList


            Instance.new(
                "UICorner",
                row
            ).CornerRadius =
                UDim.new(
                    0,
                    6
                )


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- USERNAME
            -------------------------------------------------

            local nameLabel =
                Instance.new("TextLabel")

            nameLabel.Size =
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -78,
                    1,
                    0
                )

            nameLabel.Position =
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    8,
                    0,
                    0
                )

            nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency =
                1

            nameLabel.Text =
                tostring(username)

            nameLabel.TextColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    230,
                    230,
                    230
                )

            nameLabel.Font =
                Enum.Font.Gotham

            nameLabel.TextSize =
                11

            nameLabel.TextXAlignment =
                Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

            nameLabel.Parent =
                row


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- REMOVE BUTTON
            -------------------------------------------------

            local removeButton =
                Instance.new("TextButton")

            removeButton.Size =
                UDim2.new(
                    0,
                    64,
                    0,
                    22
                )

            removeButton.Position =
                UDim2.new(
                    1,
                    -68,
                    0.5,
                    -11
                )

            removeButton.BackgroundColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    55,
                    55,
                    70
                )

            removeButton.BorderSizePixel =
                0

            removeButton.Text =
                "Remove"

            removeButton.TextColor3 =
                Color3.fromRGB(
                    240,
                    240,
                    240
                )

            removeButton.Font =
                Enum.Font.GothamBold

            removeButton.TextSize =
                10

            removeButton.Parent =
                row


            Instance.new(
                "UICorner",
                removeButton
            ).CornerRadius =
                UDim.new(
                    0,
                    5
                )


            removeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

                if TargetRuntime.RemoveEveryoneWhitelist then

                    TargetRuntime.RemoveEveryoneWhitelist(
                        username
                    )

                else

                    -------------------------------------------------
                    -- Fallback if runtime function isn't available
                    -------------------------------------------------

                    for index =
                        #settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist,
                        1,
                        -1 do

                        if settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist[index]
                            == username then

                            table.remove(
                                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist,
                                index
                            )

                            break

                        end

                    end

                end

                refreshWhitelist()

            end)

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- ADD USER
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function addWhitelistUser()

        local username =
            usernameBox.Text

        username =
            string.gsub(
                username,
                "^%s*(.-)%s*$",
                "%1"
            )

        if username == "" then
            return
        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Resolve exact names, display names, and a
        -- unique partial match from the current server.
        -------------------------------------------------

        local loweredInput =
            string.lower(username)

        local exactPlayer
        local partialMatches = {}

        for _, candidate in ipairs(
            Services.Players:GetPlayers()
        ) do

            if candidate ~= player then

                local loweredName =
                    string.lower(candidate.Name)

                local loweredDisplayName =
                    string.lower(candidate.DisplayName)

                if loweredName == loweredInput
                or loweredDisplayName == loweredInput then

                    exactPlayer = candidate
                    break

                elseif loweredName:sub(1, #loweredInput)
                    == loweredInput
                or loweredDisplayName:sub(1, #loweredInput)
                    == loweredInput then

                    table.insert(
                        partialMatches,
                        candidate
                    )

                end

            end

        end

        if exactPlayer then

            username = exactPlayer.Name

        elseif #partialMatches == 1 then

            username = partialMatches[1].Name

        elseif #partialMatches > 1 then

            whitelistTitle.Text =
                "Fling All Whitelist — multiple matches"

            return

        end

        if TargetRuntime.AddEveryoneWhitelist then

            local added =
                TargetRuntime.AddEveryoneWhitelist(
                username
            )

            if not added then

                whitelistTitle.Text =
                    "Fling All Whitelist — already added"

                return

            end

        else

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Basic fallback
            -------------------------------------------------

            local normalized =
                string.lower(
                    username
                )

            local alreadyExists =
                false

            for _, existing in ipairs(
                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist
            ) do

                if string.lower(existing)
                    == normalized then

                    alreadyExists =
                        true

                    break

                end

            end

            if not alreadyExists then

                table.insert(
                    settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist,
                    normalized
                )

            end

        end

        usernameBox.Text =
            ""

        whitelistTitle.Text =
            "Fling All Whitelist"

        refreshWhitelist()

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- ADD BUTTON
    -------------------------------------------------

    addButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        addWhitelistUser()

    end)


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- PRESS ENTER TO ADD
    -------------------------------------------------

    usernameBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)

        if enterPressed then

            addWhitelistUser()

        end

    end)

    clearButton.TextColor3 =
        Color3.fromRGB(
            230,
            230,
            230
        )

    clearButton.Font =
        Enum.Font.GothamBold

    clearButton.TextSize =
        11

    clearButton.Parent =
        whitelistSection


    Instance.new(
        "UICorner",
        clearButton
    ).CornerRadius =
        UDim.new(
            0,
            6
        )


    clearButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

        if TargetRuntime.ClearEveryoneWhitelist then

            TargetRuntime.ClearEveryoneWhitelist()

        else

            settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist =
                {}

        end

        refreshWhitelist()

    end)


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- INITIAL DRAW
    -------------------------------------------------

    refreshWhitelist()

end

local function createPlayerTab()
	local page = pages["Player"]

	local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20)
	scroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
	scroll.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 32)
	scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
	scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 6
	scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
	scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	scroll.ScrollingEnabled = true
	scroll.Active = true
	scroll.TopImage = ""
	scroll.BottomImage = ""
	scroll.MidImage = ""
	scroll.Parent = page

    task.defer(function()
        scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, scroll.AbsoluteCanvasSize.Y)
    end)

	Instance.new("UICorner", scroll).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
	layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	layout.Parent = scroll

    layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
            0,
            0,
            0,
            layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10
        )
    end)

	local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
	padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
	padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
	padding.Parent = scroll

	local pfpRow = Instance.new("Frame")
	pfpRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 70)
	pfpRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	pfpRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
	pfpRow.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", pfpRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	local thumbType = Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot
	local thumbSize = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100
	local content = Services.Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(player.UserId, thumbType, thumbSize)

	local pfpImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	pfpImage.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 54)
	pfpImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -27)
	pfpImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pfpImage.Image = content
	pfpImage.Parent = pfpRow
	Instance.new("UICorner", pfpImage).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

	local pfpName = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	pfpName.Size = UDim2.new(1, -75, 0, 22)
	pfpName.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 12)
	pfpName.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pfpName.Text = "@" .. player.Name
	pfpName.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	pfpName.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	pfpName.TextSize = 13
	pfpName.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	pfpName.Parent = pfpRow

	local pfpDisplay = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	pfpDisplay.Size = UDim2.new(1, -75, 0, 18)
	pfpDisplay.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 36)
	pfpDisplay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pfpDisplay.Text = player.DisplayName
	pfpDisplay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
	pfpDisplay.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	pfpDisplay.TextSize = 11
	pfpDisplay.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	pfpDisplay.Parent = pfpRow

	local function makeInfoRow(labelText, valueText)
		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 38)
		row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.Parent = scroll

		Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

		local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		label.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
		label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 0)
		label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		label.Text = labelText
		label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
		label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

		label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		value.Text = valueText
		value.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
		value.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
		value.TextSize = 11
		value.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		value.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		value.Parent = row

		return value
	end

	makeInfoRow("Username", "@" .. player.Name)
	makeInfoRow("Display Name", player.DisplayName)
	makeInfoRow("User ID", tostring(player.UserId))
	makeInfoRow("Account Age", tostring(player.AccountAge) .. "d")
	makeInfoRow("Rank", roles[player.Name] or "User")
	local coordsVal = makeInfoRow("Position", "0, 0, 0")

	local healthVal = makeInfoRow("Health", "...")
	local pingVal = makeInfoRow("Ping", "...")

	local spacer = Instance.new("Frame")
	spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
	spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	spacer.Parent = scroll

	local rejoinBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	rejoinBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 38)
	rejoinBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	rejoinBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	rejoinBtn.Text = "Rejoin Server"
	rejoinBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
	rejoinBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	rejoinBtn.TextSize = 12
	rejoinBtn.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", rejoinBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	createButtonAnimation(
		rejoinBtn,
		Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60),
		Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38),
		Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 28)
	)

	rejoinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
			Title = "Lunacy Hub",
			Text = "Rejoining server... Remember to re-inject the script after rejoin!",
			Duration = 5
		})
		task.wait(1)
		local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
		local ok = pcall(function()
			TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, player)
		end)
		if not ok then
			pcall(function()
				TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, player)
			end)
		end
	end)

	local serverHopBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	serverHopBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 38)
	serverHopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	serverHopBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
	serverHopBtn.Text = "Server Hop"
	serverHopBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
	serverHopBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	serverHopBtn.TextSize = 12
	serverHopBtn.Parent = scroll
	Instance.new("UICorner", serverHopBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

	createButtonAnimation(
		serverHopBtn,
		Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60),
		Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38),
		Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 28)
	)

	serverHopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local placeId = game.PlaceId
		local currentJobId = game.JobId

		local success, result = pcall(function()
			return Services.HttpService:JSONDecode(
				game:HttpGet(
					"https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" ..
					placeId ..
					"/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"
				)
			)
		end)

		if success and result and result.data then
			local servers = {}

			for _, server in ipairs(result.data) do
				if server.id ~= currentJobId and server.playing < server.maxPlayers then
					table.insert(servers, server.id)
				end
			end

			if #servers > 0 then
				local randomServer = servers[math.random(1, #servers)]

				game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Lunacy Hub",
					Text = "Teleporting to another server...",
					Duration = 3
				})

				Services.TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, randomServer, player)
			else
				game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
					Title = "Lunacy Hub",
					Text = "No available servers found.",
					Duration = 3
				})
			end
		else
			game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
				Title = "Lunacy Hub",
				Text = "Failed to fetch servers.",
				Duration = 3
			})
		end
	end)

    local stats = game:GetService("Stats")
    local serverStats = stats.Network.ServerStatsItem
	local pingStat = serverStats:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.1) do
            if humanoid then
                healthVal.Text =
                    math.floor(humanoid.Health) .. " / " .. math.floor(humanoid.MaxHealth)
            end

            if root then
                local pos = root.Position
                coordsVal.Text =
                    math.floor(pos.X) .. ", " ..
                    math.floor(pos.Y) .. ", " ..
                    math.floor(pos.Z)
            end

            if pingStat then
                pingVal.Text = math.floor(pingStat:GetValue()) .. " ms"
            end
        end
    end)

end

local function createTeleportsTab()

    local page = pages["Teleports"]
    local scroll = createScrollingPage(page)

    local safeActive = false
    local lastPosition = nil

    createActionButton(scroll, "Island", function()

        if root then

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(-130,48,10)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

            end)

        end

    end)

    createActionButton(scroll, "Tower", function()

        if root then

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame = CFrame.new(-243,194,292)
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

            end)

        end

    end)

    -- Safe toggle button
local row = Instance.new("Frame")
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
row.Parent = scroll

local safeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
safeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
safeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
safeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
safeBtn.Text = "Safe (Off)"
safeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240)
safeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
safeBtn.TextSize = 12
safeBtn.Parent = row

Instance.new("UICorner", safeBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    safeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        if not safeActive then
            Services.TweenService:Create(safeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    safeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        if not safeActive then
            Services.TweenService:Create(safeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    safeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        safeActive = not safeActive

        if safeActive then
            lastPosition = root and root.CFrame
            if root then
                bypassAntiTeleport(function()
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(1000, 195, 380)
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            end
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                humanoid.JumpPower = 0
            end
            safeBtn.Text = "Safe (On)"
            Services.TweenService:Create(safeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 170, 90)
            }):Play()
        else
            if root and lastPosition then
                bypassAntiTeleport(function()
                    root.CFrame = lastPosition
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end)
            end
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = currentSpeed
                humanoid.JumpPower = 50
            end
            safeBtn.Text = "Safe (Off)"
            Services.TweenService:Create(safeBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
            }):Play()
        end
    end)

    -- Keep frozen while safe is active
    Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if safeActive and root then
			antiTeleport.Bypass = true
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(1000, 195, 380)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position
			antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame
			antiTeleport.Bypass = false
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                humanoid.JumpPower = 0
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Store original values AFTER all UI is fully createdw
storeOriginalTransparencies(main)
-- Run initialization animation
local function runInitialization()
	local steps = {
		{text = "Loading assets...", duration = 0.6},
		{text = "Initializing features...", duration = 0.8},
		{text = "Setting up UI...", duration = 0.6},
		{text = "Ready!", duration = 0.4},
	}
	
	local totalProgress = 0
	for _, step in ipairs(steps) do
		initStatus.Text = step.text
		local stepDuration = step.duration
		local stepSize = 1 / #steps
		local startProgress = totalProgress
		
		-- Animate loading bar for this step
		Services.TweenService:Create(
			loadingBar,
			TweenInfo.new(stepDuration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
			{Size = UDim2.new(startProgress + stepSize, 0, 1, 0)}
		):Play()
		
		-- Update percentage text
		for i = 0, stepDuration * 60 do
			local progress = startProgress + (i / (stepDuration * 60)) * stepSize
			initPercent.Text = math.floor(progress * 100) .. "%"
			task.wait(1/60)
		end
		
		totalProgress = totalProgress + stepSize
	end
	
	-- Final "Ready!" state
	initPercent.Text = "100%"
	task.wait(0.3)
	
	-- Show main UI first (invisible)
	main.Visible = true
	
	-- Set all main UI elements to transparent initially
	local function setTransparent(element)
		if element:IsA("Frame") or element:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
			element.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
		if element:IsA("TextLabel") or element:IsA("TextButton") then
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
		if element:IsA("ImageLabel") or element:IsA("ImageButton") then
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
			element.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
		
		for _, child in ipairs(element:GetChildren()) do
			setTransparent(child)
		end
	end
	
	setTransparent(main)
	
	-- Fade out all initialization elements
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		initContainer,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		initTitle,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{TextTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		initStatus,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{TextTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		loadingBarBG,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		loadingBar,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{BackgroundTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	Services.TweenService:Create(
		initPercent,
		TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut),
		{TextTransparency = 1}
	):Play()
	
	task.wait(0.5)
	
	-- Fade in main UI
	local function fadeInElement(element)
		if element:IsA("GuiObject") and originalTransparencies[element] then
			local original = originalTransparencies[element]
			local props = {}
			
			if original.bg then
				props.BackgroundTransparency = original.bg
			end
			if original.text then
				props.TextTransparency = original.text
			end
			if original.img then
				props.ImageTransparency = original.img
			end
			
			if next(props) then
				Services.TweenService:Create(element, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), props):Play()
			end
		end
		
		for _, child in ipairs(element:GetChildren()) do
			fadeInElement(child)
		end
	end
	
	fadeInElement(main)
	switchTab("Home")
	
	task.wait(0.6)

	game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
	Title = "Lunacy Hub",
	Text = "yo wsg",
	Duration = 5
})
game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
	Title = "Lunacy Hub",
	Text = "left alt to toggle ui btw",
	Duration = 5
})

local playerRank = getRank(player.Name)

game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
	Title = "Lunacy Hub",
	Text = "Welcome, " .. player.Name .. "! Your rank is: " .. playerRank,
	Duration = 5
})

	initScreen:Destroy()
end

-- Run initialization on startup
runInitialization()
--================================================
-- LOGIC
--================================================
local updateOrbitSystem

local function initializeOrbitSystem()

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Collector Helpers
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function collectorAdd(
        collector,
        lookup,
        part
    )

        if lookup[part] then
            return
        end

        lookup[part] = true

        table.insert(
            collector,
            part
        )

    end

    local function collectorRemove(
        collector,
        lookup,
        part
    )

        if not lookup[part] then
            return
        end

        lookup[part] = nil

        local index = table.find(
            collector,
            part
        )

        if index then

            table.remove(
                collector,
                index
            )

        end

    end

    local function cleanupCollector(
        collector,
        lookup
    )

        for i = #collector, 1, -1 do

            local part = collector[i]

            if not part
            or not part.Parent then

                collectorRemove(
                    collector,
                    lookup,
                    part
                )

            end

        end

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Event-Driven Building Collector
    -------------------------------------------------

    local structure

    local structureConnections = {}
    local workspaceConnections = {}

    local buildingsWereEnabled = false
    local vehiclesWereEnabled = false

    local pendingBuildingChecks = {}
    local vehicleAssemblyRoots = {}

    local function disconnectBuildingConnections()

        for _, connection in ipairs(structureConnections) do
            connection:Disconnect()
        end

        table.clear(structureConnections)

    end

    local function clearBuildingCollector()

        table.clear(orbitCollectors.Buildings)
        table.clear(orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings)

        table.clear(orbitCollectors.Vehicles)
        table.clear(orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles)

    end

    local MAX_CONNECTED_BUILDING_PARTS = 50
    local MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_MASS = 5000
    local MAX_BUILDING_PART_SIZE = 100
    local MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_EXTENT = 100
    local MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_VOLUME = 100000

    local function rebuildVehicleAssemblyRoots()

        table.clear(vehicleAssemblyRoots)

        if not structure then
            return
        end

        for i = #AntiSit.Seats, 1, -1 do

            local seat =
                AntiSit.Seats[i]

            if not seat
            or not seat.Parent then

                table.remove(
                    AntiSit.Seats,
                    i
                )

            elseif seat:IsDescendantOf(structure) then

                local assemblyRoot =
                    seat.AssemblyRootPart

                if assemblyRoot then

                    vehicleAssemblyRoots[assemblyRoot] =
                        true

                end

            end

        end

    end

    local function isVehiclePart(part)

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        if vehicleAssemblyRoots[assemblyRoot] then
            return true
        end

        local current = part

        while current
        and current ~= structure do

            ----------------------------------
            -- Detect vehicle containers even
            -- when they are Folders, not Models.
            ----------------------------------

            local name =
                current.Name:lower()

            if name:find("vehicle", 1, true)
            or name:find("car", 1, true)
            or name:find("truck", 1, true)
            or name:find("bus", 1, true)
            or name:find("jeep", 1, true)
            or name:find("van", 1, true)
            or name:find("boat", 1, true)
            or name:find("bike", 1, true)
            or name:find("train", 1, true)
            or name:find("cart", 1, true)
            or name:find("taxi", 1, true)
            or name:find("limo", 1, true)
            or name:find("trailer", 1, true)
            or name:find("tractor", 1, true)
            or name:find("forklift", 1, true)
            or name:find("tank", 1, true)
            or name:find("plane", 1, true)
            or name:find("aircraft", 1, true)
            or name:find("helicopter", 1, true)
            or name:find("wheel", 1, true)
            or name:find("tire", 1, true) then

                return true

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Some games use Seat instead of
            -- VehicleSeat for their vehicles.
            ----------------------------------

            if current:IsA("Model")
            and (
                current:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Seat", true)
                or current:FindFirstChild("Wheel", true)
                or current:FindFirstChild("Wheels", true)
                or current:FindFirstChild("Tire", true)
            ) then

                return true

            end

            current = current.Parent

        end

        return false

    end

    local function isPlayerCharacterPart(part)

        local current = part

        while current
        and current ~= workspace do

            if current:IsA("Model")
            and Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(current) then

                return true

            end

            current = current.Parent

        end

        return false

    end

    local function stopOrbitAssembly(part)

        if not part
        or not part.Parent
        or not part:IsA("BasePart") then

            return

        end

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        assemblyRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
            Vector3.zero

        assemblyRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
            Vector3.zero

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Orbit Part Preparation
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function destroyConflictingMovers(part)

        for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do

            if child:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity")
            or child:IsA("BodyForce")
            or child:IsA("BodyGyro")
            or child:IsA("BodyPosition")
            or child:IsA("BodyThrust")
            or child:IsA("BodyVelocity")
            or child:IsA("RocketPropulsion") then

                child:Destroy()

            end

        end

    end

    local function prepareOrbitPart(part)

        if not part
        or not part.Parent
        or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end

        if orbit.Prepared[part] then
            return
        end

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        local isOrbitHazard =
            orbitCollectorLookup.Avalanche[part] == true
            or orbitCollectorLookup.Volcano[part] == true

        ----------------------------------
        -- Gather the entire assembly
        ----------------------------------

        local assemblyParts = {
            assemblyRoot
        }

        local seen = {
            [assemblyRoot] = true
        }

        for _, connectedPart in ipairs(
            assemblyRoot:GetConnectedParts(true)
        ) do

            if not seen[connectedPart] then

                seen[connectedPart] = true

                table.insert(
                    assemblyParts,
                    connectedPart
                )

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Save original state
        ----------------------------------

        orbit.Original[part] = {
            Parts = {},
            IsHazard = isOrbitHazard
        }

        for _, assemblyPart in ipairs(
            assemblyParts
        ) do

            if assemblyPart
            and assemblyPart.Parent then

                orbit.Original[part].Parts[assemblyPart] = {

                    CanCollide =
                        assemblyPart.CanCollide,

                    CanTouch =
                        assemblyPart.CanTouch,

                    CanQuery =
                        assemblyPart.CanQuery,

                    Massless =
                        assemblyPart.Massless,

                    CustomPhysicalProperties =
                        assemblyPart.CustomPhysicalProperties,

                }

                ----------------------------------
                -- Physics preparation
                ----------------------------------

                assemblyPart.CanCollide = false

                if isOrbitHazard then
                    assemblyPart.CanTouch = false
                    assemblyPart.CanQuery = false
                end

                assemblyPart.Massless = true

                assemblyPart.CustomPhysicalProperties =
                    PhysicalProperties.new(
                        0.01,
                        0,
                        0,
                        0,
                        0
                    )

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Remove competing legacy movers
        ----------------------------------

        destroyConflictingMovers(
            assemblyRoot
        )

        ----------------------------------
        -- Attachment
        ----------------------------------

        local attachment =
            Instance.new("Attachment")

        attachment.Name =
            "LunacyOrbitAttachment"

        attachment.Parent =
            assemblyRoot

        ----------------------------------
        -- AlignPosition
        ----------------------------------

        local alignPosition =
            Instance.new("AlignPosition")

        alignPosition.Name =
            "LunacyOrbitAlignPosition"

        alignPosition.Mode =
            Enum.PositionAlignmentMode.OneAttachment

        alignPosition.Attachment0 =
            attachment

        alignPosition.ApplyAtCenterOfMass =
            true

        alignPosition.ReactionForceEnabled =
            false

        alignPosition.RigidityEnabled =
            false

        alignPosition.MaxForce =
            1000000000

        alignPosition.MaxVelocity =
            math.huge

        alignPosition.Responsiveness =
            200

        alignPosition.Position =
            assemblyRoot.Position

        alignPosition.Parent =
            assemblyRoot

        ----------------------------------
        -- AlignOrientation
        ----------------------------------

        local alignOrientation =
            Instance.new("AlignOrientation")

        alignOrientation.Name =
            "LunacyOrbitAlignOrientation"

        alignOrientation.Mode =
            Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment

        alignOrientation.Attachment0 =
            attachment

        alignOrientation.ReactionTorqueEnabled =
            false

        alignOrientation.RigidityEnabled =
            false

        alignOrientation.MaxTorque =
            1000000000

        alignOrientation.MaxAngularVelocity =
            math.huge

        alignOrientation.Responsiveness =
            100

        ----------------------------------
        -- Start disabled so existing
        -- presets behave exactly as before.
        ----------------------------------

        alignOrientation.Enabled =
            false

        alignOrientation.CFrame =
            assemblyRoot.CFrame.Rotation

        alignOrientation.Parent =
            assemblyRoot
            
        ----------------------------------
        -- AngularVelocity
        ----------------------------------

        local angularVelocity =
            Instance.new("AngularVelocity")

        angularVelocity.Name =
            "LunacyOrbitAngularVelocity"

        angularVelocity.Attachment0 =
            attachment

        angularVelocity.RelativeTo =
            Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World

        angularVelocity.MaxTorque =
            1000000000

        angularVelocity.AngularVelocity =
            Vector3.zero

        angularVelocity.Parent =
            assemblyRoot

        ----------------------------------
        -- Store mover references
        ----------------------------------

        orbit.Movers[part] = {

            Root =
                assemblyRoot,

            Attachment =
                attachment,

            AlignPosition =
                alignPosition,

            AlignOrientation =
                alignOrientation,

            AngularVelocity =
                angularVelocity,

        }

        orbit.Prepared[part] = true

    end

    local function restoreOrbitPart(part)

        ----------------------------------
        -- Remove orbit movers
        ----------------------------------

        local movers =
            orbit.Movers[part]

        if movers then

            if movers.AlignPosition then
                movers.AlignPosition:Destroy()
            end

            if movers.AlignOrientation then
                movers.AlignOrientation:Destroy()
            end

            if movers.AngularVelocity then
                movers.AngularVelocity:Destroy()
            end

            if movers.Attachment then
                movers.Attachment:Destroy()
            end

        end

        orbit.Movers[part] = nil

        ----------------------------------
        -- Restore original properties
        ----------------------------------

        local original =
            orbit.Original[part]

        if original
        and original.Parts then

            for assemblyPart, state in pairs(
                original.Parts
            ) do

                if assemblyPart
                and assemblyPart.Parent then

                    assemblyPart.CanCollide =
                        state.CanCollide

                    assemblyPart.CanTouch =
                        state.CanTouch

                    assemblyPart.CanQuery =
                        state.CanQuery

                    assemblyPart.Massless =
                        state.Massless

                    assemblyPart.CustomPhysicalProperties =
                        state.CustomPhysicalProperties

                end

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Stop leftover motion
        ----------------------------------

        if part
        and part.Parent then

            stopOrbitAssembly(part)

        end

        orbit.Prepared[part] = nil
        orbit.Original[part] = nil

    end

    local function updateOrbitPreparation()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Restore Parts Leaving Orbit
        ----------------------------------

        local toRestore = {}

        for part in pairs(orbit.Prepared) do

            if not orbit.Lookup[part] then

                table.insert(
                    toRestore,
                    part
                )

            end

        end

        for _, part in ipairs(toRestore) do

            restoreOrbitPart(part)

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Prepare Current Orbit Parts
        ----------------------------------

        for _, part in ipairs(orbit.Parts) do

            if part
            and part.Parent then

                prepareOrbitPart(part)

            end

        end

    end
    
    local function releaseOrbitVehicleParts()

        for i = #orbitCollectors.Vehicles, 1, -1 do

            local part = orbitCollectors.Vehicles[i]

            if part and part.Parent then
                part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end

            collectorRemove(
                orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                part
            )

        end

    end

    local function getSafeBuildingAssemblyRoot(part)

        if not part:IsA("BasePart") then
            return nil
        end

        if not part:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
        or part.Anchored then

            return nil

        end

        if isPlayerCharacterPart(part)
        or part.Name == "Handle"
        or part.Name == "AvalanchePart"
        or part.Name == "MtnPart"
        or part.Name == "MeteorTemplate"
        or part.Name == "Lava"
        or part.Name:sub(1, 6) == "Lunacy"
        or table.find(disasterState.AvalancheParts, part)
        or table.find(disasterState.MeteorParts, part)
        or table.find(disasterState.VolcanoParts, part) then

            return nil

        end

        local isVehicle =
            isVehiclePart(part)

        if isVehicle
        and not settings.OrbitVehicles then

            return nil

        end

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        if not assemblyRoot
        or not assemblyRoot:IsDescendantOf(workspace)
        or assemblyRoot.Anchored
        or table.find(disasterState.AvalancheParts, assemblyRoot)
        or table.find(disasterState.MeteorParts, assemblyRoot)
        or table.find(disasterState.VolcanoParts, assemblyRoot) then

            return nil

        end

        local connectedParts =
            assemblyRoot:GetConnectedParts(true)

        if isPlayerCharacterPart(assemblyRoot) then
            return nil
        end

        for _, connectedPart in ipairs(connectedParts) do

            if isPlayerCharacterPart(connectedPart) then
                return nil
            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Normal buildings must be small,
        -- detached debris assemblies.
        --
        -- Vehicles bypass these limits only
        -- when Orbit Vehicles is enabled.
        ----------------------------------

        if not isVehicle then

            local assemblyVolume =
                assemblyRoot.Size.X
                * assemblyRoot.Size.Y
                * assemblyRoot.Size.Z

            for _, connectedPart in ipairs(connectedParts) do

                assemblyVolume +=
                    connectedPart.Size.X
                    * connectedPart.Size.Y
                    * connectedPart.Size.Z

            end

            if assemblyVolume >
                MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_VOLUME then

                return nil

            end

            if #connectedParts >
                MAX_CONNECTED_BUILDING_PARTS then

                return nil

            end

            if assemblyRoot.AssemblyMass >
                MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_MASS then

                return nil

            end

            if assemblyRoot.Size.Magnitude >
                MAX_BUILDING_PART_SIZE then

                return nil

            end

            local assemblyExtent =
                assemblyRoot.Size.Magnitude * 0.5

            for _, connectedPart in ipairs(connectedParts) do

                local extent =
                    (connectedPart.Position
                    - assemblyRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    + connectedPart.Size.Magnitude * 0.5

                assemblyExtent =
                    math.max(
                        assemblyExtent,
                        extent
                    )

            end

            if assemblyExtent >
                MAX_BUILDING_ASSEMBLY_EXTENT then

                return nil

            end

        end

        return assemblyRoot

    end

    local function checkBuildingPart(part)

        pendingBuildingChecks[part] = nil

        if not part
        or not part.Parent
        or not part:IsA("BasePart") then

            return

        end

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        ----------------------------------
        -- When vehicle collection is off,
        -- stop and remove the entire
        -- vehicle assembly immediately.
        ----------------------------------

        local isVehicle =
            isVehiclePart(part)

        if isVehicle
        and not settings.OrbitVehicles then

            stopOrbitAssembly(assemblyRoot)

            collectorRemove(
                orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
                part
            )

            collectorRemove(
                orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
                assemblyRoot
            )

            collectorRemove(
                orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                part
            )

            collectorRemove(
                orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                assemblyRoot
            )

            return

        end

        collectorRemove(
            orbitCollectors.Buildings,
            orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
            part
        )

        collectorRemove(
            orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
            orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
            part
        )

        if not settings.OrbitBuildings
        and not settings.OrbitVehicles then

            return

        end

        local safeAssemblyRoot =
            getSafeBuildingAssemblyRoot(part)

        if not safeAssemblyRoot then
            return
        end

        if isVehicle then
            collectorAdd(
                orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                safeAssemblyRoot
            )
        else
            collectorAdd(
                orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
                safeAssemblyRoot
            )
        end

    end

    local function queueBuildingCheck(part)

        if not part
        or not part:IsA("BasePart") then

            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- One check per welded assembly,
        -- not one check per individual part.
        ----------------------------------

        local assemblyRoot =
            part.AssemblyRootPart
            or part

        if pendingBuildingChecks[assemblyRoot] then
            return
        end

        pendingBuildingChecks[assemblyRoot] =
            true

        task.defer(function()

            if pendingBuildingChecks[assemblyRoot] then

                checkBuildingPart(assemblyRoot)

            end

        end)

    end

    local function queueJointParts(joint)

        local part0 = joint.Part0
        local part1 = joint.Part1

        task.defer(function()

            if not part0
            or not part0.Parent
            or not part1
            or not part1.Parent then

                return

            end

            local root0 =
                part0.AssemblyRootPart
                or part0

            local root1 =
                part1.AssemblyRootPart
                or part1

            ----------------------------------
            -- If both parts still share one
            -- assembly, the joint removal did
            -- not actually detach anything.
            ----------------------------------

            if root0 == root1 then
                return
            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- A real split occurred. Check
            -- both resulting assemblies.
            ----------------------------------

            queueBuildingCheck(root0)
            queueBuildingCheck(root1)

        end)

    end

    local function scanStructureOnce()

        if not settings.OrbitBuildings
        and not settings.OrbitVehicles then

            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Collect loose map assemblies across
        -- the workspace. Category-specific
        -- disaster parts and player characters
        -- are filtered by the safety function.
        ----------------------------------

        rebuildVehicleAssemblyRoots()

        for _, instance in ipairs(
            workspace:GetDescendants()
        ) do

            if instance:IsA("BasePart")
            and not instance.Anchored then

                queueBuildingCheck(instance)

            end

        end

    end

    local function bindStructure(newStructure)

        disconnectBuildingConnections()

        structure = newStructure

        clearBuildingCollector()

        if not structure then
            return
        end

        table.insert(
            structureConnections,

            structure.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)

                if instance:IsA("Seat") then

                    task.defer(function()
                        rebuildVehicleAssemblyRoots()
                    end)

                end

            end)
        )

        table.insert(
            structureConnections,

            structure.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(instance)

                if instance:IsA("BasePart") then

                    pendingBuildingChecks[instance] = nil

                    collectorRemove(
                        orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                        orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
                        instance
                    )

                    collectorRemove(
                        orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                        orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                        instance
                    )

                elseif instance:IsA("JointInstance")
                or instance:IsA("WeldConstraint") then

                    queueJointParts(instance)

                end

            end)
        )

        scanStructureOnce()

        task.defer(function()

            if structure ~= newStructure then
                return
            end

            rebuildVehicleAssemblyRoots()

            if not settings.OrbitVehicles then

                for assemblyRoot in pairs(
                    vehicleAssemblyRoots
                ) do

                    checkBuildingPart(
                        assemblyRoot
                    )

                end

            end

        end)

    end

    table.insert(
        workspaceConnections,

        workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)

            if (
                settings.OrbitBuildings
                or settings.OrbitVehicles
            )
            and instance:IsA("BasePart")
            and not instance.Anchored then

                queueBuildingCheck(instance)

            end

        end)
    )

    table.insert(
        workspaceConnections,

        workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(instance)

            if instance:IsA("BasePart") then

                pendingBuildingChecks[instance] = nil

                collectorRemove(
                    orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                    orbitCollectorLookup.Buildings,
                    instance
                )

                collectorRemove(
                    orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                    orbitCollectorLookup.Vehicles,
                    instance
                )

            end

        end)
    )

    table.insert(
        workspaceConnections,

        workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)

            if child.Name == "Structure" then
                bindStructure(child)
            end

        end)
    )

    table.insert(
        workspaceConnections,

        workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)

            if child == structure then

                bindStructure(
                    workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")
                )

            end

        end)
    )

    bindStructure(
        workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")
    )

    local function updateBuildingCollector()

        if settings.OrbitBuildings
        == buildingsWereEnabled
        and settings.OrbitVehicles
        == vehiclesWereEnabled then

            return

        end

        local vehiclesJustDisabled =
            vehiclesWereEnabled
            and not settings.OrbitVehicles

        buildingsWereEnabled =
            settings.OrbitBuildings

        vehiclesWereEnabled =
            settings.OrbitVehicles

        ----------------------------------
        -- Explicitly release vehicles:
        -- remove them and erase the last
        -- orbit velocity they were given.
        ----------------------------------

        if vehiclesJustDisabled then
            releaseOrbitVehicleParts()
        end

        if not buildingsWereEnabled
        and not vehiclesWereEnabled then

            clearBuildingCollector()
            return

        end

        scanStructureOnce()

    end

    local function updateAvalancheCollector()

        if not settings.OrbitAvalanche then

            table.clear(orbitCollectors.Avalanche)
            table.clear(orbitCollectorLookup.Avalanche)

            return

        end

        cleanupCollector(
            orbitCollectors.Avalanche,
            orbitCollectorLookup.Avalanche
        )

        for _, part in ipairs(disasterState.AvalancheParts) do

            if part.Parent
            and part:IsA("BasePart") then

                collectorAdd(
                    orbitCollectors.Avalanche,
                    orbitCollectorLookup.Avalanche,
                    part
                )

            end

        end

    end

    local function updateMeteorCollector()

        if not settings.OrbitMeteor then

            table.clear(orbitCollectors.Meteor)
            table.clear(orbitCollectorLookup.Meteor)

            return

        end

        cleanupCollector(
            orbitCollectors.Meteor,
            orbitCollectorLookup.Meteor
        )

        for _, part in ipairs(disasterState.MeteorParts) do

            if part.Parent
            and part:IsA("BasePart") then

                collectorAdd(
                    orbitCollectors.Meteor,
                    orbitCollectorLookup.Meteor,
                    part
                )

            end

        end

    end

    local function updateVolcanoCollector()

        if not settings.OrbitVolcano then

            table.clear(orbitCollectors.Volcano)
            table.clear(orbitCollectorLookup.Volcano)

            return

        end

        cleanupCollector(
            orbitCollectors.Volcano,
            orbitCollectorLookup.Volcano
        )

        for _, part in ipairs(disasterState.VolcanoParts) do

            if part.Parent
            and part:IsA("BasePart") then

                collectorAdd(
                    orbitCollectors.Volcano,
                    orbitCollectorLookup.Volcano,
                    part
                )

            end

        end

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Merge
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function rebuildOrbitList()

        table.clear(orbit.Parts)
        table.clear(orbit.Lookup)

        local function mergeCollector(
            collector,
            rejectPlayerParts
        )

            for _, part in ipairs(collector) do

                if part
                and part.Parent
                and (
                    not rejectPlayerParts
                    or not isPlayerCharacterPart(part)
                )
                and not orbit.Lookup[part] then

                    orbit.Lookup[part] = true

                    table.insert(
                        orbit.Parts,
                        part
                    )

                end

            end

        end

        if settings.OrbitBuildings then

            mergeCollector(
                orbitCollectors.Buildings,
                true
            )

        end

        if settings.OrbitVehicles then

            mergeCollector(
                orbitCollectors.Vehicles,
                true
            )

        end

        if settings.OrbitAvalanche then
            mergeCollector(orbitCollectors.Avalanche)
        end

        if settings.OrbitMeteor then
            mergeCollector(orbitCollectors.Meteor)
        end

        if settings.OrbitVolcano then
            mergeCollector(orbitCollectors.Volcano)
        end

    end

    local function updateOrbitPreparation()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Restore Parts Leaving Orbit
        ----------------------------------

        for part in pairs(orbit.Prepared) do

            if not orbit.Lookup[part] then

                restoreOrbitPart(part)

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Prepare Current Orbit Parts
        ----------------------------------

        for _, part in ipairs(orbit.Parts) do

            if part
            and part.Parent then

                prepareOrbitPart(part)

            end

        end

    end

    local function updateOrbitInfo()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Remove Missing Parts
        ----------------------------------

        for part in pairs(orbit.Info) do

            if not orbit.Lookup[part] then

                orbit.Info[part] = nil

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Create New Parts
        ----------------------------------

        local count =
            #orbit.Parts

        if count == 0 then
            return
        end

        for index, part in ipairs(orbit.Parts) do

            if not orbit.Info[part] then

                local angle =
                    ((index - 1) / count)
                    * math.pi * 2

                orbit.Info[part] = {

                    ----------------------------------

                    CurrentAngle = angle,
                    DesiredAngle = angle,

                    ----------------------------------

                    CurrentRadius =
                        orbitState.Radius,

                    DesiredRadius =
                        orbitState.Radius,

                    ----------------------------------

                    CurrentHeight = 0,
                    DesiredHeight = 0,

                    ----------------------------------

                    Ring = 1,

                }

            end

        end

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Movement
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function setOrbitPartOrientation(
        part,
        targetCFrame,
        enabled
    )

        local movers =
            orbit.Movers[part]

        if not movers then
            return
        end

        local alignOrientation =
            movers.AlignOrientation

        if not alignOrientation
        or not alignOrientation.Parent then
            return
        end

        if not enabled then

            alignOrientation.Enabled =
                false

            return

        end

        alignOrientation.CFrame =
            targetCFrame.Rotation

        alignOrientation.Enabled =
            true

    end

    local function moveOrbitPart(
        part,
        targetPosition,
        spinInPlace
    )

        local movers =
            orbit.Movers[part]

        if not movers then
            return
        end

        local alignPosition =
            movers.AlignPosition

        local alignOrientation =
            movers.AlignOrientation

        local angularVelocity =
            movers.AngularVelocity

        if not alignPosition
        or not alignPosition.Parent then
            return
        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Safety Bubble
        ----------------------------------

        if not spinInPlace then

            local rootPart =
                movers.Root

            if rootPart
            and rootPart.Parent then

                local fromPlayer =
                    rootPart.Position
                    - root.Position

                local safeRadius =
                    8
                    + (
                        rootPart.Size.Magnitude
                        * 0.5
                    )

                local distance =
                    fromPlayer.Magnitude

                if distance < safeRadius
                and distance > 0 then

                    targetPosition =
                        targetPosition
                        + fromPlayer.Unit
                        * (
                            safeRadius
                            - distance
                        )

                end

            end

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Position Control
        ----------------------------------

        alignPosition.Position =
            targetPosition

        ----------------------------------
        -- Physical Spin
        ----------------------------------

        if angularVelocity
        and angularVelocity.Parent then

            if spinInPlace then

                if alignOrientation then
                    alignOrientation.Enabled = false
                end

                angularVelocity.AngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.new(
                        0,
                        math.max(
                            8,
                            orbitState.Speed * 2
                        ),
                        0
                    )

            else

                angularVelocity.AngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

        end

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Animation
    -------------------------------------------------

    local TAU = math.pi * 2

    local spinningPresets = {
        Saturn = true,
        Spiral = true,
        DNA = true,
        Sphere = true,
        Wave = true,
        Vortex = true,
        Torus = true,
        Flower = true,
        Cube = true,
    }

    local orientationModes = {

        Saturn = "Outward",
        Spiral = "Outward",
        DNA = "Outward",
        Sphere = "Outward",
        Wave = "Outward",
        Vortex = "Outward",
        Torus = "Outward",
        Flower = "Outward",

        Cube = "Outward",
        Cage = "Outward",

        Halo = "Outward",
        Portal = "Outward",

        Grid = "Fixed",
        Shield = "Fixed",
        Throne = "Fixed",

        Hand = "Fixed",
        Heart = "Fixed",
        ["Angel Wings"] = "Fixed",

    }

    local function smoothValue(
        current,
        target,
        speed,
        deltaTime
    )

        local alpha =
            1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)

        return current +
            (target - current) * alpha

    end

    local function smoothAngle(
        current,
        target,
        speed,
        deltaTime
    )

        local difference =
            (target - current + math.pi)
            % TAU
            - math.pi

        local alpha =
            1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)

        return current +
            difference * alpha

    end

    local function getOrbitLayout(
        index,
        count,
        info
    )

        local preset =
            orbit.TargetPreset

        ----------------------------------
        -- Saturn: concentric rings
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Saturn" then

            local ringCount =
                math.max(
                    1,
                    math.floor(orbitState.RingCount)
                )

            local ringIndex =
                (index - 1) % ringCount

            local positionInRing =
                math.floor((index - 1) / ringCount)

            local partsInRing =
                math.ceil(
                    (count - ringIndex)
                    / ringCount
                )

            local slotAngle =
                (positionInRing / partsInRing)
                * TAU

            local ringSpacing =
                math.max(
                    8,
                    orbitState.Radius * 0.35
                )

            local centeredRing =
                ringIndex
                - ((ringCount - 1) * 0.5)

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius
                + centeredRing * ringSpacing

            info.Ring = ringIndex + 1

            return slotAngle + orbitState.Rotation,
                   radius,
                   0

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Spiral: one rising helix
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Spiral" then

            local progress = 0.5

            if count > 1 then

                progress =
                    (index - 1)
                    / (count - 1)

            end

            local turns = 3

            local angle =
                progress * turns * TAU
                + orbitState.Rotation

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius

            local height =
                (progress - 0.5)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 2.2

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- DNA: double helix with rungs
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "DNA" then

            ----------------------------------
            -- Every four parts form one
            -- horizontal DNA rung:
            --
            -- outer strand
            -- inner rung
            -- inner rung
            -- outer strand
            ----------------------------------

            local lane =
                (index - 1) % 4

            local rungIndex =
                math.floor((index - 1) / 4)

            local rungCount =
                math.ceil(count / 4)

            local progress = 0.5

            if rungCount > 1 then

                progress =
                    rungIndex
                    / (rungCount - 1)

            end

            local turns = 3

            local baseAngle =
                progress * turns * TAU
                + orbitState.Rotation

            local oppositeSide =
                lane == 1
                or lane == 3

            local angle =
                baseAngle

            if oppositeSide then
                angle += math.pi
            end

            local isOuterStrand =
                lane == 0
                or lane == 1

            local radius

            if isOuterStrand then

                radius =
                    orbitState.Radius

            else

                radius =
                    orbitState.Radius * 0.35

            end

            local height =
                (progress - 0.5)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 2.2

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Sphere
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Sphere" then

            local progress =
                (index - 0.5) / count

            local vertical =
                1 - (progress * 2)

            local horizontal =
                math.sqrt(
                    math.max(
                        0,
                        1 - (vertical * vertical)
                    )
                )

            local goldenAngle =
                math.pi * (3 - math.sqrt(5))

            local angle =
                (index - 1) * goldenAngle
                + orbitState.Rotation

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius
                * horizontal

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius
                * vertical

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Wave
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Wave" then

            local slotAngle =
                ((index - 1) / count)
                * TAU

            local angle =
                slotAngle + orbitState.Rotation

            local height =
                math.sin(
                    slotAngle * 3
                    + orbitState.Rotation
                )
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.35

            return angle,
                   orbitState.Radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Vortex
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Vortex" then

            local progress = 0.5

            if count > 1 then

                progress =
                    (index - 1)
                    / (count - 1)

            end

            local angle =
                progress * 4 * TAU
                + orbitState.Rotation

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius
                * (0.25 + progress * 1.1)

            local height =
                (progress - 0.5)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 2.5

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Torus
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Torus" then

            local goldenAngle =
                math.pi * (3 - math.sqrt(5))

            local majorRadius =
                orbitState.Radius

            local minorRadius =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.35

            local angle =
                (index - 1) * goldenAngle
                + orbitState.Rotation

            local tubeAngle =
                (index - 1)
                * TAU
                * 0.61803398875

            local radius =
                majorRadius
                + math.cos(tubeAngle)
                * minorRadius

            local height =
                math.sin(tubeAngle)
                * minorRadius

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Flower
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Flower" then

            local slotAngle =
                ((index - 1) / count)
                * TAU

            local petalCount = 6

            local petalShape =
                (1 + math.cos(
                    slotAngle * petalCount
                ))
                * 0.5

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius
                * (0.3 + petalShape * 0.7)

            local height =
                math.sin(
                    slotAngle * petalCount
                )
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.12

            return slotAngle + orbitState.Rotation,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Cube
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Cube" then

            local edge =
                (index - 1) % 12

            local pointIndex =
                math.floor((index - 1) / 12)

            local pointsPerEdge =
                math.ceil(count / 12)

            local progress = 0.5

            if pointsPerEdge > 1 then

                progress =
                    pointIndex
                    / (pointsPerEdge - 1)

            end

            local halfSize =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.7

            local line =
                -halfSize
                + progress * halfSize * 2

            local x = 0
            local y = 0
            local z = 0

            if edge == 0 then
                x, y, z = line, -halfSize, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 1 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, -halfSize, line

            elseif edge == 2 then
                x, y, z = -line, -halfSize, halfSize

            elseif edge == 3 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, -halfSize, -line

            elseif edge == 4 then
                x, y, z = line, halfSize, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 5 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, halfSize, line

            elseif edge == 6 then
                x, y, z = -line, halfSize, halfSize

            elseif edge == 7 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, halfSize, -line

            elseif edge == 8 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, line, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 9 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, line, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 10 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, line, halfSize

            else
                x, y, z = -halfSize, line, halfSize
            end

            local radius =
                Vector3.new(x, 0, z).Magnitude

            local angle =
                math.atan2(z, x)
                + orbitState.Rotation

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   y

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Cage: stationary wireframe cube
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Cage" then

            local edge =
                (index - 1) % 12

            local pointIndex =
                math.floor((index - 1) / 12)

            local pointsPerEdge =
                math.ceil(count / 12)

            local progress = 0.5

            if pointsPerEdge > 1 then

                progress =
                    pointIndex
                    / (pointsPerEdge - 1)

            end

            local halfSize =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.7

            local line =
                -halfSize
                + progress * halfSize * 2

            local x = 0
            local y = 0
