            local z = 0

            ----------------------------------
            -- Bottom square
            ----------------------------------

            if edge == 0 then
                x, y, z = line, -halfSize, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 1 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, -halfSize, line

            elseif edge == 2 then
                x, y, z = -line, -halfSize, halfSize

            elseif edge == 3 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, -halfSize, -line

            ----------------------------------
            -- Top square
            ----------------------------------

            elseif edge == 4 then
                x, y, z = line, halfSize, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 5 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, halfSize, line

            elseif edge == 6 then
                x, y, z = -line, halfSize, halfSize

            elseif edge == 7 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, halfSize, -line

            ----------------------------------
            -- Vertical corners
            ----------------------------------

            elseif edge == 8 then
                x, y, z = -halfSize, line, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 9 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, line, -halfSize

            elseif edge == 10 then
                x, y, z = halfSize, line, halfSize

            else
                x, y, z = -halfSize, line, halfSize
            end

            local radius =
                Vector3.new(x, 0, z).Magnitude

            local angle =
                math.atan2(z, x)

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   y

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Halo: stationary ring overhead
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Halo" then

            local slotAngle =
                ((index - 1) / count)
                * TAU

            local radius =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.7

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.8

            return slotAngle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Portal: stationary vertical ring
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Portal" then

            local slotAngle =
                ((index - 1) / count)
                * TAU

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local portalRadius =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.45

            local forwardDistance =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.9

            local horizontalOffset =
                forward * forwardDistance
                + right
                * math.cos(slotAngle)
                * portalRadius

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.65
                + math.sin(slotAngle)
                * portalRadius

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Grid: stationary overhead panel
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Grid" then

            local columns =
                math.ceil(math.sqrt(count))

            local rows =
                math.ceil(count / columns)

            local column =
                (index - 1) % columns

            local row =
                math.floor((index - 1) / columns)

            local xProgress = 0.5
            local zProgress = 0.5

            if columns > 1 then

                xProgress =
                    column / (columns - 1)

            end

            if rows > 1 then

                zProgress =
                    row / (rows - 1)

            end

            local halfSize =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.8

            local localX =
                (xProgress - 0.5)
                * halfSize * 2

            local localZ =
                (zProgress - 0.5)
                * halfSize * 2

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                right * localX
                + forward * localZ

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.9

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Shield: stationary curved wall
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Shield" then

            local columns =
                math.ceil(math.sqrt(count * 1.5))

            local rows =
                math.ceil(count / columns)

            local column =
                (index - 1) % columns

            local row =
                math.floor((index - 1) / columns)

            local horizontalProgress = 0.5
            local verticalProgress = 0.5

            if columns > 1 then

                horizontalProgress =
                    column / (columns - 1)

            end

            if rows > 1 then

                verticalProgress =
                    row / (rows - 1)

            end

            local side =
                (horizontalProgress - 0.5)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 1.8

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.65
                + (verticalProgress - 0.5)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 1.5

            local curve =
                (1 - math.abs(
                    horizontalProgress - 0.5
                ) * 2)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.25

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                forward
                * (orbitState.Radius * 0.9 + curve)
                + right * side

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Throne: stationary backrest
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Throne" then

            local lane =
                (index - 1) % 5

            local laneIndex =
                math.floor((index - 1) / 5)

            local pointsPerLane =
                math.ceil(count / 5)

            local progress = 0.5

            if pointsPerLane > 1 then

                progress =
                    laneIndex
                    / (pointsPerLane - 1)

            end

            local sidePositions = {
                -1,
                1,
                -0.55,
                0,
                0.55,
            }

            local side =
                sidePositions[lane + 1]
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.65

            local isPillar =
                lane == 0
                or lane == 1

            local heightRange =
                orbitState.Radius * 1.35

            if isPillar then

                heightRange =
                    orbitState.Radius * 1.75

            end

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.1
                + progress * heightRange

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                -forward
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.9
                + right * side

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Hand: waving beside the player
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Hand" then

            local handSize =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.48

            local wavePhase =
                math.sin(
                    orbitState.AnimationTime * 3.4
                )

            local palmCount =
                math.min(
                    count,
                    math.max(
                        6,
                        math.floor(count * 0.5)
                    )
                )

            local localX = 0
            local localY = 0

            ----------------------------------
            -- Palm
            ----------------------------------

            if index <= palmCount then

                local columns =
                    math.min(
                        4,
                        math.ceil(math.sqrt(palmCount))
                    )

                local rows =
                    math.ceil(palmCount / columns)

                local column =
                    (index - 1) % columns

                local row =
                    math.floor((index - 1) / columns)

                local xProgress = 0.5
                local yProgress = 0.5

                if columns > 1 then
                    xProgress = column / (columns - 1)
                end

                if rows > 1 then
                    yProgress = row / (rows - 1)
                end

                localX =
                    (xProgress - 0.5)
                    * handSize * 1.4

                localY =
                    yProgress
                    * handSize * 0.9

            ----------------------------------
            -- Fingers
            ----------------------------------

            else

                local fingerSlot =
                    index - palmCount

                local finger =
                    (fingerSlot - 1) % 5

                local fingerIndex =
                    math.floor(
                        (fingerSlot - 1) / 5
                    )

                local fingerPartCount =
                    count - palmCount

                local partsPerFinger =
                    math.ceil(fingerPartCount / 5)

                local fingerProgress = 0.5

                if partsPerFinger > 1 then

                    fingerProgress =
                        fingerIndex
                        / (partsPerFinger - 1)

                end

                local fingerOffsets = {
                    -0.75,
                    -0.35,
                    0,
                    0.35,
                    0.7,
                }

                local fingerLengths = {
                    0.55,
                    0.95,
                    1.15,
                    1.05,
                    0.85,
                }

                localX =
                    fingerOffsets[finger + 1]
                    * handSize

                localY =
                    handSize * 0.9
                    + fingerProgress
                    * fingerLengths[finger + 1]
                    * handSize

                ----------------------------------
                -- Thumb points outward instead
                -- of straight upward.
                ----------------------------------

                if finger == 0 then

                    localX -=
                        fingerProgress
                        * handSize
                        * 0.45

                    localY -=
                        fingerProgress
                        * handSize
                        * 0.35

                end

                ----------------------------------
                -- Fingertips bend during wave
                ----------------------------------

                localX +=
                    wavePhase
                    * (fingerProgress ^ 2)
                    * handSize
                    * 0.18

            end

            ----------------------------------
            -- Swing the whole hand from its
            -- wrist, making a clear wave.
            ----------------------------------

            local waveAngle =
                wavePhase * math.rad(28)

            local cosine =
                math.cos(waveAngle)

            local sine =
                math.sin(waveAngle)

            local rotatedX =
                localX * cosine
                - localY * sine

            local rotatedY =
                localX * sine
                + localY * cosine

            localX = rotatedX
            localY = rotatedY

            ----------------------------------
            -- Position hand to the player's
            -- right, slightly in front.
            ----------------------------------

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                right
                * (
                    orbitState.Radius * 0.8
                    + localX
                )
                + forward
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.25

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.15
                + localY

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Heart: stationary player emote
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Heart" then

            local progress =
                (index - 1) / count

            local t =
                progress * TAU

            ----------------------------------
            -- Parametric heart outline
            ----------------------------------

            local sine =
                math.sin(t)

            local x =
                16 * sine * sine * sine

            local y =
                13 * math.cos(t)
                - 5 * math.cos(2 * t)
                - 2 * math.cos(3 * t)
                - math.cos(4 * t)

            local heartSize =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.55

            local localX =
                (x / 16)
                * heartSize

            local localY =
                (y / 17)
                * heartSize

            ----------------------------------
            -- Keep the heart to the player's
            -- right and facing forward.
            ----------------------------------

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                right
                * (
                    orbitState.Radius * 1.05
                    + localX
                )
                + forward
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.35

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.8
                + localY

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Angel Wings: feathered aura
        ----------------------------------

        if preset == "Angel Wings" then

            local side =
                (index - 1) % 2

            local sideSign = -1

            if side == 1 then
                sideSign = 1
            end

            local wingSlot =
                math.floor((index - 1) / 2)

            ----------------------------------
            -- Three feather layers per wing
            ----------------------------------

            local layer =
                wingSlot % 3

            local featherIndex =
                math.floor(wingSlot / 3)

            local wingSlots =
                math.ceil(count / 2)

            local pointsPerLayer =
                math.ceil(wingSlots / 3)

            local progress = 0.5

            if pointsPerLayer > 1 then

                progress =
                    featherIndex
                    / (pointsPerLayer - 1)

            end

            local featherSpread =
                1.25 - layer * 0.12

            local span =
                orbitState.Radius
                * (
                    0.35
                    + progress * featherSpread
                )

            local height =
                orbitState.Radius * 0.4
                + math.sin(progress * math.pi)
                * orbitState.Radius
                * (0.55 - layer * 0.1)
                - progress
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.25
                - layer
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.14

            ----------------------------------
            -- Gentle wing flap: the tips
            -- move more than the roots.
            ----------------------------------

            local flap =
                math.sin(
                    orbitState.AnimationTime * 2.5
                )
                * orbitState.Radius
                * 0.18

            height +=
                flap
                * (0.25 + progress * 0.75)

            local localSide =
                sideSign * span

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local forward =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                ).Unit

            local right =
                Vector3.new(
                    -forward.Z,
                    0,
                    forward.X
                )

            local horizontalOffset =
                -forward
                * orbitState.Radius
                * (0.7 + layer * 0.08)
                + right * localSide

            local angle =
                math.atan2(
                    horizontalOffset.Z,
                    horizontalOffset.X
                )

            local radius =
                horizontalOffset.Magnitude

            return angle,
                   radius,
                   height

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Fallback
        ----------------------------------

        local slotAngle =
            ((index - 1) / count)
            * TAU

        return slotAngle + orbitState.Rotation,
            orbitState.Radius,
            0

    end

    local function updateOrbitControl(
        deltaTime
    )

        if not root then
            return Vector3.zero
        end

        local targetCenter =
            root.Position

        if settings.OrbitControl
        and orbitState.ControlHeld then

            local mouse =
                player:GetMouse()

            if mouse
            and mouse.Hit then

                targetCenter =
                    mouse.Hit.Position

            end

        end

        if not orbitState.ControlCenter then

            orbitState.ControlCenter =
                root.Position

        end

        local alpha =
            1 - math.exp(-3 * deltaTime)

        orbitState.ControlCenter =
            orbitState.ControlCenter:Lerp(
                targetCenter,
                alpha
            )

        return orbitState.ControlCenter

    end

    local function updateOrbitPartOrientation(
        part,
        offset,
        compactControlActive
    )

        ----------------------------------
        -- Compact mode controls spin
        -- with AngularVelocity instead.
        ----------------------------------

        if compactControlActive then

            setOrbitPartOrientation(
                part,
                CFrame.identity,
                false
            )

            return

        end

        local mode =
            orientationModes[
                orbit.TargetPreset
            ]

        ----------------------------------
        -- Outward
        ----------------------------------

        if mode == "Outward" then

            local horizontalDirection =
                Vector3.new(
                    offset.X,
                    0,
                    offset.Z
                )

            if horizontalDirection.Magnitude
            <= 0.001 then

                setOrbitPartOrientation(
                    part,
                    CFrame.identity,
                    false
                )

                return

            end

            local desiredOrientation =
                CFrame.lookAt(
                    Vector3.zero,
                    horizontalDirection.Unit
                )

            setOrbitPartOrientation(
                part,
                desiredOrientation,
                true
            )

            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Fixed
        ----------------------------------

        if mode == "Fixed" then

            local look =
                root.CFrame.LookVector

            local flatLook =
                Vector3.new(
                    look.X,
                    0,
                    look.Z
                )

            if flatLook.Magnitude
            <= 0.001 then

                setOrbitPartOrientation(
                    part,
                    CFrame.identity,
                    false
                )

                return

            end

            local desiredOrientation =
                CFrame.lookAt(
                    Vector3.zero,
                    flatLook.Unit
                )

            setOrbitPartOrientation(
                part,
                desiredOrientation,
                true
            )

            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- No orientation control
        ----------------------------------

        setOrbitPartOrientation(
            part,
            CFrame.identity,
            false
        )

    end

    local function updateOrbitAnimation(
        deltaTime
    )

        orbitState.AnimationTime += deltaTime

        local orbitCenter =
            updateOrbitControl(deltaTime)

        ----------------------------------
        -- Advance the layout clock
        ----------------------------------

        orbitState.Rotation =
            (orbitState.Rotation
            + orbitState.Speed * deltaTime)
            % TAU

        local count =
            #orbit.Parts

        if count == 0 then
            return
        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Slow preset changes only
        ----------------------------------

        local rearrangeSpeed = 2.5

        local compactControlActive =
            settings.OrbitControl
            and orbitState.ControlHeld
            and settings.OrbitControlMode
                == "Compact"

        local presetSpins =
            spinningPresets[orbit.TargetPreset]
            == true
            and not compactControlActive

        for index, part in ipairs(orbit.Parts) do

            local info =
                orbit.Info[part]

            if info then

                local angle,
                      radius,
                      height =
                    getOrbitLayout(
                        index,
                        count,
                        info
                    )

                ----------------------------------
                -- Compact Control: gather parts
                -- into a small ball at the held
                -- cursor position.
                ----------------------------------

                if compactControlActive then

                    ----------------------------------
                    -- True Compact mode:
                    -- every part targets the exact
                    -- same held cursor position.
                    ----------------------------------

                    angle = 0
                    radius = 0
                    height = 0

                end

                info.DesiredAngle = angle
                info.DesiredRadius = radius
                info.DesiredHeight = height

                ----------------------------------
                -- Keep normal orbit spinning
                -- independent of transitions.
                ----------------------------------

                if presetSpins then

                    info.CurrentAngle +=
                        orbitState.Speed
                        * deltaTime

                end

                ----------------------------------
                -- Smooth Rearrangement
                ----------------------------------

                info.CurrentAngle =
                    smoothAngle(
                        info.CurrentAngle,
                        info.DesiredAngle,
                        rearrangeSpeed,
                        deltaTime
                    )

                info.CurrentRadius =
                    smoothValue(
                        info.CurrentRadius,
                        info.DesiredRadius,
                        rearrangeSpeed,
                        deltaTime
                    )

                info.CurrentHeight =
                    smoothValue(
                        info.CurrentHeight,
                        info.DesiredHeight,
                        rearrangeSpeed,
                        deltaTime
                    )

                local offset =
                    Vector3.new(
                        math.cos(info.CurrentAngle)
                            * info.CurrentRadius,

                        info.CurrentHeight,

                        math.sin(info.CurrentAngle)
                            * info.CurrentRadius
                    )

                local targetPosition =
                    orbitCenter + offset

                ----------------------------------
                -- Orientation
                ----------------------------------

                updateOrbitPartOrientation(
                    part,
                    offset,
                    compactControlActive
                )

                ----------------------------------
                -- Position
                ----------------------------------

                moveOrbitPart(
                    part,
                    targetPosition,
                    compactControlActive
                )

            end

        end

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Main Update
    -------------------------------------------------

    updateOrbitSystem = function(deltaTime)

        orbit.Enabled = settings.OrbitSystem

        if not orbit.Enabled then

            orbitState.ControlHeld = false
            orbitState.ControlCenter = nil

            ----------------------------------
            -- Restore Prepared Parts
            ----------------------------------

            local preparedParts = {}

            for part in pairs(orbit.Prepared) do

                table.insert(
                    preparedParts,
                    part
                )

            end

            for _, part in ipairs(preparedParts) do

                restoreOrbitPart(part)

            end

            ----------------------------------

            table.clear(orbit.Parts)
            table.clear(orbit.Lookup)
            table.clear(orbit.Info)

            return

        end

        orbitState.Radius = settings.OrbitRadius
        orbitState.Speed = settings.OrbitSpeed
        orbitState.RingCount = settings.OrbitRingCount

        orbit.TargetPreset = settings.OrbitPreset

        ----------------------------------
        -- Collectors
        ----------------------------------

        updateBuildingCollector()

        updateAvalancheCollector()

        updateMeteorCollector()

        updateVolcanoCollector()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Merge
        ----------------------------------

        rebuildOrbitList()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Preparation
        ----------------------------------

        updateOrbitPreparation()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Orbit Info
        ----------------------------------

        updateOrbitInfo()

        ----------------------------------
        -- Animation
        ----------------------------------

        updateOrbitAnimation(deltaTime)

    end

end

initializeOrbitSystem()

Services.UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(
    function(input, gameProcessed)

        if gameProcessed then
            return
        end

        if input.UserInputType
        ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

            return

        end

        if not settings.OrbitControl
        or not settings.OrbitSystem then

            return

        end

        if Services.UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end

        orbitState.ControlHeld = true

    end
)

Services.UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(
    function(input)

        if input.UserInputType
        == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then

            orbitState.ControlHeld = false

        end

    end
)

_G.__LunacyUpdateRuntime = nil

function _G.__LunacyInitializeRuntime()

    local noFallBusy = false

    local function updateNoFallDamage()

        if not settings.NoFallDamage
        or settings.TouchFling then
            return
        end

        if noFallBusy then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        noFallBusy = true

        local previousVelocity =
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
            Vector3.zero

        task.spawn(function()

            Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

            if root
            and root.Parent
            and settings.NoFallDamage then

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    previousVelocity

            end

            noFallBusy = false

        end)

    end

    local function updateVelocityGuard()

        if not settings.VelocityGuard
        or settings.TouchFling then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horizontal = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z).Magnitude

        if horizontal > 140 or math.abs(vel.Y) > 120 then

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                math.clamp(vel.X, -70, 70),
                math.clamp(vel.Y, -60, 80),
                math.clamp(vel.Z, -70, 70)
            )

        end

    end

    local function updateAntiVoidAutoTP()

        if not settings.AntiVoidAutoTP then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local destroyHeight =
            VoidProtection.OriginalDestroyHeight

        if root.Position.Y <
            destroyHeight + 10 then

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame =
                    CFrame.new(
                        -250,
                        195,
                        310
                    )

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end)

        end

    end

    local function updateAntiTeleport()

        if not settings.AntiTeleport then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        local currentPos = root.Position

        if antiTeleport.Bypass then

            antiTeleport.LastPos = currentPos
            antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame

        elseif antiTeleport.LastPos then

            local distance =
                (currentPos - antiTeleport.LastPos).Magnitude

            if distance > 20 then

                local restoreCFrame

                if antiTeleport.LastCFrame then
                    restoreCFrame = antiTeleport.LastCFrame
                else
                    restoreCFrame = CFrame.new(antiTeleport.LastPos)
                end

                task.spawn(function()

                    for i = 1,5 do

                        if not root or not root.Parent then
                            break
                        end

                        root.CFrame = restoreCFrame
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                        antiTeleport.LastPos = restoreCFrame.Position
                        antiTeleport.LastCFrame = restoreCFrame

                        task.wait(0.05)

                    end

                end)

                local now = tick()

                if now - antiTeleport.LastNotif >= ANTITELEPORT_NOTIF_COOLDOWN then

                    antiTeleport.LastNotif = now

                    game.StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                        Title = "Lunacy Hub - Defense",
                        Text = "Anti Teleport: Blocked a teleport attempt.",
                        Duration = 3
                    })

                end

            else

                antiTeleport.LastPos = currentPos
                antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame

            end

        else

            antiTeleport.LastPos = currentPos
            antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame

        end

    end

    local function updateFollow()

        if not settings.Following then
            return
        end

        if not settings.TargetPlayer then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        local targetChar = settings.TargetPlayer.Character

        if not targetChar then
            return
        end

        local targetRoot =
            targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not targetRoot then
            return
        end

        antiTeleport.Bypass = true

        root.CFrame =
            targetRoot.CFrame *
            CFrame.new(0, 0, settings.FollowDistance)

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

        antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position

        antiTeleport.Bypass = false

    end

    local function updateFlingTouch(targetOverride, freeRun)

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Normal Target Fling Touch checks
        -------------------------------------------------

        if not freeRun then

            if not TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive then
                return
            end

            if TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned then
                return
            end

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Don't fight with free-use modes
            -------------------------------------------------

            if settings.ClickFollow
            or settings.EveryoneFollow then
                return
            end

        end

        if TargetRuntime.FlingTouchBusy then
            return
        end

        local target =
            targetOverride
            or settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local targetChar =
            target.Character

        if not targetChar then
            return
        end

        local targetRoot =
            targetChar:FindFirstChild(
                "HumanoidRootPart"
            )

        if not targetRoot then
            return
        end

        local targetHumanoid =
            targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                "Humanoid"
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Reset history when target changes
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.LastTarget ~= target then

            TargetRuntime.LastTarget =
                target

            TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
                Vector3.zero

            TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                0

        end

        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchBusy =
            true

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Current velocity
        -------------------------------------------------

        local currentVelocity =
            targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local horizontalVelocity =
            Vector3.new(
                currentVelocity.X,
                0,
                currentVelocity.Z
            )

        local horizontalSpeed =
            horizontalVelocity.Magnitude

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Prediction strength
        -------------------------------------------------

        local basePredictionTime =
            (settings.PredictionStrength or 20)
            / 100

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Timing
        -------------------------------------------------

        local now =
            time()

        local deltaTime =
            TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime
            and math.clamp(
                now
                - TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime,
                1 / 240,
                0.1
            )
            or 1 / 60

        TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
            now

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Measure acceleration
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity then

            local acceleration =
                (
                    currentVelocity
                    - TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity
                ) / deltaTime

            acceleration =
                Vector3.new(
                    acceleration.X,
                    0,
                    acceleration.Z
                )

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Clamp extreme acceleration spikes
            -------------------------------------------------

            if acceleration.Magnitude > 250 then

                acceleration =
                    acceleration.Unit * 250

            end

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Smooth acceleration
            -------------------------------------------------

            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
                TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration:Lerp(
                    acceleration,
                    0.25
                )

        end

        TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
            currentVelocity

        local horizontalAcceleration =
            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Detect turning
        -------------------------------------------------

        local turnAmount = 0

        local lastHorizontal =
            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity

        if lastHorizontal
        and lastHorizontal.Magnitude > 1
        and horizontalSpeed > 1 then

            local dot =
                math.clamp(
                    lastHorizontal.Unit:Dot(
                        horizontalVelocity.Unit
                    ),
                    -1,
                    1
                )

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- 0 = straight
            -- 1 = complete reversal
            -------------------------------------------------

            turnAmount =
                (1 - dot) * 0.5

        end

        TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
            horizontalVelocity

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Actual horizontal position
        -------------------------------------------------

        local actualHorizontal =
            Vector3.new(
                targetRoot.Position.X,
                0,
                targetRoot.Position.Z
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Circular Movement Detection
        -------------------------------------------------

        local accelerationMagnitude =
            horizontalAcceleration.Magnitude

        local lateralAcceleration =
            Vector3.zero

        if horizontalSpeed > 1 then

            local moveDirection =
                horizontalVelocity.Unit

            ----------------------------------
            -- Remove acceleration that points
            -- forward/backward.
            --
            -- What's left is sideways turning
            -- acceleration.
            ----------------------------------

            local forwardAcceleration =
                moveDirection
                * horizontalAcceleration:Dot(
                    moveDirection
                )

            lateralAcceleration =
                horizontalAcceleration
                - forwardAcceleration

        end

        local lateralMagnitude =
            lateralAcceleration.Magnitude

        ----------------------------------
        -- A player running in circles has:
        --
        -- decent movement speed
        -- +
        -- sustained sideways acceleration
        ----------------------------------

        local circleLikely =
            horizontalSpeed > 8
            and lateralMagnitude > 22

        if circleLikely then

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                math.clamp(
                    TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                    + 0.05,
                    0,
                    1
                )

        else

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                math.clamp(
                    TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                    - 0.12,
                    0,
                    1
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Dynamic prediction based on speed
        -------------------------------------------------

        local speedScale =
            math.clamp(
                horizontalSpeed / 16,
                0,
                1
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Reduce normal lead during sharp turns
        -------------------------------------------------

        local turnPredictionScale =
            1 - math.clamp(
                turnAmount * 2.5,
                0,
                0.85
            )

        local adjustedPredictionTime =
            basePredictionTime
            * speedScale
            * turnPredictionScale

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Reduce acceleration influence during turns
        -------------------------------------------------

        local accelerationScale =
            1 - math.clamp(
                turnAmount * 4,
                0,
                0.95
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Normal horizontal prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        local horizontalPrediction =
            actualHorizontal
            + horizontalVelocity
                * adjustedPredictionTime
            + horizontalAcceleration
                * (
                    0.35
                    * adjustedPredictionTime
                    * adjustedPredictionTime
                    * accelerationScale
                )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Circle Counter-Prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence > 0.55 then

            ----------------------------------
            -- The more certain we are that
            -- they're circling, the less we
            -- attempt to lead them.
            ----------------------------------

            local circleStrength =
                math.clamp(
                    (
                        TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                        - 0.55
                    ) / 0.35,
                    0,
                    1
                )

            horizontalPrediction =
                horizontalPrediction:Lerp(
                    actualHorizontal,
                    circleStrength
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Tiny movement = exact position
        -------------------------------------------------

        if horizontalSpeed < 1.5 then

            horizontalPrediction =
                actualHorizontal

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Maximum horizontal lead
        -------------------------------------------------

        local MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD =
            9

        local leadOffset =
            horizontalPrediction
            - actualHorizontal

        if leadOffset.Magnitude
            > MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD then

            horizontalPrediction =
                actualHorizontal
                + leadOffset.Unit
                * MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Vertical prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        local currentY =
            targetRoot.Position.Y

        local verticalVelocity =
            currentVelocity.Y

        local predictedY =
            currentY

        local airborne =
            targetHumanoid
            and targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial
                == Enum.Material.Air

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Tiny vertical movement = exact Y
        -------------------------------------------------

        if math.abs(verticalVelocity) < 1.5 then

            predictedY =
                currentY

        elseif airborne then

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Ballistic jump / fall prediction
            -------------------------------------------------

            predictedY =
                currentY
                + verticalVelocity
                    * basePredictionTime
                - 0.5
                    * workspace.Gravity
                    * basePredictionTime
                    * basePredictionTime

        else

            predictedY =
                currentY

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Vertical safety clamp
        -------------------------------------------------

        local MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET =
            4

        predictedY =
            math.clamp(
                predictedY,
                currentY - MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET,
                currentY + MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Pull vertical prediction back toward target
        -- as their Y velocity becomes smaller
        -------------------------------------------------

        local verticalSpeed =
            math.abs(
                verticalVelocity
            )

        local recenterAlpha =
            1 - math.clamp(
                verticalSpeed / 20,
                0,
                1
            )

        predictedY =
            predictedY
            + (
                currentY
                - predictedY
            ) * recenterAlpha

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Completely stationary target
        -------------------------------------------------

        local stationary =
            horizontalSpeed < 1.5
            and math.abs(verticalVelocity) < 1.5

        local predictedPosition

        if stationary then

            predictedPosition =
                targetRoot.Position

        else

            predictedPosition =
                Vector3.new(
                    horizontalPrediction.X,
                    predictedY,
                    horizontalPrediction.Z
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Match target rotation
        -------------------------------------------------

        local rotation =
            targetRoot.CFrame
            - targetRoot.Position

        antiTeleport.Bypass =
            true

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Move onto prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        root.CFrame =
            CFrame.new(
                predictedPosition
            ) * rotation

        antiTeleport.LastPos =
            root.Position

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Original single velocity pulse
        -------------------------------------------------

        local originalVelocity =
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
            Vector3.new(
                math.random(-150, 150),
                -25000,
                math.random(-150, 150)
            )

        Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Restore velocity
        -------------------------------------------------

        if root
        and root.Parent then

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                originalVelocity

            antiTeleport.LastPos =
                root.Position

        end

        antiTeleport.Bypass =
            false

        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchBusy =
            false

    end

    local function updateAutoTeleport()

        if not TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive then
            return
        end

        if not settings.TargetPlayer then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        local targetChar = settings.TargetPlayer.Character

        if not targetChar then
            return
        end

        local targetRoot =
            targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not targetRoot then
            return
        end

        local distance =
            (root.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude

        if distance <= 50 then
            return
        end

        antiTeleport.Bypass = true

        root.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position

        antiTeleport.Bypass = false

    end

    local function updateTPOnDeath()

        if not TargetRuntime.TPOnDeathActive then

            TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled = false
            return

        end

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

            TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled = false
            return

        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local targetCharacter =
            target.Character

        if not targetCharacter then

            TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled = false
            return

        end

        local targetHumanoid =
            targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                "Humanoid"
            )

        local targetHead =
            targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                "Head"
            )

        if not targetHumanoid then
            return
        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Alive again / new character
        ----------------------------------

        if targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then

            TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled = false
            return

        end

        ----------------------------------
        -- Already handled this death
        ----------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled then
            return
        end

        if not targetHead then
            return
        end

        TargetRuntime.TPDeathHandled =
            true

        ----------------------------------
        -- Teleport to dead target's head
        ----------------------------------

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame =
                targetHead.CFrame

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end)

    end

    local function updateFlingSpin(targetOverride, freeRun)

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Normal Target Fling Spin checks
        -------------------------------------------------

        if not freeRun then

            if not TargetRuntime.FlingSpinActive then

                if root
                and root.Parent then

                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                        Vector3.zero

                end

                return

            end

            if TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned then
                return
            end

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Don't fight with free-use modes
            -------------------------------------------------

            if settings.ClickFollow
            or settings.EveryoneFollow then
                return
            end

        end

        if TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy then
            return
        end

        local target =
            targetOverride
            or settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local targetChar =
            target.Character

        if not targetChar then
            return
        end

        local targetRoot =
            targetChar:FindFirstChild(
                "HumanoidRootPart"
            )

        if not targetRoot then
            return
        end

        local targetHumanoid =
            targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                "Humanoid"
            )

        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy =
            true

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Reset prediction history when target changes
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.LastTarget ~= target then

            TargetRuntime.LastTarget =
                target

            TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
                Vector3.zero

            TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
                nil

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                0

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Current target movement
        -------------------------------------------------

        local currentVelocity =
            targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local horizontalVelocity =
            Vector3.new(
                currentVelocity.X,
                0,
                currentVelocity.Z
            )

        local horizontalSpeed =
            horizontalVelocity.Magnitude

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Prediction slider
        -------------------------------------------------

        local basePredictionTime =
            (settings.PredictionStrength or 20)
            / 100

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Prediction timing
        -------------------------------------------------

        local now =
            time()

        local deltaTime =
            TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime
            and math.clamp(
                now
                - TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime,
                1 / 240,
                0.1
            )
            or 1 / 60

        TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
            now

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Acceleration
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity then

            local acceleration =
                (
                    currentVelocity
                    - TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity
                ) / deltaTime

            acceleration =
                Vector3.new(
                    acceleration.X,
                    0,
                    acceleration.Z
                )

            if acceleration.Magnitude > 250 then

                acceleration =
                    acceleration.Unit * 250

            end

            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
                TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration:Lerp(
                    acceleration,
                    0.25
                )

        end

        TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
            currentVelocity

        local horizontalAcceleration =
            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Turning detection
        -------------------------------------------------

        local turnAmount = 0

        local lastHorizontal =
            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity

        if lastHorizontal
        and lastHorizontal.Magnitude > 1
        and horizontalSpeed > 1 then

            local dot =
                math.clamp(
                    lastHorizontal.Unit:Dot(
                        horizontalVelocity.Unit
                    ),
                    -1,
                    1
                )

            turnAmount =
                (1 - dot) * 0.5

        end

        TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
            horizontalVelocity

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Actual horizontal position
        -------------------------------------------------

        local actualHorizontal =
            Vector3.new(
                targetRoot.Position.X,
                0,
                targetRoot.Position.Z
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Detect sustained circular movement
        -------------------------------------------------

        local lateralAcceleration =
            Vector3.zero

        if horizontalSpeed > 1 then

            local moveDirection =
                horizontalVelocity.Unit

            local forwardAcceleration =
                moveDirection
                * horizontalAcceleration:Dot(
                    moveDirection
                )

            lateralAcceleration =
                horizontalAcceleration
                - forwardAcceleration

        end

        local lateralMagnitude =
            lateralAcceleration.Magnitude

        local circleLikely =
            horizontalSpeed > 8
            and lateralMagnitude > 22

        if circleLikely then

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                math.clamp(
                    TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                    + 0.05,
                    0,
                    1
                )

        else

            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
                math.clamp(
                    TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                    - 0.12,
                    0,
                    1
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Dynamic prediction from movement speed
        -------------------------------------------------

        local speedScale =
            math.clamp(
                horizontalSpeed / 16,
                0,
                1
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Reduce lead during sharp turns
        -------------------------------------------------

        local turnPredictionScale =
            1 - math.clamp(
                turnAmount * 2.5,
                0,
                0.85
            )

        local adjustedPredictionTime =
            basePredictionTime
            * speedScale
            * turnPredictionScale

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Acceleration influence
        -------------------------------------------------

        local accelerationScale =
            1 - math.clamp(
                turnAmount * 4,
                0,
                0.95
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Normal horizontal prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        local horizontalPrediction =
            actualHorizontal
            + horizontalVelocity
                * adjustedPredictionTime
            + horizontalAcceleration
                * (
                    0.35
                    * adjustedPredictionTime
                    * adjustedPredictionTime
                    * accelerationScale
                )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Circle counter-prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence > 0.55 then

            local circleStrength =
                math.clamp(
                    (
                        TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                        - 0.55
                    ) / 0.35,
                    0,
                    1
                )

            horizontalPrediction =
                horizontalPrediction:Lerp(
                    actualHorizontal,
                    circleStrength
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Tiny horizontal movement = exact position
        -------------------------------------------------

        if horizontalSpeed < 1.5 then

            horizontalPrediction =
                actualHorizontal

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Maximum lead
        -------------------------------------------------

        local MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD =
            9

        local leadOffset =
            horizontalPrediction
            - actualHorizontal

        if leadOffset.Magnitude
            > MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD then

            horizontalPrediction =
                actualHorizontal
                + leadOffset.Unit
                * MAX_HORIZONTAL_LEAD

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Vertical prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        local currentY =
            targetRoot.Position.Y

        local verticalVelocity =
            currentVelocity.Y

        local predictedY =
            currentY

        local airborne =
            targetHumanoid
            and targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial
                == Enum.Material.Air

        if math.abs(verticalVelocity) < 1.5 then

            predictedY =
                currentY

        elseif airborne then

            predictedY =
                currentY
                + verticalVelocity
                    * basePredictionTime
                - 0.5
                    * workspace.Gravity
                    * basePredictionTime
                    * basePredictionTime

        else

            predictedY =
                currentY

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Vertical clamp
        -------------------------------------------------

        local MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET =
            4

        predictedY =
            math.clamp(
                predictedY,
                currentY - MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET,
                currentY + MAX_VERTICAL_OFFSET
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Recenter Y as vertical motion slows
        -------------------------------------------------

        local verticalSpeed =
            math.abs(
                verticalVelocity
            )

        local recenterAlpha =
            1 - math.clamp(
                verticalSpeed / 20,
                0,
                1
            )

        predictedY =
            predictedY
            + (
                currentY
                - predictedY
            ) * recenterAlpha

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Stationary target
        -------------------------------------------------

        local stationary =
            horizontalSpeed < 1.5
            and math.abs(verticalVelocity) < 1.5

        local predictedPosition

        if stationary then

            predictedPosition =
                targetRoot.Position

        else

            predictedPosition =
                Vector3.new(
                    horizontalPrediction.X,
                    predictedY,
                    horizontalPrediction.Z
                )

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- IMPORTANT:
        --
        -- Preserve CURRENT rotation instead of target
        -- rotation so CFrame positioning doesn't reset
        -- the violent spin every update.
        -------------------------------------------------

        local currentRotation =
            root.CFrame
            - root.Position

        antiTeleport.Bypass =
            true

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Lock POSITION to prediction
        -------------------------------------------------

        root.CFrame =
            CFrame.new(
                predictedPosition
            ) * currentRotation

        antiTeleport.LastPos =
            root.Position

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Violent multi-axis tumble
        -------------------------------------------------

        local spinStrength =
            10000

        local spinX =
            math.random(
                -spinStrength,
                spinStrength
            )

        local spinY =
            math.random(
                -spinStrength,
                spinStrength
            )

        local spinZ =
            math.random(
                -spinStrength,
                spinStrength
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Don't allow an axis to accidentally be
        -- almost stationary.
        -------------------------------------------------

        local function strengthenAxis(value)

            local minimumSpin =
                10000

            if math.abs(value) < minimumSpin then

                if value < 0 then
                    return -minimumSpin
                else
                    return minimumSpin
                end

            end

            return value

        end

        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
            Vector3.new(
                strengthenAxis(spinX),
                strengthenAxis(spinY),
                strengthenAxis(spinZ)
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Keep translational physics from throwing
        -- us away from the predicted position.
        -------------------------------------------------

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
            Vector3.zero

        antiTeleport.Bypass =
            false

        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy =
            false

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- BOT FLING SPIN RUNTIME
    -------------------------------------------------

    BotMode.FlingSpinTarget =
        nil

    BotMode.FlingSpinStartCFrame =
        nil

    BotMode.FlingSpinLoop =
        false


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- RESTORE BOT TO ORIGINAL POSITION
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function restoreBotFlingSpinPosition(savedCFrame)

        if not savedCFrame then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end


        local function clearMomentum()

            if character
            and character.Parent then

                for _, descendant in ipairs(
                    character:GetDescendants()
                ) do

                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then

                        descendant.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                        descendant.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                    end

                end

            end


            if root
            and root.Parent then

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FIRST RESTORE
        -------------------------------------------------

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame =
                savedCFrame

            clearMomentum()

            antiTeleport.LastPos =
                root.Position

            antiTeleport.LastCFrame =
                root.CFrame

        end)


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- HARD SETTLE
        -------------------------------------------------

        task.spawn(function()

            Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            if not root
            or not root.Parent then
                return
            end

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame =
                    savedCFrame

                clearMomentum()

                antiTeleport.LastPos =
                    root.Position

                antiTeleport.LastCFrame =
                    root.CFrame

            end)


            Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            if not root
            or not root.Parent then
                return
            end

            clearMomentum()

        end)

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- RESET FLING SPIN HISTORY
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function resetBotFlingSpinHistory()

        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy =
            false

        TargetRuntime.LastTarget =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
            Vector3.zero

        TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
            0

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- START BOT FLING SPIN
    -------------------------------------------------

    BotMode.StartFlingSpin =
        function(target, loopMode)

            if not target
            or target == player
            or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
                return
            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- SAVE ORIGINAL LOCATION
            --
            -- A fresh Fling Spin session saves wherever
            -- the bot currently is.
            -------------------------------------------------

            if not BotMode.FlingSpinTarget
            and root
            and root.Parent then

                BotMode.FlingSpinStartCFrame =
                    root.CFrame

            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- SET MODE + TARGET
            -------------------------------------------------

            BotMode.FlingSpinLoop =
                loopMode == true

            BotMode.FlingSpinTarget =
                target

            BotMode.FlingSpinPausedForStand =
                false


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- START FRESH PREDICTION HISTORY
            -------------------------------------------------

            resetBotFlingSpinHistory()

        end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- STOP BOT FLING SPIN
    -------------------------------------------------

    BotMode.StopFlingSpin =
        function(restorePosition)

            local savedCFrame =
                BotMode.FlingSpinStartCFrame


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- CLEAR SESSION
            -------------------------------------------------

            BotMode.FlingSpinTarget =
                nil

            BotMode.FlingSpinStartCFrame =
                nil

            BotMode.FlingSpinLoop =
                false


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- RESET SPIN STATE
            -------------------------------------------------

            resetBotFlingSpinHistory()


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- RETURN TO ORIGINAL LOCATION
            -------------------------------------------------

            if restorePosition then

                restoreBotFlingSpinPosition(
                    savedCFrame
                )

            elseif root
            and root.Parent then

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

            if BotMode.ResumeStandAfterFlingSpin then
                BotMode.ResumeStandAfterFlingSpin()
            end

        end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- UPDATE BOT FLING SPIN
    -------------------------------------------------

    BotMode.UpdateFlingSpin =
        function()

            local target =
                BotMode.FlingSpinTarget

            if not target then
                return
