            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- PLAYER ACTUALLY LEFT SERVER
            --
            -- Both normal and loop mode end here because
            -- there is nothing left to wait for.
            -------------------------------------------------

            if target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

                BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
                    true
                )

                return

            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- CHARACTER MISSING
            --
            -- Normal:
            --     End + return home.
            --
            -- Loop:
            --     Keep target stored and wait for respawn.
            -------------------------------------------------

            local targetCharacter =
                target.Character

            if not targetCharacter then

                if BotMode.FlingSpinLoop then

                    resetBotFlingSpinHistory()

                    if BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits then
                        BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits()
                    end

                    if root
                    and root.Parent then

                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                    end

                    return

                end


                BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
                    true
                )

                return

            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- HUMANOID MISSING
            -------------------------------------------------

            local targetHumanoid =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                    "Humanoid"
                )

            if not targetHumanoid then

                if BotMode.FlingSpinLoop then

                    resetBotFlingSpinHistory()

                    if BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits then
                        BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits()
                    end

                    if root
                    and root.Parent then

                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                    end

                    return

                end


                BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
                    true
                )

                return

            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- TARGET DEAD
            -------------------------------------------------

            if targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then

                if BotMode.FlingSpinLoop then

                    resetBotFlingSpinHistory()

                    if BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits then
                        BotMode.ResumeStandWhileFlingSpinWaits()
                    end

                    if root
                    and root.Parent then

                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                    end

                    return

                end


                BotMode.StopFlingSpin(
                    true
                )

                return

            end


            -------------------------------------------------
            -- TARGET IS ALIVE
            --
            -- Both modes use the exact existing Spin
            -- Follow engine.
            -------------------------------------------------

            if BotMode.SuspendTemporaryStandForFlingSpin then
                BotMode.SuspendTemporaryStandForFlingSpin()
            end

            updateFlingSpin(
                target,
                true
            )

        end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- FREE FOLLOW RUNTIME
    -------------------------------------------------

    local FreeFollowRuntime = {

        ClickTarget = nil,
        ClickStartCFrame = nil,
        ClickStartedAt = nil,

        EveryoneTarget = nil,
        EveryoneStartCFrame = nil,

        EveryonePlayers = {},
        EveryoneIndex = 0,
        EveryoneTargetStartedAt = nil,

        AuraTarget = nil,
        AuraStartCFrame = nil,
        AuraTargetStartedAt = nil,
        AuraCooldowns = {},

    }


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- RESET SHARED PREDICTION HISTORY
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

        TargetRuntime.LastTarget =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
            Vector3.zero

        TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime =
            nil

        TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence =
            0

        TargetRuntime.FlingTouchBusy =
            false

        TargetRuntime.FlingSpinBusy =
            false

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- RESTORE CHARACTER TO A SAVED POSITION
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function restoreFreeFollowPosition(savedCFrame)

        if not savedCFrame then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local function clearCharacterMomentum()

            if character
            and character.Parent then

                for _, descendant in ipairs(
                    character:GetDescendants()
                ) do

                    if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then

                        descendant.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                        descendant.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            Vector3.zero

                    end

                end

            end

            if root
            and root.Parent then

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

        end


        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Restore position
            -------------------------------------------------

            root.CFrame =
                savedCFrame

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- First momentum clear
            -------------------------------------------------

            clearCharacterMomentum()

            antiTeleport.LastPos =
                root.Position

            antiTeleport.LastCFrame =
                root.CFrame

        end)


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Clear again after physics updates.
        -------------------------------------------------

        task.spawn(function()

            Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            if not root
            or not root.Parent then
                return
            end

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame =
                    savedCFrame

                clearCharacterMomentum()

                antiTeleport.LastPos =
                    root.Position

                antiTeleport.LastCFrame =
                    root.CFrame

            end)

            Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            if not root
            or not root.Parent then
                return
            end

            clearCharacterMomentum()

        end)

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- RUN SELECTED FREE FOLLOW METHOD
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function normalizeWhitelistName(name)

        if typeof(name) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end

        name =
            string.gsub(
                name,
                "^%s*(.-)%s*$",
                "%1"
            )

        if name == "" then
            return nil
        end

        return string.lower(name)

    end


    local function isEveryoneFollowWhitelisted(targetPlayer)

        if not targetPlayer then
            return false
        end

        local whitelist =
            settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist

        if typeof(whitelist) ~= "table" then
            return false
        end

        local targetName =
            string.lower(
                targetPlayer.Name
            )

        for _, whitelistedName in ipairs(
            whitelist
        ) do

            if normalizeWhitelistName(
                whitelistedName
            ) == targetName then

                return true

            end

        end

        return false

    end

    local function freeFollowTimedOut(startedAt)

        if not startedAt then
            return false
        end

        local timeout =
            tonumber(settings.FreeFollowTimeout)
            or 15

        if timeout <= 0 then
            return false
        end

        return time() - startedAt >= timeout

    end

    TargetRuntime.UpdateFlingTumble = function(target)

        if not target
        or target == player
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players
        or not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local targetCharacter = target.Character
        local targetRoot = targetCharacter
            and targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                "HumanoidRootPart"
            )

        local targetHumanoid = targetCharacter
            and targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                "Humanoid"
            )

        if not targetRoot
        or not targetHumanoid
        or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        if TargetRuntime.LastTarget ~= target then
            TargetRuntime.LastTarget = target
            TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity = nil
            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity = nil
            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration = Vector3.zero
            TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime = nil
            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence = 0
        end

        local now = time()
        local velocity = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(
            velocity.X,
            0,
            velocity.Z
        )
        local horizontalSpeed = horizontalVelocity.Magnitude
        local predictionTime =
            (settings.PredictionStrength or 20) / 100
        local deltaTime = TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime
            and math.clamp(
                now - TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime,
                1 / 240,
                0.1
            )
            or 1 / 60

        TargetRuntime.LastPredictTime = now

        if TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity then
            local acceleration =
                (velocity - TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity)
                / deltaTime

            acceleration = Vector3.new(
                acceleration.X,
                0,
                acceleration.Z
            )

            if acceleration.Magnitude > 250 then
                acceleration = acceleration.Unit * 250
            end

            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration =
                TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration:Lerp(
                    acceleration,
                    0.25
                )
        end

        TargetRuntime.LastTargetVelocity = velocity

        local horizontalAcceleration =
            TargetRuntime.SmoothedAcceleration
        local turnAmount = 0
        local lastHorizontal =
            TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity

        if lastHorizontal
        and lastHorizontal.Magnitude > 1
        and horizontalSpeed > 1 then
            local dot = math.clamp(
                lastHorizontal.Unit:Dot(
                    horizontalVelocity.Unit
                ),
                -1,
                1
            )
            turnAmount = (1 - dot) * 0.5
        end

        TargetRuntime.LastHorizontalVelocity =
            horizontalVelocity

        local actualHorizontal = Vector3.new(
            targetRoot.Position.X,
            0,
            targetRoot.Position.Z
        )
        local lateralAcceleration = Vector3.zero

        if horizontalSpeed > 1 then
            local moveDirection = horizontalVelocity.Unit
            local forwardAcceleration =
                moveDirection
                * horizontalAcceleration:Dot(moveDirection)
            lateralAcceleration =
                horizontalAcceleration - forwardAcceleration
        end

        local circleLikely =
            horizontalSpeed > 8
            and lateralAcceleration.Magnitude > 22

        TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence = math.clamp(
            TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence
                + (circleLikely and 0.05 or -0.12),
            0,
            1
        )

        local speedScale = math.clamp(
            horizontalSpeed / 16,
            0,
            1
        )
        local turnPredictionScale =
            1 - math.clamp(turnAmount * 2.5, 0, 0.85)
        local adjustedPredictionTime =
            predictionTime
            * speedScale
            * turnPredictionScale
        local accelerationScale =
            1 - math.clamp(turnAmount * 4, 0, 0.95)
        local horizontalPrediction =
            actualHorizontal
            + horizontalVelocity * adjustedPredictionTime
            + horizontalAcceleration
                * (0.35
                * adjustedPredictionTime
                * adjustedPredictionTime
                * accelerationScale)

        if TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence > 0.55 then
            local circleStrength = math.clamp(
                (TargetRuntime.CircleConfidence - 0.55) / 0.35,
                0,
                1
            )
            horizontalPrediction = horizontalPrediction:Lerp(
                actualHorizontal,
                circleStrength
            )
        end

        if horizontalSpeed < 1.5 then
            horizontalPrediction = actualHorizontal
        end

        local leadOffset =
            horizontalPrediction - actualHorizontal

        if leadOffset.Magnitude > 9 then
            horizontalPrediction =
                actualHorizontal + leadOffset.Unit * 9
        end

        local currentY = targetRoot.Position.Y
        local verticalVelocity = velocity.Y
        local predictedY = currentY
        local airborne =
            targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air

        if math.abs(verticalVelocity) >= 1.5
        and airborne then
            predictedY =
                currentY
                + verticalVelocity * predictionTime
                - 0.5 * workspace.Gravity
                    * predictionTime * predictionTime
        end

        predictedY = math.clamp(
            predictedY,
            currentY - 4,
            currentY + 4
        )

        local recenterAlpha =
            1 - math.clamp(
                math.abs(verticalVelocity) / 20,
                0,
                1
            )
        predictedY = predictedY
            + (currentY - predictedY) * recenterAlpha

        local stationary =
            horizontalSpeed < 1.5
            and math.abs(verticalVelocity) < 1.5
        local predictedPosition = stationary
            and targetRoot.Position
            or Vector3.new(
                horizontalPrediction.X,
                predictedY,
                horizontalPrediction.Z
            )

        local currentRotation =
            root.CFrame - root.Position
        local goal =
            CFrame.new(predictedPosition)
            * currentRotation

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame = goal

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = velocity

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                root.CFrame.RightVector
                * 65000

        end)

    end

    TargetRuntime.SetTouchFling = function(enabled)

        TargetRuntime.TouchFlingGeneration =
            (TargetRuntime.TouchFlingGeneration or 0) + 1

        if not enabled then
            return
        end

        local generation =
            TargetRuntime.TouchFlingGeneration

        task.spawn(function()

            while settings.TouchFling
            and TargetRuntime.TouchFlingGeneration
                == generation do

                if root
                and root.Parent
                and not FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget then

                    local pulseRoot = root
                    local originalVelocity =
                        pulseRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local originalAngularVelocity =
                        pulseRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity

                    TargetRuntime.TouchFlingPulseStep =
                        (TargetRuntime.TouchFlingPulseStep or 0)
                        % 4 + 1

                    local pulseStep =
                        TargetRuntime.TouchFlingPulseStep
                    local pulseVelocity

                    if pulseStep == 1 then
                        pulseVelocity = Vector3.new(28000, 750, 0)
                    elseif pulseStep == 2 then
                        pulseVelocity = Vector3.new(-28000, 750, 0)
                    elseif pulseStep == 3 then
                        pulseVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 750, 28000)
                    else
                        pulseVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 750, -28000)
                    end

                    pulseRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                        pulseVelocity
                    pulseRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                        Vector3.new(45000, 45000, 45000)

                    Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

                    if pulseRoot
                    and pulseRoot.Parent then
                        pulseRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                            originalVelocity
                        pulseRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                            originalAngularVelocity
                    end

                else

                    Services.RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

                end

                Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

            end

        end)

    end

    local function runFreeFollow(target)

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return
        end

        if settings.FreeFollowMethod
            == "Fling (Spin)" then

            updateFlingSpin(
                target,
                true
            )

        elseif settings.FreeFollowMethod
            == "Fling (Tumble)" then

            TargetRuntime.UpdateFlingTumble(
                target
            )

        else

            updateFlingTouch(
                target,
                true
            )

        end

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- CHECK WHETHER THE NORMAL SAFETY CONDITION
    -- WOULD HAVE TRIGGERED
    --
    -- IMPORTANT:
    -- This does NOT activate the real safety pin.
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function freeFollowSafetyTriggered(target)

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then
            return true
        end

        local targetCharacter =
            target.Character

        if not targetCharacter then
            return true
        end

        local targetRoot =
            targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                "HumanoidRootPart"
            )

        local targetHumanoid =
            targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                "Humanoid"
            )

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FLING SPIN:
        -- Normally pins when target dies / respawns.
        -------------------------------------------------

        if settings.FreeFollowMethod
            == "Fling (Spin)" then

            if not targetHumanoid
            or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                return true
            end

            if not targetRoot then
                return true
            end

            return false

        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FLING TOUCH:
        -- Normally pins when target is flung.
        -------------------------------------------------

        if not targetRoot then
            return true
        end

        local speed =
            targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude

        local FLING_TRIGGER_SPEED =
            140

        if speed >= FLING_TRIGGER_SPEED then
            return true
        end

        return false

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- STOP CLICK FOLLOW SESSION
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function stopClickFollow(restorePosition)

        if restorePosition then

            restoreFreeFollowPosition(
                FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartCFrame
            )

        elseif root
        and root.Parent then

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.ClickTarget =
            nil

        FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartCFrame =
            nil

        FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartedAt =
            nil

        resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- GET PLAYER FROM CLICKED PART
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function getPlayerFromClickedPart(part)

        local current =
            part

        while current
        and current ~= workspace do

            local clickedPlayer =
                Services.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(
                    current
                )

            if clickedPlayer then
                return clickedPlayer
            end

            current =
                current.Parent

        end

        return nil

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- CLICK FOLLOW UPDATE
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function updateClickFollow()

        if not settings.ClickFollow then
            return
        end

        local target =
            FreeFollowRuntime.ClickTarget

        if not target then
            return
        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Existing safety condition
        -------------------------------------------------

        if freeFollowSafetyTriggered(target) then

            stopClickFollow(true)

            return

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Timeout safety guard
        -------------------------------------------------

        if freeFollowTimedOut(
            FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartedAt
        ) then

            stopClickFollow(true)

            return

        end

        runFreeFollow(target)

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- BUILD EVERYONE LIST
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function advanceEveryoneFollow()

        local list =
            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers

        if #list == 0 then

            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
                nil

            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
                nil

            return

        end

        for _ = 1, #list do

            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex
                + 1

            if FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex
                > #list then

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
                    1

            end

            local candidate =
                list[
                    FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex
                ]

            if candidate
            and candidate ~= player
            and candidate.Parent
                == Services.Players then

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
                    candidate

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
                    time()

                resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

                return

            end

        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
            nil

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
            nil

    end

    local function buildEveryoneFollowList()

        local list = {}

        for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(
            Services.Players:GetPlayers()
        ) do

            if otherPlayer ~= player
            and not isEveryoneFollowWhitelisted(
                otherPlayer
            ) then

                table.insert(
                    list,
                    otherPlayer
                )

            end

        end

        table.sort(
            list,
            function(a, b)

                return string.lower(a.Name)
                    < string.lower(b.Name)

            end
        )

        return list

    end

    TargetRuntime.AddEveryoneWhitelist =
        function(username)

            local normalized =
                normalizeWhitelistName(
                    username
                )

            if not normalized then
                return false
            end

            settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist =
                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist
                or {}

            for _, existing in ipairs(
                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist
            ) do

                if normalizeWhitelistName(
                    existing
                ) == normalized then

                    return false

                end

            end

            table.insert(
                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist,
                normalized
            )

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Rebuild active Everyone Follow session
            -------------------------------------------------

            if settings.EveryoneFollow then

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
                    buildEveryoneFollowList()

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
                    0

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
                    nil

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
                    nil

                advanceEveryoneFollow()

            end

            return true

        end


    TargetRuntime.RemoveEveryoneWhitelist =
        function(username)

            local normalized =
                normalizeWhitelistName(
                    username
                )

            if not normalized then
                return false
            end

            local whitelist =
                settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist

            if typeof(whitelist) ~= "table" then
                return false
            end

            for index = #whitelist, 1, -1 do

                if normalizeWhitelistName(
                    whitelist[index]
                ) == normalized then

                    table.remove(
                        whitelist,
                        index
                    )

                    if settings.EveryoneFollow then

                        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
                            buildEveryoneFollowList()

                        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
                            0

                        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
                            nil

                        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
                            nil

                        advanceEveryoneFollow()

                    end

                    return true

                end

            end

            return false

        end


    TargetRuntime.ClearEveryoneWhitelist =
        function()

            settings.EveryoneFollowWhitelist =
                {}

            if settings.EveryoneFollow then

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
                    buildEveryoneFollowList()

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
                    0

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
                    nil

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
                    nil

                advanceEveryoneFollow()

            end

        end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- Keep an active Fling All queue synchronized
    -- when players join or leave the server.
    -------------------------------------------------

    Services.Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()

        if not settings.EveryoneFollow then
            return
        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
            buildEveryoneFollowList()

        if not FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget then

            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex = 0
            advanceEveryoneFollow()

        end

    end)

    Services.Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)

        if not settings.EveryoneFollow then
            return
        end

        task.defer(function()

            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
                buildEveryoneFollowList()

            if FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget
                == leavingPlayer then

                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex = 0
                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget = nil
                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt = nil
                advanceEveryoneFollow()

            end

        end)

    end)

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- START EVERYONE FOLLOW
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function startEveryoneFollow()

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneStartCFrame =
            root.CFrame

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
            buildEveryoneFollowList()

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
            0

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
            nil

        resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

        advanceEveryoneFollow()

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- STOP EVERYONE FOLLOW
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function stopEveryoneFollow(restorePosition)

        if restorePosition then

            restoreFreeFollowPosition(
                FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneStartCFrame
            )

        elseif root
        and root.Parent then

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget =
            nil

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneStartCFrame =
            nil

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryonePlayers =
            {}

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneIndex =
            0

        FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt =
            nil

        resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

    end

    -------------------------------------------------
    -- EVERYONE FOLLOW UPDATE
    -------------------------------------------------

    local function updateEveryoneFollow()

        if not settings.EveryoneFollow then
            return
        end

        local target =
            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTarget

        if not target then

            advanceEveryoneFollow()

            return

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Target left the server
        -------------------------------------------------

        if target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

            advanceEveryoneFollow()

            return

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Existing safety condition
        -------------------------------------------------

        if freeFollowSafetyTriggered(target) then

            advanceEveryoneFollow()

            return

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Timeout guard
        -------------------------------------------------

        if freeFollowTimedOut(
            FreeFollowRuntime.EveryoneTargetStartedAt
        ) then

            advanceEveryoneFollow()

            return

        end

        runFreeFollow(target)

    end

    local function stopFlingAura(restorePosition, target)

        if restorePosition then
            restoreFreeFollowPosition(
                FreeFollowRuntime.AuraStartCFrame
            )
        end

        if target then
            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraCooldowns[target] =
                time() + 1
        end

        FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget = nil
        FreeFollowRuntime.AuraStartCFrame = nil
        FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTargetStartedAt = nil
        TargetRuntime.FlingAuraActive = false

        resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

    end

    local function findFlingAuraTarget()

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return nil
        end

        local nearestTarget = nil
        local nearestDistance =
            tonumber(settings.FlingAuraRadius)
            or 15

        for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(
            Services.Players:GetPlayers()
        ) do

            local otherCharacter =
                otherPlayer ~= player
                and otherPlayer.Character
            local otherRoot =
                otherCharacter
                and otherCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                    "HumanoidRootPart"
                )
            local otherHumanoid =
                otherCharacter
                and otherCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                    "Humanoid"
                )

            if otherRoot
            and otherHumanoid
            and otherHumanoid.Health > 0
            and time() >= (
                FreeFollowRuntime.AuraCooldowns[otherPlayer]
                or 0
            ) then

                local distance =
                    (otherRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude

                if distance <= nearestDistance then
                    nearestDistance = distance
                    nearestTarget = otherPlayer
                end

            end

        end

        return nearestTarget

    end

    local function updateFlingAura()

        if not settings.FlingAura then

            if FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget then
                stopFlingAura(
                    true,
                    FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget
                )
            end

            return

        end

        if settings.ClickFollow
        or settings.EveryoneFollow then

            if FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget then
                stopFlingAura(
                    true,
                    FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget
                )
            end

            return

        end

        local target =
            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget

        if not target then

            target = findFlingAuraTarget()

            if not target then
                return
            end

            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTarget = target
            TargetRuntime.FlingAuraActive = true
            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraStartCFrame =
                root and root.CFrame or nil
            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTargetStartedAt =
                time()

            resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

        end

        if freeFollowSafetyTriggered(target)
        or freeFollowTimedOut(
            FreeFollowRuntime.AuraTargetStartedAt
        ) then

            stopFlingAura(true, target)
            return

        end

        runFreeFollow(target)

    end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- EXPOSE CLICK FOLLOW TO UI
    -------------------------------------------------

    TargetRuntime.SetClickFollow =
        function(enabled)

            settings.ClickFollow =
                enabled

            if enabled then

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Disable Everyone Follow
                -------------------------------------------------

                if settings.EveryoneFollow then

                    settings.EveryoneFollow =
                        false

                    stopEveryoneFollow(true)

                end

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Clear any old Click Follow session.
                --
                -- Do NOT restore because we're starting fresh.
                -------------------------------------------------

                stopClickFollow(false)

            else

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Toggle disabled:
                -- restore and completely wipe session.
                -------------------------------------------------

                stopClickFollow(true)

            end

        end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- EXPOSE EVERYONE FOLLOW TO UI
    -------------------------------------------------

    TargetRuntime.SetEveryoneFollow =
        function(enabled)

            settings.EveryoneFollow =
                enabled

            if enabled then

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Disable Click Follow
                -------------------------------------------------

                if settings.ClickFollow then

                    settings.ClickFollow =
                        false

                    stopClickFollow(true)

                end

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Wipe any previous Everyone session
                -------------------------------------------------

                stopEveryoneFollow(false)

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Start completely fresh
                -------------------------------------------------

                startEveryoneFollow()

            else

                -------------------------------------------------
                -- Toggle disabled:
                -- restore original position and wipe everything.
                -------------------------------------------------

                stopEveryoneFollow(true)

            end

        end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- EXPOSE METHOD DROPDOWN TO UI
    -------------------------------------------------

    TargetRuntime.SetFreeFollowMethod =
        function(method)

            if method ~= "Fling (Touch)"
            and method ~= "Fling (Spin)"
            and method ~= "Fling (Tumble)" then
                return
            end

            settings.FreeFollowMethod =
                method

            -------------------------------------------------
            -- Clear old prediction data when switching
            -- between Predict / Spin.
            -------------------------------------------------

            resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

            if root
            and root.Parent then

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end

        end


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- CLICK LISTENER
    --
    -- Connected once when initializeRuntime runs.
    -------------------------------------------------

    local mouse =
        player:GetMouse()

    mouse.Button1Down:Connect(function()

        if not settings.ClickFollow then
            return
        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Everyone mode shouldn't accept clicks.
        -------------------------------------------------

        if settings.EveryoneFollow then
            return
        end

        local clickedPart =
            mouse.Target

        if not clickedPart then
            return
        end

        local clickedPlayer =
            getPlayerFromClickedPart(
                clickedPart
            )

        if not clickedPlayer
        or clickedPlayer == player then
            return
        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Clicking another player while already active:
        -- return to where the previous click began.
        -------------------------------------------------

        if FreeFollowRuntime.ClickTarget then

            stopClickFollow(true)

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Save fresh starting position.
        -------------------------------------------------

    FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartCFrame =
        root.CFrame

    FreeFollowRuntime.ClickTarget =
        clickedPlayer

    FreeFollowRuntime.ClickStartedAt =
        time()

    resetFreeFollowPredictionHistory()

    end)


    -------------------------------------------------
    -- EXPOSE UPDATE FUNCTIONS
    --
    -- Your existing runtime loop can call these.
    -------------------------------------------------

    TargetRuntime.UpdateClickFollow =
        updateClickFollow

    TargetRuntime.UpdateEveryoneFollow =
        updateEveryoneFollow

    TargetRuntime.UpdateFlingAura =
        updateFlingAura

    TargetRuntime.UpdateSafetyPin = function()

        local target =
            settings.TargetPlayer

        if not target
        or target.Parent ~= Services.Players then

            TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
                false

            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned =
                false

            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                nil

            return

        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        local targetCharacter =
            target.Character

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FLING SPIN:
        -- Pin while target is dead / respawning
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.FlingSpinActive then

            if not targetCharacter then

                TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
                    true

            else

                local targetHumanoid =
                    targetCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass(
                        "Humanoid"
                    )

                if not targetHumanoid
                or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then

                    TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
                        true

                else

                    TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
                        false

                end

            end

        else

            TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned =
                false

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FLING TOUCH:
        -- Pin when target gets flung
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive
        and targetCharacter then

            local targetRoot =
                targetCharacter:FindFirstChild(
                    "HumanoidRootPart"
                )

            if targetRoot then

                local speed =
                    targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude

                ----------------------------------
                -- Trigger at a high speed.
                ----------------------------------

                local FLING_TRIGGER_SPEED =
                    140

                ----------------------------------
                -- Require a much lower speed
                -- before considering them settled.
                ----------------------------------

                local FLING_RELEASE_SPEED =
                    70

                local FLING_SETTLE_TIME =
                    0.35

                if not TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned then

                    if speed >= FLING_TRIGGER_SPEED then

                        TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned =
                            true

                        TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                            nil

                    end

                else

                    ----------------------------------
                    -- Already protected.
                    -- Don't release immediately.
                    ----------------------------------

                    if speed <= FLING_RELEASE_SPEED then

                        if not TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt then

                            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                                time()

                        elseif time()
                            - TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt
                            >= FLING_SETTLE_TIME then

                            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned =
                                false

                            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                                nil

                        end

                    else

                        ----------------------------------
                        -- Velocity rose again.
                        -- Restart settle timer.
                        ----------------------------------

                        TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                            nil

                    end

                end

            end

        else

            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned =
                false

            TargetRuntime.PredictFlingSettledAt =
                nil

        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- Final pin
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.SpinDeathPinned
        or TargetRuntime.PredictFlingPinned then

            bypassAntiTeleport(function()

                root.CFrame =
                    AUTOFARM_CFRAME

                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                    Vector3.zero

            end)

        end

    end

    local function updateCFrameSpeed()

        if not settings.CFrameSpeed then
            return
        end

        if not root or not humanoid then
            return
        end

        if not CanUseControls() then
            return
        end

        local moveDirection = Vector3.zero
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            moveDirection += camera.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            moveDirection -= camera.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            moveDirection += camera.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            moveDirection -= camera.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if moveDirection.Magnitude > 0 then

            moveDirection = moveDirection.Unit

            root.CFrame +=
                moveDirection *
                (settings.CFrameSpeedValue / 60)

        end

    end

    local function updateCFrameFly()

        if not settings.Fly then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        if not CanUseControls() then
            return
        end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local direction = Vector3.zero

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            direction += Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
            direction -= Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if direction.Magnitude > 0 then
            direction = direction.Unit
        end

        flyObjects.Velocity.Velocity = direction * settings.FlySpeed

        flyObjects.Gyro.CFrame = cam.CFrame

    end

    local function updateVelocityFly()

        if not settings.Fly then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        if not CanUseControls() then
            return
        end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local direction = Vector3.zero

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            direction += Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
            direction -= Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if direction.Magnitude > 0 then
            direction = direction.Unit
        end

        flyObjects.Velocity.Velocity = direction * settings.FlySpeed

    end

    local function updateLinearVelocityFly()

        if not settings.Fly then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        if not CanUseControls() then
            return
        end

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        local direction = Vector3.zero

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
            direction -= cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
            direction += cam.CFrame.RightVector
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
            direction += Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if Services.UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
            direction -= Vector3.yAxis
        end

        if direction.Magnitude > 0 then
            direction = direction.Unit
        end

        flyObjects.LinearVelocity.VectorVelocity =
            direction * settings.FlySpeed

        flyObjects.AlignOrientation.CFrame = cam.CFrame

    end

    local function updateFly()

        if not settings.Fly then
            return
        end

        if settings.FlyMethod == "CFrame" then

            updateCFrameFly()

        elseif settings.FlyMethod == "Velocity" then

            updateVelocityFly()

        elseif settings.FlyMethod == "LinearVelocity" then

            updateLinearVelocityFly()

        end

    end

    local function updateAutoFarm()

        if not settings.AutoFarm then
            return
        end

        if not character or not root then
            return
        end

        root.CFrame = AUTOFARM_CFRAME

    end

    local function updateAntiSit()

        for _, seat in ipairs(AntiSit.Seats) do

            if seat.Parent then
                seat.Disabled = settings.AntiSit
            end

        end

    end

    local function updateAutoEmployee()

        if not settings.AutoEmployee then
            autoState.employee.Triggered = false
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Find the picture
        --------------------------------------------------

        local picture

        local structure = workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")

        if structure then

            local factory = structure:FindFirstChild("Factory Frenzy")

            if factory then

                local building = factory:FindFirstChild("Building")

                if building then
                    picture = building:FindFirstChild("Picture")
                end

            end

        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Picture doesn't exist anymore
        --------------------------------------------------

        if not picture then
            autoState.employee.Triggered = false
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Already claimed this round
        --------------------------------------------------

        if autoState.employee.Triggered then
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Claim Employee of the Year
        --------------------------------------------------

        autoState.employee.Triggered = true

        local oldPosition = root.CFrame

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()
            root.CFrame = picture:GetPivot()
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        task.wait(0.3)

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()
            root.CFrame = oldPosition
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

    end

    local function updateAutoPrisoner()

        if not settings.AutoPrisoner then

            autoState.prisoner.Triggered = false

            return

        end

        if not root
        or not root.Parent then
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Find Prison Panic
        --------------------------------------------------

        local structure =
            workspace:FindFirstChild("Structure")

        if not structure then

            autoState.prisoner.Triggered = false

            return

        end

        local prisonPanic =
            structure:FindFirstChild(
                "Prison Panic"
            )

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Map doesn't exist anymore
        --------------------------------------------------

        if not prisonPanic then

            autoState.prisoner.Triggered = false

            return

        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Find prisoner admission trigger
        --------------------------------------------------

        local admissionPart =
            prisonPanic:FindFirstChild(
                "PrisonerAdmissionPart"
            )

        if not admissionPart
        or not admissionPart:IsA("BasePart") then

            autoState.prisoner.Triggered = false

            return

        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Already triggered this round
        --------------------------------------------------

        if autoState.prisoner.Triggered then
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Trigger prisoner admission
        --------------------------------------------------

        autoState.prisoner.Triggered = true

        local oldPosition =
            root.CFrame

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame =
                admissionPart.CFrame

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end)

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Give the game's touch system time
        -- to register us inside the trigger
        --------------------------------------------------

        task.wait(0.3)

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Return to previous location
        --------------------------------------------------

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame =
                oldPosition

            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.zero

        end)

    end

    local function updateRemoveMugshotUI()

        if not settings.RemoveMugshotUI then
            return
        end

        removeMugshotGui()

    end

    local function updateRemoveBlizzardUI()

        if not settings.RemoveBlizzardUI then
            return
        end

        removeBlizzardGui()

    end

    local function updateRemoveSandstormUI()

        if not settings.RemoveSandstormUI then
            return
        end

        removeSandstormGui()

    end

    _G.__LunacyUpdateRuntime = function()

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- ANTI SIT
        -------------------------------------------------

        if settings.AntiSit ~= lastState.AntiSit then

            lastState.AntiSit =
                settings.AntiSit

            updateAntiSit()

        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- ANTI BANG
        -------------------------------------------------

        if settings.AntiBang ~= lastState.AntiBang
        or settings.AntiBangAnimations ~= lastState.AntiBangAnimations then

            lastState.AntiBang =
                settings.AntiBang

            lastState.AntiBangAnimations =
                settings.AntiBangAnimations


            if settings.AntiBang
            or settings.AntiBangAnimations then

                if AntiBang.CharacterConnection then

                    AntiBang.CharacterConnection:Disconnect()

                    AntiBang.CharacterConnection =
                        nil

                end


                if player.Character then

                    connectAntiBang(
                        player.Character
                    )

                end


                AntiBang.CharacterConnection =
                    player.CharacterAdded:Connect(
                        function(character)

                            connectAntiBang(
                                character
                            )

                        end
                    )

            else

                disconnectAntiBang()

            end

        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- CORE DEFENSE
        -------------------------------------------------

        updateNoFallDamage()
        updateVelocityGuard()
        AntiFling.UpdateProtection()
        updateAntiVoidAutoTP()
        updateAntiTeleport()


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- NORMAL TARGET FEATURES
        -------------------------------------------------

        updateFollow()
        updateFlingTouch()
        updateAutoTeleport()
        updateTPOnDeath()
        updateFlingSpin()


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- NORMAL TARGET SAFETY PIN
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.UpdateSafetyPin then
            TargetRuntime.UpdateSafetyPin()
        end


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- FREE CLICK / EVERYONE FOLLOW
        -------------------------------------------------

        if TargetRuntime.UpdateClickFollow then
            TargetRuntime.UpdateClickFollow()
        end

        if TargetRuntime.UpdateEveryoneFollow then
            TargetRuntime.UpdateEveryoneFollow()
        end

        if TargetRuntime.UpdateFlingAura then
            TargetRuntime.UpdateFlingAura()
        end

        -------------------------------------------------
        -- MOVEMENT
        -------------------------------------------------

        updateCFrameSpeed()
        updateFly()


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- AUTOMATION
        -------------------------------------------------

        updateAutoFarm()
        updateAutoEmployee()
        updateAutoPrisoner()


        -------------------------------------------------
        -- REMOVE UI
        -------------------------------------------------

        updateRemoveMugshotUI()
        updateRemoveBlizzardUI()
        updateRemoveSandstormUI()

    end

end

_G.__LunacyInitializeRuntime()

function _G.__LunacyInitializeDisasterProtection()

	local disasterTimers = {
	    Avalanche = 0,
	    Tsunami = 0,
		Volcano = 0,
		Meteor = 0,
	}

    local SAFE_LOCATION = CFrame.new(
        -250, 195, 310
    )

	local function exitProtection()

	    if not protection.Active then
	        return
	    end

 	   if not character or not root then
	        return
	    end

	    root.CFrame = protection.Position

	    protection.Position = nil
	    protection.Active = false
	    protection.Owner = nil

	end

    local function escapeProtection()

        if not protection.Active then
            return
        end

        if not root then
            return
        end

        bypassAntiTeleport(function()

            root.CFrame = SAFE_LOCATION
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero

        end)

        protection.Position = nil
        protection.Active = false
        protection.Owner = nil
        protection.Started = 0

    end

    local function updateProtectionEscape()

        if not settings.ProtectionEscape then
            return
        end

        if not protection.Active then
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Round End Mode
        --------------------------------------------------

        if settings.ProtectionEscapeMode == "Round End" then

            local uis = player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("UIs")
            if not uis then
                return
            end

            local survivorDisplay =
                uis:FindFirstChild("survivorDisplay")

            if not survivorDisplay then
                return
            end

            if not survivorDisplay.Visible then
                return
            end

            escapeProtection()

            return

        end

        --------------------------------------------------
        -- Timer Mode
        --------------------------------------------------

        if settings.ProtectionEscapeMode ~= "Timer" then
            return
        end

        if tick() - protection.Started <
            settings.ProtectionEscapeDelay then

            return

        end

        escapeProtection()

    end

	local function avalancheNearby(radius)

	    if not root then
	        return false
	    end

	    local center

	    if protection.Active and protection.Position then
	        center = protection.Position.Position
	    else
	        center = root.Position
	    end

	    for i = #disasterState.AvalancheParts, 1, -1 do

	        local part = disasterState.AvalancheParts[i]

	        if not part or not part.Parent then
	            table.remove(disasterState.AvalancheParts, i)
	
	        elseif (part.Position - center).Magnitude <= radius then
	            return true
	        end
	
	    end

	    return false

	end

    local function tsunamiNearby(radius)

        if not root then
            return false
        end

        local center

        if protection.Active and protection.Position then
            center = protection.Position.Position
        else
            center = root.Position
        end

        for i = #disasterState.TsunamiParts, 1, -1 do

            local part = disasterState.TsunamiParts[i]

            if not part or not part.Parent then

                table.remove(disasterState.TsunamiParts, i)

            else
                -- Tsunamis are a wide vertical wall. Height difference does
                -- not make the wave safe, so measure on the X/Z plane.
                local horizontalOffset = Vector2.new(
                    part.Position.X - center.X,
                    part.Position.Z - center.Z
                )

                if horizontalOffset.Magnitude <= radius then

                    return true

                end

            end

        end

        return false

    end

    local function meteorNearby(radius)

        if not root then
            return false
        end

        local center

        if protection.Active
        and protection.Position then

            center =
                protection.Position.Position

        else

            center =
                root.Position

        end

        for i = #disasterState.MeteorParts, 1, -1 do

            local meteor =
                disasterState.MeteorParts[i]

            if not meteor
            or not meteor.Parent then

                table.remove(
                    disasterState.MeteorParts,
                    i
                )

            elseif
                (meteor.Position - center).Magnitude
                <= radius then

                return true

            end

        end

        return false

    end

	local function updateAntiAvalanche()

	    if not character or not root then
	        return
	    end

	    if not settings.AntiAvalanche then

	        if protection.Owner == "Avalanche" then
	            protection.Position = nil
	            protection.Active = false
	            protection.Owner = nil
	        end

	        disasterTimers.Avalanche = 0

	        return
	    end

	    if avalancheNearby(settings.AvalancheRadius) then

	        if not protection.Active then
	            protection.Position = root.CFrame
	            protection.Active = true
	            protection.Owner = "Avalanche"
                protection.Started = tick()
	        end

	        disasterTimers.Avalanche = tick()

	        -- Keep the player pinned at Autofarm
			root.CFrame = AUTOFARM_CFRAME

		elseif protection.Active and protection.Owner == "Avalanche" then

			root.CFrame = AUTOFARM_CFRAME

			if tick() - disasterTimers.Avalanche >= 1 then
				exitProtection()
			end

		end

	end

	local function updateAntiTsunami()

		if not character or not root then
			return
		end

		if not settings.AntiTsunami then

			if protection.Owner == "Tsunami" then
				exitProtection()
			end

			disasterTimers.Tsunami = 0
			return
		end

		if tsunamiNearby(settings.TsunamiRadius) then

			if not protection.Active then
				protection.Position = root.CFrame
				protection.Active = true
				protection.Owner = "Tsunami"
                protection.Started = tick()
			end

			disasterTimers.Tsunami = tick()

		end

		if protection.Active and protection.Owner == "Tsunami" then

			antiTeleport.Bypass = true
			root.CFrame = AUTOFARM_CFRAME
			root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
			antiTeleport.LastPos = root.Position
			antiTeleport.LastCFrame = root.CFrame
			antiTeleport.Bypass = false

			if tick() - disasterTimers.Tsunami >= 2.5 then
				exitProtection()
			end

		end

	end

	local function volcanoNearby(radius)

		if not root then
			return false
		end

		local center

		if protection.Active and protection.Position then
			center = protection.Position.Position
		else
			center = root.Position
		end

		for _, lava in ipairs(disasterState.VolcanoParts) do

			if lava.Parent and (lava.Position - center).Magnitude <= radius then
				return true
			end

		end

		return false

	end

	local function updateAntiVolcano()

		if not character or not root then
			return
		end

		if not settings.AntiVolcano then

			if protection.Owner == "Volcano" then
				exitProtection()
			end

			disasterTimers.Volcano = 0
			return
		end

		if volcanoNearby(settings.VolcanoRadius) then

			if not protection.Active then
				protection.Position = root.CFrame
				protection.Active = true
				protection.Owner = "Volcano"
                protection.Started = tick()
			end

			disasterTimers.Volcano = tick()

		end

		if protection.Active and protection.Owner == "Volcano" then

			root.CFrame = AUTOFARM_CFRAME

			if tick() - disasterTimers.Volcano >= 2 then

				-- Final safety check before returning.
				if not volcanoNearby(settings.VolcanoRadius) then
					exitProtection()
				end

			end

		end

	end

    local function updateAntiMeteor()

        if not character
        or not root then
            return
        end

        if not settings.AntiMeteor then

            if protection.Owner == "Meteor" then
                exitProtection()
            end

            disasterTimers.Meteor = 0

            return

        end

        if meteorNearby(
            settings.MeteorRadius
        ) then

            if not protection.Active then

                protection.Position =
                    root.CFrame

                protection.Active =
                    true

                protection.Owner =
                    "Meteor"

                protection.Started =
                    tick()

            end

            disasterTimers.Meteor =
                tick()

        end

        if protection.Active
        and protection.Owner == "Meteor" then

            root.CFrame =
                AUTOFARM_CFRAME

            if tick()
                - disasterTimers.Meteor
                >= 2 then

                if not meteorNearby(
                    settings.MeteorRadius
                ) then

                    exitProtection()

                end

            end

        end

    end

    player.PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)

        if child.Name == "BlizzardGui" and settings.RemoveBlizzardUI then
            child:Destroy()

        elseif child.Name == "SandStormGui" and settings.RemoveSandstormUI then
            child:Destroy()

        elseif child.Name == "MugshotGui" and settings.RemoveMugshotUI then
            child:Destroy()

        end

    end)

	workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)

		if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then
			return
		end

		if obj.Name == "AvalanchePart" then

			if not table.find(disasterState.AvalancheParts, obj) then
				table.insert(disasterState.AvalancheParts, obj)
			end

		elseif obj:FindFirstAncestor("TsunamiWave") then

			if not table.find(disasterState.TsunamiParts, obj) then
				table.insert(disasterState.TsunamiParts, obj)
			end

		end

	end)

	workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)

		if obj.Name ~= "AvalanchePart" then
			return
		end

		local index = table.find(disasterState.AvalancheParts, obj)

		if index then
			table.remove(disasterState.AvalancheParts, index)
		end

	end)

	for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name == "AvalanchePart" then
			if not table.find(disasterState.AvalancheParts, obj) then
				table.insert(disasterState.AvalancheParts, obj)
			end
		end
	end

	for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if obj:IsA("BasePart")
			and obj:FindFirstAncestor("TsunamiWave") then

			if not table.find(disasterState.TsunamiParts, obj) then
				table.insert(disasterState.TsunamiParts, obj)
			end

		end
	end

	updateDisasterProtections = function()

		updateAntiAvalanche()
		updateAntiTsunami()
		updateAntiVolcano()
		updateAntiMeteor()
        updateProtectionEscape()

	end

	end

_G.__LunacyInitializeDisasterProtection()

Services.RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)

	if settings.AntiEarthquake ~= lastState.AntiEarthquake then

		lastState.AntiEarthquake = settings.AntiEarthquake
		Earthquake:SetEnabled(settings.AntiEarthquake)

	end

	if not (
		settings.NoFallDamage
        or settings.AutoEmployee
        or settings.AutoPrisoner
		or settings.VelocityGuard
		or settings.AntiVoid
		or settings.AntiFling
		or settings.AntiTeleport
		or settings.AntiBang
		or settings.Following
		or settings.ClickFollow
		or settings.EveryoneFollow
		or settings.FlingAura
		or TargetRuntime.FlingAuraActive
		or settings.TouchFling
		or TargetRuntime.FlingTouchActive
		or TargetRuntime.AutoTeleportActive
        or TargetRuntime.FlingSpinActive
        or TargetRuntime.TPOnDeathActive
		or highlightActive
		or settings.CFrameSpeed
		or settings.Noclip
		or settings.Fly
		or settings.AutoFarm
		or settings.AntiAvalanche
		or settings.AntiTsunami
		or settings.AntiEarthquake
		or settings.AntiVolcano
		or settings.AntiMeteor
		or settings.AntiSit
        or settings.OrbitSystem
	) then
		return
	end

	if not character or not root or not root.Parent then
		return
	end
		
    _G.__LunacyUpdateRuntime()
	updateDisasterProtections()
    updateOrbitSystem(deltaTime)

end)

player:GetMouse().Button1Down:Connect(function()

    if not inputState.ClickTPHeld then
        return
    end

    if Services.UwserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
        return
    end

    local mouse = player:GetMouse()

    if mouse.Hit then
        ClickTeleport(mouse.Hit.Position)
    end

end)
